InspectAuthorityEpochTransitionRequestV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_id`、`transition_id`、`resolution_id`、`activation_id`、`transition_stage`、`caller_nonce`，transition_stage只有preparation、resolution或cancellation；同一signer role的InspectAuthorityEpochTransitionResponseV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_digest`、`transition_id`、`resolution_id`、`transition_stage`、`terminal_outcome_or_null`、`stored_request_digest_or_null`、`stored_transition_envelope_or_null`、`current_provider_head_digest`、`inspected_at`。stored envelope按stage只能是AuthorityEpochTransitionV1、AuthorityEpochTransitionResolutionReceiptV1或承担cancellation tombstone的ClaimV1RuntimeInstanceRebindV1。evidence digest、root digest、effective/terminal chain与selected/current epoch逐字段进入provider Head、lease/context与readiness。

provider已经terminal、但真正PITR丢失provider所接受的DB row时，禁止用current transaction重新猜测旧body，也禁止先做任何recovery-only rebind。coordinator先用fresh terminal Inspect锁定唯一CAS赢家与terminal Provider Head，再在当前DB gap anchor提交下述recovery intent；只有intent committed后，holder/timeline改变才可用绑定该intent与suffix的DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1逐次追加scope=epoch_transition_recovery的deferred rebind。没有holder变化时suffix可为空。所有入口都要求provider自terminal以来没有ordinary、policy、下一epoch或staging mutation；唯一允许的later history是本intent绑定的recovery-only rebind suffix及零ordinary的restricted lease rollover。否则本协议不恢复普通domain effect，固定fail closed。

append-only AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryIntentV1 body字段恰为 `recovery_intent_id`、`recovery_id`、`recovery_application_id`、`transition_id`、`resolution_id`、`cancellation_id`、`terminal_application_id`、`activation_id`、`expected_terminal_outcome`、`provider_terminal_inspect_digest`、`provider_terminal_rebind_digest_or_null`、`provider_terminal_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`expected_provider_current_epoch`、`expected_provider_epoch_transition_chain_digest`、`expected_provider_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest`、`observed_transition_intent_row_count`、`observed_transition_intent_digest_or_null`、`observed_transition_application_row_count`、`observed_transition_application_digest_or_null`、`observed_transition_resolution_row_count`、`observed_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`observed_transition_cancellation_row_count=0`、`observed_terminal_application_row_count=0`、`pre_intent_database_authority_head_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_result_digest_or_null`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_runtime_chain_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`transaction_nonce`、`recovery_reason=pitr_epoch_db_preimage_missing`、`created_at`。gap-anchor result/runtime字段必须等于同row current/latest字段；resolved时provider terminal rebind为null且terminal runtime chain等于gap anchor，cancelled时它是唯一C0→C1 terminal cancellation rebind且terminal runtime chain等于该response digest。前三种observed count各只能0或1并与digest nullability严格对应，application/resolution只能成对0/0或1/1，existing body稍后必须与provider transcript byte-identical；cancellation/terminal application已存在或DB已有gap-anchor result之后的successor时不是本恢复路径。row只引用插入前Head，提交后DatabaseAuthorityHead设置pending recovery intent；suffix ID从此immutable且不能换ID续接。recovery intent与普通pending transition、staging hold/recovery互斥，无TTL、cancel、UPDATE或DELETE。

intent提交后从locked DB重算包含该intent的database rebind-gap anchor Head。若该Head runtime tuple与terminal Provider Head逐字相等且不追加rebind，则取得fresh DatabaseAuthorityHeadAttestationV1；否则取得绑定fixed terminal anchor、same suffix prefix与live candidate的fresh DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1。每次holder/timeline改变只能以后一种attestation追加下一ordinal 1..64；provider只注册new holder、保留terminal outcome、把response追加到rollback-resistant suffix、发rollover-only lease并把Head recovery state设为required，DB对应AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1必须保持absent。最终holder对完整prefix再取得fresh exact-one ordinary/gap attestation、§6.1 row-bound challenge/DatabaseCommitAttestationV1与purpose=epoch_transition_recovery的fresh DatabaseIncarnationProofV1。RecoverAuthorityEpochTransitionRequestV1 body字段恰为 `recovery_id`、`recovery_transcript_id`、`recovery_intent_digest`、`recovery_intent_commit_challenge_digest`、`recovery_intent_commit_attestation_digest`、`activation_id`、`transition_id`、`resolution_id`、`cancellation_id`、`terminal_application_id`、`expected_terminal_outcome`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`pre_intent_database_authority_head_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_head_digest`、`database_authority_head_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_rebind_gap_attestation_digest_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_count`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_digest_or_null`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_identity_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_incarnation_registration_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`database_rebind_gap_anchor_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`provider_current_database_identity_digest`、`provider_current_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`provider_current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`provider_current_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`provider_current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`request_nonce`。`recovery_transcript_id=SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.epoch-recovery-transcript-id.v1") || 0x00 || raw recovery UUID || 0x00 || raw suffix UUID || 0x00 || ASCII(canonical count) || 0x00 || raw tail digest-or-32-zero-bytes)`，因此每个suffix prefix有唯一idempotency key且无self-reference。request的gap-anchor六字段必须逐字等于intent current/lineage/registration/latest-result/runtime-chain/binding及embedded anchor Head；provider-current五字段必须逐字等于expected Provider Head、fresh proof与gap attestation candidate。ordinary/gap attestation恰一non-null；ordinary分支要求count=0、tail=null且两组tuple逐字相等，gap分支要求attestation/suffix count/tail、commit proof与provider-current proof逐字相等。provider消费exact challenge，验证DB anchor、完整deferred suffix与immutable terminal ledger，只能导出原CAS已决定的resolved或cancelled_by_rebind outcome，不能重跑或改选terminal CAS。

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryV1 body字段恰为request全部字段加 `request_digest`、`provider_current_epoch`、`provider_epoch_transition_chain_digest`、`provider_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_runtime_chain_digest`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix`、`terminal_db_group_anchor_state`、`source_database_commit_anchor_schema`、`source_database_commit_anchor_digest`、`transcript_count`、`transcript`、`provider_consumed_database_anchor_count`、`provider_consumed_database_anchors`、`result_provider_head_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`recovered_at`。deferred suffix数组长度必须等于request count并按ordinal 1..count连续，每项字段恰为 `ordinal`、`rebind_id`、`gap_attestation_digest`、`prefix_decision_digest_or_null`、`prefix_decision_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`prefix_decision_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`runtime_lease_termination_digest`、`timeline_lineage_attestation_digest_or_null`、`authorization_digest`、`request_digest`、`evidence_bundle_digest`、`rebinding_registration_digest`、`response_digest`、`post_provider_head_digest`、`previous_runtime_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_chain_digest`、`previous_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`previous_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`initial_restricted_lease_digest_or_null`。required→rebind项的三个decision字段全null；transcript_issued→replacement项三者全present并逐字等于authorization proof。count=0时数组空、tail digest null且tail runtime chain等于provider terminal runtime chain；count>0时request tail等于最后response、tail runtime chain等于最后current chain。terminal_db_group_anchor_state只有unconsumed或provider_consumed_exact。transcript按ordinal从0连续排序，每项字段恰为 `ordinal`、`transcript_kind`、`schema`、`body_digest`、`canonical_body_or_null`、`canonical_envelope_or_null`，kind只有provider_request_body、canonical_evidence_bundle、database_immutable_body或external_signed_envelope，两个nullable preimage恰一非null。provider_consumed_database_anchors按 `(schema,body_digest)` 排序，每项字段恰为 `schema`、`body_digest`、`first_consuming_provider_object_digest`。

resolved exact-set必须覆盖原intent、transition request/bundle/response、DB application+resolution、resolution commit challenge/attestation、Resolve request/bundle/receipt，以及provider若已消费的原terminal application与lease-renew transcript；cancelled exact-set必须覆盖intent、可选成对application/resolution、terminal cancellation rebind authorization/request/bundle/rebinding-registration/response，以及provider若已消费的原rebind-result+cancellation+terminal-application三row与catchup renew transcript。两分支还必须exact覆盖deferred suffix每个gap attestation、termination、可选lineage、authorization、request、bundle、rebinding-registration、response、post-Head与restricted lease；每个transcript_issued后产生的replacement项还必须覆盖唯一prefix decision body、commit challenge/attestation及被supersede的recovery request/bundle/response/checkpoint Head与失效边。相邻runtime/identity/lineage链必须直连，允许的full-Head间隔只有上段同prefix checkpoint，provider current Head tuple必须等于tail且不能有extra recovery rebind或checkpoint。source commit anchor按terminal分支只能是exact resolution DatabaseCommitAttestationV1或内嵌pre-cancellation Head的DatabaseAuthorityHeadAttestationV1。provider扫描该namespace immutable history并列出每个被后续provider object直接或传递引用的DB body；声称unconsumed但history存在引用、missing/extra/reordered/duplicate suffix/checkpoint/decision preimage、同ID不同body、错误outcome/chain或opaque substitute固定拒绝。response不改变effective/terminal chain，但原子把Head recovery state设为transcript_issued并绑定request digest；同一recovery_transcript ID/body exact retry返回同一response，同prefix任一字段变化fixed conflict。transcript_issued后通常只允许Inspect、以同transaction apply decision保护的exact DB application与terminal-application catchup；若current holder在DB application前永久失效，唯一例外是先提交same prefix的replacement_rebind decision并取得其gap-bound commit proof，再用same intent/suffix追加下一ordinal deferred rebind。provider只消费该exact decision/proof，原子把state退回required、清空request digest并使旧transcript对DB application失效；新holder随后用更长prefix的新deterministic recovery_transcript ID取得完整replacement transcript。旧response永久可审计但其tail不等current Head，不能被采用。apply decision已提交后永远禁止deferred replacement；holder丢失只能走§4.4 epoch_transition_recovery_applied受限rebind。除此之外不得以同recovery ID改写或缩短prefix。

restricted epoch recovery transaction按migration latch→runtime singleton→epoch recovery intent→prefix decision singleton→intent/application/resolution→terminal cancellation result/cancellation/terminal-application→historical replacement decisions→deferred rebind results→recovery application固定顺序加锁，transaction内不调用provider/attestor。它验证fresh provider response、exact-one ordinary/gap attestation、current lineage、DB gap anchor与provider pre-transition Head在非epoch authority/ordinary anchors上exact相等，并要求DB result链尚未越过intent记录的gap anchor。取得全部锁后第一项authoritative write必须为current prefix插入decision kind=apply；non-null prefix key唯一冲突会使整个transaction回滚，因此任何已提交replacement decision都不能与application双赢。随后按依赖顺序只补缺失历史：原transition intent；resolved或cancelled中provider曾观察到的exact application+resolution pair；resolved terminal application，或cancelled分支中对应provider C1的AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1、AuthorityEpochTransitionCancellationV1与terminal application同transaction triple；以及provider transcript中每份已消费但因PITR缺失的older-prefix replacement decision exact historical body。然后按provider suffix ordinal追加每份deferred AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1：cancelled的第一份以前述C1 result为previous，resolved的第一份以gap-anchor result为previous，后续逐字引用同transaction刚插入/复用的直接前驱；每份previous/current runtime chain、identity与lineage以及nullable decision proof必须等于相邻provider response，最后DB result digest/runtime chain/current tuple必须等于provider tail。deferred request观察的latest DB result仍是原gap anchor，不得伪造已提交successor；仅此restricted transaction允许其生成row的previous result不同于request observed anchor。已存在row必须byte-identical；pair/triple/decision proof部分存在、suffix missing/reordered/duplicate、direct-write R2 over missing R1、wrong previous chain、extra successor、ordinary/domain anchor落后、另一个pending transition或staging state固定拒绝。

凡body digest已出现在provider_consumed_database_anchors中，必须逐字恢复provider transcript中的historical body，包括原 `database_transaction_id`、snapshot、database point与time；普通insert guard仍要求这些字段等于current transaction，只有本restricted branch在同时验证provider-signed transcript、原commit/Head attestation及受信lineage后可插入historical bytes。terminal_db_group_anchor_state=unconsumed时，intent/application/resolution仍按provider固定的exact historical body恢复，但尚未被provider引用的resolved terminal application或cancelled triple在本current transaction按原预分配semantic ID创建；不能用current tx重算任何已被provider固定的digest。deferred suffix response已经由provider固定，所以对应result body中provider-derived字段必须逐字来自transcript，只有本transaction定义的recorded point/time按schema生成且整个有序result suffix原子可见。

同transaction最后插入 AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryApplicationV1；body字段恰为 `recovery_application_id`、`recovery_intent_digest`、`recovery_transcript_id`、`provider_epoch_transition_recovery_digest`、`recovery_prefix_key_digest`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_digest`、`transition_id`、`resolution_id`、`cancellation_id`、`terminal_application_id`、`terminal_outcome`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_count`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_digest_or_null`、`provider_tail_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`database_tail_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`database_tail_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`post_rehydration_database_identity_digest`、`post_rehydration_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`source_database_commit_anchor_schema`、`source_database_commit_anchor_digest`、`pre_rehydration_database_authority_head_digest`、`rehydrated_row_count`、`rehydrated_rows`、`post_rehydration_database_authority_head_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`rehydrated_at`。selected transcript ID/digest、suffix count/tail、prefix key、apply decision digest与transaction开始时fresh current Provider Head必须逐字相等；decision与application共享transaction ID/snapshot/database point且decision不反向引用application。任何被更长suffix supersede的旧transcript或replacement decision winner固定拒绝。rehydrated_rows按依赖顺序，每项字段恰为 `schema`、`body_digest`、`rehydration_kind`，kind只有restored_exact_historical_body、reused_exact_existing_body或created_current_transaction_terminal_body；older-prefix replacement decision属于第一种，current apply decision由独立字段绑定且不伪装为rehydrated row。application digest不复制到Head且不推进effective/terminal chain，但其exact存在使pending recovery field清空；post Head只包含该closed result，不引用application或decision digest，因此同transaction可重算而无摘要环。application与decision仍进入fixed registry、readiness audit与Down zero。commit后DB effective/terminal Head及runtime/identity/lineage tail必须分别等于provider terminal与current Head，pending recovery intent清空；provider recovery marker与受限lease仍使ordinary关闭，直到§6.3 terminal_application_catchup成功。

在任何provider消费该apply结果之前，caller必须用§6.1 `row_kind=authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application`、`commit_proof_purpose=external_admission`为exact recovery application取得fresh challenge/DatabaseCommitAttestationV1。其related-row set与archive_fences必须分别exact覆盖同transaction apply decision body以及application+decision两个global keys；attestor trusted-read两row、验证application单向引用decision、transaction ID/snapshot/database point逐字相等，并在一个archive transaction中锁定两个body latch、related edge、primary challenge/attestation entry。apply decision自身仍不取得独立challenge或entry。该application effect只能由直接terminal_application_catchup或首个scope=epoch_transition_recovery_applied rebind之一首次原子消费；Provider Head CAS同时写入first consumer及§6.1 history ordinal 1，竞争者只能在刷新Head与proof后按已有history继续。

每次applied-scope rebind及每次catchup都必须使用本次live candidate上的fresh trusted-read application attestation与signed matched Inspect response，不能只传递第一份challenge/attestation。尚未消费时purpose仍为external_admission；一旦history nonempty，purpose必须为archive_continuity，body/set与global generations保持不变但archive_sequence继续增长。fresh candidate尚未成为provider holder时，§4.4 PostRecovery attestation先从机械continuity anchor验证旧holder/history与新candidate，再作为challenge唯一bridge；provider current holder上的catchup proof则不需要该bridge。provider成功mutation只把本次fresh entry追加为history event，first consumer永不改变。proof尚未取得、matched Inspect不含本次entry、archive closure不完整或history tail不直连时，applied rebind、catchup、ordinary proof与readiness全部关闭。response loss按recovery application ID、apply decision、post Head、challenge与archive Inspect exact检查；DB row仍在时不得重跑transaction。

再次PITR沿同一recovery恢复而不得改用另一个 `recovery_id`。若两个global latches均absent且provider history为空，restricted rerun必须先在live candidate row仍为0时取得application+decision两份signed Inspect precondition，再以§6.1 `fence_reason=epoch_recovery_pair_rerun`原子推进两个generation；只有fence成功后才能用同一预分配decision/application semantic IDs提交current transaction bodies并取得绑定新generation的challenge。旧attestation append先赢时fence失败且只能exact restore旧body；fence先赢时所有旧challenge永久stale。禁止在未fence时仅“废弃旧challenge”后生成different transaction-local body。

若任一global latch已经锁定，则无论provider是否已消费，都只能在historical restricted branch从application primary Inspect的exact body+related edge/global decision latch逐字恢复成对body及原transaction ID/snapshot/database point/time，并要求application attestation的exact COMMIT LSN严格晚于timeline_fork_cut；related-only decision不能单独充当fork-cut proof。若provider已有history，bundle还必须逐字恢复每个fresh application proof、全部已成功applied rebind/result、唯一catchup及marker-cleared rebind state；history tail、current Provider Head与DB result链必须直连。marker已清且catchup后零unrelated authority mutation时只可进入§4.4 `post_catchup_marker_cleared`；发生later ordinary/policy/epoch/staging mutation、provider消费记录与archive不一致、缺related body/global edge、pending result未先reconcile或commit LSN不满足§4.2时固定fail closed。

缺失applied-result按“谁第一次把该result digest作为accepted DB tail消费”唯一分支恢复：下一mutation是applied rebind时，只能用§4.2 exact next-winning PostRecovery witness恢复D_i；下一mutation是terminal_application_catchup时，只能用该winning Renew bundle中本来就要求的fresh DatabaseAuthorityHeadAttestationV1，其内嵌Head/latest-result、exact D_i preimage、request DB Head与provider winning CAS必须直连，且D_i.recorded_database_point同样严格晚于cut；没有下一provider consumer的history tail只能用§4.4 dedicated result reconcile从保存的同rebind authorization/request/registration/response/post-Head生成或逐字恢复该单一result，不能借未消费PostRecovery/Head proof进入historical branch。一个result只匹配一行，later applied rebind优先于更晚catchup而不能由caller选更方便的witness；missing/extra/alternate witness、consumer顺序不直连或两类witness同时声称first consumer固定拒绝。

AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryPrefixDecisionV1 是每个可应用transcript prefix的DB内唯一仲裁。body字段恰为 `decision_id`、`recovery_prefix_key_digest`、`activation_id`、`recovery_id`、`recovery_intent_digest`、`recovery_transcript_id`、`provider_epoch_transition_recovery_digest`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_count`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_digest_or_null`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`expected_provider_recovery_request_digest`、`decision_kind`、`next_deferred_runtime_rebind_ordinal_or_null`、`pre_decision_database_authority_head_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`transaction_nonce`、`decided_at`。decision kind只有apply或replacement_rebind；row append-only且禁止UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE。该body只仲裁decision kind、recovery/prefix/current transcript/expected Provider Head、replacement时的next ordinal与pre-decision DB tuple，绝不绑定candidate、rebind ID、termination、new identity/lineage/runtime/lease。

在任一decision transaction开始前，B11必须通过commit-attestor/Fence signer的authenticated reservation API，先在其位于DB/PITR failure domain之外的rollback-resistant semantic-ID journal对 `(activation_id,recovery_id,recovery_prefix_key_digest)` subject slot执行absent→decision_id CAS，再写reason-specific semantic-key record；B11只编排调用，不能直接写archive。该subject slot跨epoch_recovery_pair_rerun与epoch_recovery_replacement_decision_rerun共用且另有decision_id→slot反向唯一索引；同prefix所有exact retry、两种decision kind及未归档application pair的PITR重跑只能复用slot已绑定的decision ID，不能靠换reason、record ID、record time或key set生成alias。recovery application ID仍取自recovery intent预分配值并与pair reason record的两键exact集合绑定；replacement reason record只含同一个slot decision ID的单键。journal只固定semantic IDs而不固定candidate或current transaction-local body；archive一旦锁定body则以archive为准。trusted subject slot/record缺失、seal/rollback floor无效、same prefix出现第二decision ID或same decision ID跨prefix固定fail closed。

`recovery_prefix_key_digest=SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.epoch-recovery-prefix-key.v1") || 0x00 || raw recovery UUID || 0x00 || raw suffix UUID || 0x00 || ASCII(canonical count) || 0x00 || raw tail digest-or-32-zero-bytes)`，数据库以这个始终non-null的digest建UNIQUE并另有CHECK `count=0 ⇔ tail=null`，绝不依赖PostgreSQL默认允许多个NULL的nullable UNIQUE。apply允许count 0..64且next ordinal必须null，并与recovery application同transaction提交；replacement_rebind只允许count 0..63且next ordinal=count+1，在独立transaction提交。两者都绑定current transcript_issued Head/request、same recovery/intent/transcript/suffix prefix与pre-decision DB tuple；decision不引用candidate、自身commit proof、未来authorization/rebind response或application digest。两条并发路径取得同一lock set并争用同一prefix key，先提交者永久胜出：apply winner禁止deferred replacement，replacement winner禁止DB application，但只预定不可变next ordinal而不预定候选。未提交decision可重选；已提交replacement后，每个候选都必须取得fresh DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1、fresh challenge/attestation与fresh authorization/request。同一gap/proof不得改candidate；若candidate在provider CAS前死亡或lease失效，旧gap/challenge/authorization作废，可为同一immutable decision row选择新candidate并追加新的archive challenge/attestation。provider只有在successful rebind CAS中才原子消费decision；pre-CAS failure不消费它，CAS成功后所有其他candidate proof因Head/decision single-use而stale。

replacement decision commit后若archive已经锁定其body而provider尚未消费，后续PITR丢row时必须按§4.2用exact locked body与任一matched commit attestation严格晚于fork cut恢复，再为当前candidate取得fresh gap/challenge；不能因原candidate失效改decision。若global latch absent、没有matched archived attestation且provider ledger也无consumer，恢复点只能先用candidate row count=0的fresh Inspect与§6.1 `fence_reason=epoch_recovery_replacement_decision_rerun`推进该decision key generation，再在same prefix lock下用journaled semantic ID重新仲裁。旧append先赢则exact restore，fence先赢则旧challenge永久stale；一旦archive或provider任一侧存在证据，就只能exact restore/exact retry。

replacement decision提交后按DAG `transcript/Head → prefix decision → fresh gap attestation(candidate,decision digest) → candidate-bound row challenge/attestation → authorization/rebind CAS → next transcript` 执行；§6.1 archive可为同一locked decision body保存多份不同candidate challenge，commit proof通过gap digest绑定candidate，authorization/request/provider response/DB deferred result与下一transcript exact-set逐字传递实际赢家的decision与proof。provider消费后第二次PITR缺row时，只能用provider transcript中的exact body以及winning challenge/attestation按§4.2严格晚于fork cut规则恢复；缺任一preimage永久fail closed。apply DAG为 `transcript/Head → same-transaction apply decision+recovery application → atomic two-key application proof → fresh matched proof on each consumer candidate → direct catchup或applied rebind CAS → history-bound fresh continuity proof → catchup/marker-cleared resume`；provider consumer不反向写入两份DB body，history event digest不引用response digest，因此两条DAG都无decision↔proof/response/application摘要环。

### 4.7 EmptyDatabaseInventoryV1 与 durable attempt

production Up transaction自动提交 AuthorityProtocolUpgradeIntentV1；body字段恰为 `intent_id`、`installation_id`、`installation_kind=production`、`activation_id`、`request_nonce`、`credential_policy_update_id_or_null`、`incarnation_registration_id`、`provider_absence_proof_id`、`observed_deployment_id`、`database_identity_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`classification_state=pending`、`created_at`。这些ID/body在任何对应外部调用前由B11 rollback-resistant journal生成，Up复制到immutable row；缺provider evidence时policy ID可null但其余未来ID仍预分配。它只声明“此数据库已进入不可Down的生产分类”，不声称外部环境为空；row禁止UPDATE/DELETE，并先于任何post-Up credential policy、incarnation registration、absence proof consumption或genesis call存在。

EmptyDatabaseInventoryV1 body 字段恰为 `schema_version`、`activation_id`、`deployment_id`、`database_legacy_absence_projection_digest`、`attempt_database_observation_digest`、`provider_namespace_absence_digest`、`database_incarnation_attestation_digest`、`database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`request_nonce`。它是attempt-time object，不在activation阶段伪称产生相同MVCC observation。

coordinator 在锁定扫描事务中重新验证 §4.2–§4.6 后，预先生成全部外部mutation ID并提交 immutable AuthorityProtocolUpgradeAttemptV1，随后才能调用 PrepareClaimV1Genesis。其 body 字段恰为 `attempt_id`、`upgrade_intent_digest`、`activation_id`、`preparation_id`、`completion_id`、`release_preparation_id`、`open_id`、`mode`、`deployment_id`、`request_nonce`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`database_legacy_absence_projection_digest`、`attempt_database_observation_digest`、`provider_namespace_absence_digest`、`database_inventory_digest`、`database_incarnation_attestation_digest`、`database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`created_at`。attempt最多一行且禁止UPDATE/DELETE；它必须引用DB最新runtime result链与provider `registered_pending_genesis` Head。prepared-only crash可从row恢复preparation ID，PITR或response loss不能令Down重新合法。

### 4.8 ClaimV1GenesisPreparationV1

PrepareClaimV1GenesisRequestV1 body 字段恰为 `preparation_id`、`attempt_digest`、`activation_id`、`mode`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`protocol_profile=claim_v1`、`deployment_id`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`database_inventory_digest`、`database_legacy_absence_projection_digest`、`provider_namespace_absence_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`request_nonce`。provider要求expected Head恰为registered_pending_genesis、DB result链current registration/runtime逐字段相等且没有unresolved rebind/epoch/staging transition。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1GenesisPreparationV1 response使用相同字段并额外恰有 `provider_history_empty_digest`、`provider_history_high_water`、`consumed_absence_proof_nonce`、`phase=genesis_prepared`、`prepared_at`。

provider 必须证明 namespace 从未存在 reservation、terminal record、epoch transition 或 cutover history，consume exact fresh ProviderNamespaceAbsenceV1；provider_history_empty_digest必须逐字等于该absence envelope body digest，不能是另一个opaque summary。inventory在prepared_at时等于完整latest anchor set，credential policy、runtime binding与incarnation registration当时有效，selected epoch/root符合 §4.6。genesis_prepared 期间 Reserve、Finalize、Abort、epoch transition 与普通 Head mutation 全部拒绝。

preparation ID/body immutable、single-use、rollback-resistant 且可 Inspect。相同 ID 与相同 body 是 idempotent；任一字段改变固定 conflict。response loss 只能 Inspect exact ID，不能创建第二 namespace/epoch。Preparation 不通过 TTL、取消、PITR 或数据库回滚重新开放 legacy 协议；废弃时只能永久封存 namespace并以新 activation/nonce/namespace开始。

## 5. Additive schema 与单向协议闩锁

### 5.1 migration 所有权

00006_nodecontrol.sql 只为每个现有 PL/pgSQL function 增加独立 Goose StatementBegin/StatementEnd annotation；不改变函数体、catalog semantics 或 manifest source。

所有新列、约束、trigger、guard、upgrade/source/activation objects 与 outcome/evidence columns 都由registered `00007_nodecontrol_authority_abort_serving.go` migration管理；它在Goose-owned `*sql.Tx`中执行B01审计并embed的literal Up/Down SQL assets。普通Goose CLI不注册该migration，B11 authoritative executor通过context传递typed Up/Down grant并在同一transaction完成Go verification、DB重扫、DDL与goose version写入。00007 创建的 protocol tables 是：

- control_plane_authority_protocol_migration_latches；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_downgrade_authorizations；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_upgrade_intents；
- control_plane_authority_runtime_registration_results；
- control_plane_authority_runtime_rebind_results；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_upgrade_attempts；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_intents；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_applications；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_resolutions；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_cancellations；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_terminal_applications；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_recovery_intents；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decisions；
- control_plane_authority_epoch_transition_recovery_applications；
- control_plane_authority_legacy_database_source_retirements；
- control_plane_authority_indeterminate_source_seals；
- control_plane_authority_legacy_source_seals；
- control_plane_authority_fresh_restore_requirements；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_activations；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_activation_completions；
- control_plane_authority_protocol_activation_releases；
- control_plane_authority_staging_import_capabilities；
- control_plane_authority_staging_import_capability_revocation_applications；
- control_plane_authority_staging_import_capability_recovery_intents；
- control_plane_authority_staging_import_capability_recovery_applications；
- control_plane_authority_fresh_restore_import_applications。

00007 Up在同一migration transaction自动插入AuthorityProtocolMigrationLatchV1；body字段恰为 `installation_id`、`installation_kind`、`migration_version=7`、`database_identity_digest`、`up_catalog_digest`、`down_state=locked`、`installed_at`，installation_kind只有production或disposable_fixture。production还原子插入§4.7 upgrade intent；缺失/重复latch或production intent时runtime和Down都fail closed。latch、intent、runtime registration result、attempt、每种local source seal/retirement、requirement与activation/completion/release各自singleton；runtime rebind result、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal-application/recovery-intent/recovery-prefix-decision/recovery-application、staging capability/revocation/application/recovery-intent/recovery-application与downgrade authorization按各自ID append-only并强制previous或terminal链。五张epoch/staging recovery intent/decision/application表都是INSERT-only，禁止UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE；application FK exact intent，epoch prefix decision使用始终non-null的recovery_prefix_key_digest UNIQUE与count/tail CHECK，epoch recovery application是不推进effective/terminal epoch chain的审计sidecar，但其存在按封闭matrix清除pending recovery projection。DatabaseTimelineLineageAttestationV1、DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1与DatabaseAuthorityPostRecoveryRebindAttestationV1都是external signed object，exact envelope存于runtime rebind result/evidence/transcript而不另建物理表。staging capability是import前immutable registration row，effective state从intent/application/revocation/recovery-application rows派生，不做UPDATE consume；recovery intent一旦存在只允许recovery协议，recovery application一旦存在只允许recovery revocation。只有downgrade authorization/latch被Down原子consume。production intent、runtime result、attempt、任一source retirement/seal/requirement、activation/completion/release、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal-application/recovery intent/prefix decision/application、staging capability/revocation/application/recovery intent/application或claim-v1 row存在都永久阻止Down；latch本身只能由§9.3 registered Go Down transaction消费。

### 5.2 fence profile

control_plane_authority_fences 增加显式 profile 与字段：

- authority_protocol_profile：legacy_v6 或 claim_v1；
- abort_claimed_at；
- protocol_activation_id。

既存行只标为 legacy_v6。legacy_v6 的 abort_claimed_at 与 protocol_activation_id 必须为空，R0/R1/C/A0/A1 均保持原样。

claim_v1 行必须显式写 profile并引用唯一 activation；禁止用列默认值让旧 binary 看似产生新协议记录。

### 5.3 activation barrier objects

AuthorityProtocolActivationV1 body 字段恰为 `activation_id`、`mode`、`attempt_digest`、`deployment_id`、`database_identity_digest`、`database_incarnation_attestation_digest`、`genesis_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`activation_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_legacy_absence_projection_digest`、`attempt_database_inventory_digest`、`activation_database_observation_digest`、`provider_namespace_absence_digest`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`protocol_profile=claim_v1`、`preparation_digest`、`activated_at`。activation transaction重算stable projection并要求等于attempt；它创建新的stage=activation observation而不要求snapshot digest等于attempt observation。

AuthorityProtocolActivationCompletionV1 body 字段恰为 `completion_id`、`activation_id`、`activation_digest`、`preparation_digest`、`provider_completion_digest`、`provider_completion_phase=genesis_completed_pending_release`、`database_activation_attestation_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`completed_at`。

AuthorityProtocolActivationReleaseV1 body 字段恰为 `release_preparation_id`、`open_id`、`activation_id`、`activation_digest`、`completion_digest`、`provider_release_preparation_digest`、`provider_release_phase=genesis_release_prepared`、`database_completion_attestation_digest`、`open_nonce`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`released_at`。

三张 row 均最多一行、INSERT 后禁止 UPDATE/DELETE；后一个对象逐字段引用前一个 canonical digest。任何 mismatch、额外 row 或 missing predecessor 固定拒绝。

### 5.4 claim-aware constraints

claim_v1 的有限状态必须由数据库约束与 trigger共同保证：

- unclaimed pending：abort_reason、abort_claimed_at 与 binding 均可为空；
- claimed pending：abort_reason 与 abort_claimed_at 同时存在，binding 完全为空；
- bound pending：binding 完整，claim 完全为空；
- committed terminal：binding与receipt完整，claim为空；
- aborted terminal：receipt、reason、claim与terminal time完整，binding为空。

时间顺序固定为 terminal_at >= abort_claimed_at >= reserved_at。Bind/commit 要求 claim absent；Abort terminalization 要求 exact stored reason/time claim。profile、activation ID、claim reason/time、reservation identity与既有 binding 均不可改写。

00007 必须替换 v6 的 abort-reason pair constraint为 profile-aware约束；不能在 legacy aborted 行上直接增加全局 (abort_reason IS NULL) = (abort_claimed_at IS NULL)。

### 5.5 domain 与 source-freeze guards

activation/completion/release 任一缺失时，所有 authority-bearing domain writer拒绝写入。完整 barrier存在时，新写必须：

1. 在 caller-owned DBTX 中先锁 exact fence；
2. 验证 claim-v1 profile、activation ID、incarnation registration/lease、operation/kind/scope/digest/epoch/sequence；
3. 验证 phase与claim允许该操作；
4. 才能 INSERT、UPDATE 或 DELETE 领域行。

DELETE 使用 OLD。Legacy domain 行保持原始 nullable outcome/evidence fields；不得通过 migration补写新语义。

IndeterminateSourceSealV1、LegacySourceSealV1 或 FreshRestoreRequirementV1 存在时，fence trigger 明确拒绝 legacy_v6 R0/R1 的 Bind/Finalize/Abort 与所有 R0/R1/C/A0/A1 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE；同一 source-freeze helper 被 19 条 authority-bearing domain writer以及 credential、serving、pointer、pending/recovery tables 的 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE trigger调用。guard registry 是 migration manifest 的固定清单，catalog test 必须证明没有漏表或漏 trigger。

唯一无production lease写分支是§7.2 staging_import：只在mode=fresh_restore_target、provider exclusion处于durable `held`、locked stable Head phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed、provider serving lease=null、route closed且同一transaction锁定预注册的exact StagingImportCapabilityV1并证明application=0、revocation=0时触发。capability/admission expiry只决定能否开始，不释放held exclusion；事务提交时不再依赖剩余TTL。该分支只允许manifest中pop/failure-domain/capacity-profile INSERT与固定disabled node reconstruction INSERT；拒绝UPDATE/DELETE、fence/credential/serving/pointer/pending/recovery写、未列row或任一payload变化。普通writer role不能创建capability、application或revocation，也不能选择该branch。

## 6. Genesis activation handshake

### 6.1 五阶段 closed-until-open 协议

协议不假装 PostgreSQL 与 provider 具有分布式事务；在最终 Open 前 provider始终拒绝普通 authority 操作：

前置 Runtime registration：provider registration先建立registered_pending_genesis closed Head；DB提交AuthorityRuntimeRegistrationResultV1后才允许durable attempt。若此时postmaster restart，使用该DB result/Head走§6.3 closed-phase rebind并提交AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1，不需要伪造Prepare或废弃同一activation。
0. Durable attempt：锁定扫描事务提交 AuthorityProtocolUpgradeAttemptV1；没有该 row 禁止调用 provider。
1. Provider preparation：PrepareClaimV1Genesis 创建 genesis_prepared record，绑定 attempt、incarnation、credential policy与selected epoch并阻断普通 provider操作。
2. Database activation：锁定完整表集，重算stable DatabaseLegacyAbsenceProjectionV1、创建stage=activation observation，验证 exact preparation，在一个事务中插入 AuthorityProtocolActivationV1并提交；事务中不调用 provider。提交后在事务外取得row-bound provider commit challenge，incarnation attestor自行读DB并签署 DatabaseCommitAttestationV1。
3. Completion：CompleteClaimV1Genesis原子消费activation challenge、验证database activation attestation并进入 genesis_completed_pending_release，仍阻断普通操作；随后数据库提交 AuthorityProtocolActivationCompletionV1，再按同一challenge/read-back协议签completion attestation。
4. Release preparation：用 completion row的DatabaseCommitAttestationV1调用PrepareClaimV1GenesisRelease并原子消费completion challenge；provider进入 genesis_release_prepared并返回crypto-random single-use open_nonce，仍阻断普通操作；数据库把nonce写入 AuthorityProtocolActivationReleaseV1并提交，再取得release challenge/read-back attestation。
5. Open：release row提交后才计算normal Open request body digest，并以release commit attestation和stored open_nonce调用OpenClaimV1Genesis。empty_in_place原子进入active并发放首个one-live serving lease；fresh_restore_target原子进入fresh_v7_staging_closed、lease为null并继续拒绝普通operation。open_nonce替代预计算expected digest，因此不存在release/open fixed-point cycle。

每张activation/completion/release、epoch-transition-resolution、epoch-transition-recovery-intent、epoch-transition-recovery-prefix-decision、epoch-transition-recovery-application、staging-import-capability-registration、staging-import-capability-recovery-intent或staging-import-capability-recovery-application row提交后且在下一次对应外部admission前，caller先发送 AcquireDatabaseCommitChallengeRequestV1；body字段恰为 `challenge_id`、`activation_id`、`row_kind`、`semantic_row_id`、`row_digest`、`related_row_set_digest_or_null`、`commit_proof_purpose`、`archive_fence_count`、`archive_fences`、`archive_generation_evidence_set_digest`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`expected_provider_phase`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_authority_rebind_gap_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_post_recovery_rebind_attestation_digest_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_count`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_digest_or_null`、`request_nonce`，row_kind只有activation、completion、release、epoch_transition_resolution、authority_epoch_transition_recovery_intent、authority_epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision、authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application、staging_import_capability_registration、staging_import_capability_recovery_intent或staging_import_capability_recovery_application。semantic_row_id由fixed row-kind registry指定的body ID字段重算，caller不能另选alias。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 DatabaseCommitChallengeV1 body字段恰为同一组字段加 `request_digest`、`provider_challenge_nonce`、`challenge_sequence`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过2分钟；同challenge ID/body exact retry，字段变化conflict。

`commit_proof_purpose`只有external_admission或archive_continuity。external_admission可建立global body latch，或在同body已经锁定但尚无provider first consumer时为新的live candidate追加entry；archive_continuity只允许规范明确列出的已被provider消费或已terminal的exact locked body/set，为下一次rebind、catchup、Release或Abort证明本次candidate continuity，绝不再次应用原effect、改变first consumer或换body。activation、completion、release、epoch resolution/recovery intent/replacement decision、staging capability registration与staging recovery intent固定只能external_admission；只有epoch recovery application及staging recovery application可按各自consumer history使用archive_continuity。`archive_fences`按 `(row_schema,semantic_row_id)` 的canonical UTF-8 bytes排序，每项字段恰为 `row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`archive_fence_generation`；authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application的count恰为2并exact覆盖primary application与related apply decision两个全局semantic key，其他row kind count恰为1并只覆盖primary key。`archive_generation_evidence_set_digest=SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-generation-evidence-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(bytewise-sorted array of {row_schema,semantic_row_id,archive_fence_generation,evidence_schema,evidence_digest}))`；数组key set必须与archive_fences exact相等，每项evidence只能是fresh signed InspectDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveResponseV1或FenceDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveResponseV1的exact envelope且逐字证明该generation，bundle必须携带全部preimage。evidence为Fence response时还必须展开其exact request、reason-specific authorization evidence与全部precondition Inspect envelopes；这些Inspect的expected Provider Head与candidate tuple必须逐字等于本challenge，Fence reason/key registry也须重新验证，不能把另一Head/candidate或错误reason推进的generation带入challenge。missing/extra/duplicate key、generation splice或同key混用两个evidence固定拒绝。

只有row_kind=authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application时related-row set必须non-null，且恰为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-related-row-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(bytewise-sorted exact-one array of {row_schema,semantic_row_id,row_digest}))`，唯一item必须是same transaction且被application单向引用的apply AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryPrefixDecisionV1；decision不含且不得新增application ID/digest。其他row kind该字段必须null。除recovery intent、replacement prefix decision与携带post-recovery attestation的recovery application三个例外外，gap/post-recovery attestation、suffix/tail必须null且count=0，DB identity/lineage/registration/runtime tuple必须与expected Provider Head逐字相等。recovery intent row kind必须引用intent的suffix ID；tuple仍相等时gap=null、count=0、tail=null，只有provider terminal/recovery-only runtime chain领先DB gap anchor时才可gap non-null。prefix decision row kind只接受decision_kind=replacement_rebind、Head state=transcript_issued、count 0..63与next ordinal=count+1，且gap attestation必须逐字绑定该decision digest、same intent/prefix、该次candidate与expected Head；decision body本身不含candidate，apply decision永不独立取得外部commit challenge。两个gap例外的exact DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1都必须连接DB anchor与provider current tuple，不允许其他row kind或scope复用。recovery application的post-recovery attestation例外只允许§4.4 fresh candidate proof：gap必须null，post-recovery attestation、candidate tuple、两份basis Inspect及provider history逐字相等；basis=unlocked且history为空时purpose必须external_admission并由本attestation建立两个latches，basis=locked且history nonempty时purpose必须archive_continuity，locked但尚未消费时仍可external_admission。provider current holder上的direct catchup/continuity proof则post-recovery attestation为null且tuple exact匹配current Head。gap与post-recovery attestation不得同时non-null。

下一对应provider transition原子消费challenge；两个staging intent/registration challenge分别只能由引用exact capability row的AcquireFreshV7StagingExclusion或引用exact recovery intent的RecoverStagingImportCapability消费；epoch recovery-intent challenge只能由携带相同suffix count/tail的exact RecoverAuthorityEpochTransitionRequestV1消费；prefix-decision challenge只能由同decision/gap-bound candidate/ordinal的exact recovery rebind CAS消费，pre-CAS candidate failure可为same decision取得新gap与新challenge；epoch recovery-application external_admission challenge只能由direct terminal catchup或首个epoch_transition_recovery_applied rebind首次消费，archive_continuity challenge只能由exact同recovery的后继applied rebind或catchup消费。每次consumer都必须使用本次live candidate上fresh trusted-read attestation与matched Inspect response；provider first-consumer CAS、history-tail CAS与Head mutation为同一原子mutation。staging recovery-application challenge由fresh_restore_staging_import_revoker在按row digest签发唯一revocation ID时记入rollback-resistant single-use registry，不能跨application或revocation ID复用。过期但未消费的challenge可用新ID重取，不改变immutable DB row。

fixed row-kind registry是下表封闭的10项；`semantic_row_id` 必须逐字取指定body字段，`row_schema` 由row_kind机械导出而不接受caller另传alias：

| row_kind | row_schema | semantic_id_field | related_rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| activation | `authority-protocol-activation.v1` | `activation_id` | zero |
| completion | `authority-protocol-activation-completion.v1` | `completion_id` | zero |
| release | `authority-protocol-activation-release.v1` | `release_preparation_id` | zero |
| epoch_transition_resolution | `authority-epoch-transition-resolution.v1` | `resolution_id` | zero |
| authority_epoch_transition_recovery_intent | `authority-epoch-transition-recovery-intent.v1` | `recovery_intent_id` | zero |
| authority_epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision | `authority-epoch-transition-recovery-prefix-decision.v1` | `decision_id` | zero |
| authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application | `authority-epoch-transition-recovery-application.v1` | `recovery_application_id` | exact-one `authority-epoch-transition-recovery-prefix-decision.v1` / `decision_id` / `decision_kind=apply` |
| staging_import_capability_registration | `staging-import-capability.v1` | `capability_id` | zero |
| staging_import_capability_recovery_intent | `staging-import-capability-recovery-intent.v1` | `recovery_intent_id` | zero |
| staging_import_capability_recovery_application | `staging-import-capability-recovery-application.v1` | `recovery_application_id` | zero |

registry以row_kind为唯一key；schema/ID字段、semantic ID、related cardinality或related schema任一变化固定拒绝。即使body另含activation/open/recovery/capability等ID，也不能选作archive key。

signer_role=`database_incarnation` 的 DatabaseCommitAttestationV1 body 字段恰为 `activation_id`、`row_kind`、`semantic_row_id`、`row_digest`、`related_row_set_digest_or_null`、`commit_proof_purpose`、`archive_fence_count`、`archive_fences`、`archive_generation_evidence_set_digest`、`commit_challenge_digest`、`postgres_system_id`、`timeline`、`database_oid`、`database_name`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`incarnation_id`、`incarnation_public_key_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`database_authority_rebind_gap_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_post_recovery_rebind_attestation_digest_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_count`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_tail_digest_or_null`、`commit_lsn`、`provider_challenge_nonce`、`challenge_sequence`、`archive_sequence`、`previous_archive_entry_digest_or_null`、`attested_at`。incarnation attestor不接受caller提供的row body/digest/LSN；它通过与exact runtime binding相互认证的只读DB连接，按challenge row_kind/semantic ID自行读取唯一immutable row、canonicalize并重算digest，确认row已在另一个transaction提交可见，并从PostgreSQL/data-directory之外的trusted WAL archive/decoder把该row version的xmin/inserting top-level transaction ID映射到同一postgres_system_id与原timeline上的exact COMMIT record end LSN（两阶段事务则为exact COMMIT PREPARED record end LSN）。`commit_lsn`只能是该精确终结记录位置，绝不能是稍后的flush/replay/current-WAL/observation point；记录缺失、xid映射歧义或timeline不可比一律不签。

authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application还要求attestor按related set自行trusted-read exact apply decision，重算其schema/semantic ID/body digest，验证application.`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_digest`单向等于该decision body digest；decision不引用application，related-row set则独立承诺decision schema/semantic ID/body digest。两row的database_transaction_id、transaction_snapshot_digest与database_point必须逐字相等；primary或related row不存在、多行、different或transaction不一致一律不签。其他row kind related字段必须null。current runtime lease仍有效后才签署；live tuple必须重算current identity并验证完整lineage chain。gap=null且post-recovery attestation=null时还必须逐字等于challenge的provider tuple；两种attestation恰一non-null时必须分别满足上段gap或§4.4 post-recovery唯一例外并逐字回显digest/candidate/suffix/history tuple，二者不得同时present。所有purpose、fence数组、generation evidence、suffix与related set逐字回显challenge。attestor在完成trusted-read、WAL定位与签名body构造以后、最终archive append CAS内再次验证每个global key的current generation仍等于archive_fences；stale generation即使body相同也不得append。不存在、多行、未提交、wrong predecessor、caller-supplied LSN、未授权tuple mismatch或DB identity/lineage mismatch固定拒绝，所以non-exportable key不能退化为签名oracle。

commit attestor在PostgreSQL、data directory与VM backup domain之外维护统一rollback-resistant archive。每个全局semantic key恰为 `(row_schema,semantic_row_id)`，role、owner或primary ID不进入key；每个key独立保存初始为1的`archive_fence_generation`与absent-or-exact-one canonical body latch。body latch与primary attestation stream分层：replacement decision作为primary与apply decision作为application related row命中同一个decision global key；exact body可复用，different body永久conflict。application related edge只保存decision schema/ID/digest引用，canonical decision body只存于global latch；apply decision不产生自身entry，replacement decision则拥有自己的primary stream。协议角色仍固定拒绝apply-as-primary与replacement-as-related。

authority_epoch_transition_recovery_application attestation在一个archive transaction中按canonical UTF-8 bytewise key顺序锁定application与decision两个global latches，逐键执行absent→insert、exact→reuse、different→conflict；随后在仍持有decision latch时先锁/写application forward related-edge并对该latch内唯一reverse-owner字段执行CAS，成功后才锁application primary stream/head。其他row kind只锁一个primary global latch。全局锁序固定为sorted global latches→forward related-edge row与已持有decision latch内的reverse-owner CAS→primary stream/head→challenge exact-retry key；任何实现都不得在取得primary stream/head后再获取related-edge锁。随后同key下原子分配严格递增archive_sequence并令previous_archive_entry_digest_or_null等于上一attestation body digest，sequence=1时previous=null；secondary exact-retry key恰为 `(row_schema,semantic_row_id,commit_challenge_digest)`，相同challenge永远返回byte-identical attestation且不追加sequence。body latches、related edges、exact challenge envelope、signed attestation envelope与entry/head必须同一事务提交后才可返回；任一步失败整体回滚，不得留下orphan latch、partial edge或无entry的primary stream。application proof与replacement-decision primary proof反向竞争同一decision key时只能一个完整提交。

不同body重跑前必须先关闭所有旧generation的迟到append。FenceDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveRequestV1 body字段恰为 `fence_request_id`、`activation_id`、`fence_reason`、`fence_reason_authorization_evidence_set_digest`、`fence_attempt_key_digest`、`archive_key_count`、`archive_keys`、`precondition_inspect_response_set_digest`、`request_nonce`；`fence_reason`只有epoch_recovery_pair_rerun、epoch_recovery_replacement_decision_rerun、staging_generic_row_rerun或staging_outcome_row_rerun。archive_keys按global key bytewise排序，每项字段恰为 `row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`expected_archive_fence_generation`、`precondition_archive_inspect_response_digest`。reason registry封闭为：epoch_recovery_pair_rerun必须exact覆盖same recovery intent预分配的AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryApplicationV1 key与same prefix的apply AuthorityEpochTransitionRecoveryPrefixDecisionV1 key；epoch_recovery_replacement_decision_rerun必须恰为provider recovery transcript/journal预分配的replacement decision key；staging_generic_row_rerun必须恰为provider保存的StagingImportCapabilityRecovery request/response及intent预分配的StagingImportCapabilityRecoveryApplicationV1 key；staging_outcome_row_rerun只能恰为同一staging exclusion/capability/recovery journal预分配的StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1 key，绝不允许FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1。authorization evidence set按reason携带并摘要exact intent、journal、provider request/response/Head及预分配semantic-ID preimages；Fence signer必须独立展开并机械派生上述exact key set。unknown schema、另一activation/recovery/prefix/exclusion、fixed registry的其他key或wrong reason/key组合全部拒绝。

`fence_reason_authorization_evidence_set_digest=SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-fence-reason-authorization-evidence-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"fence_reason":...,"evidence_items":[...]}))`。每个item字段恰为 `evidence_role`、`evidence_schema`、`evidence_digest`，按三字段canonical UTF-8/bytes tuple升序；同role恰一、duplicate tuple、missing/extra role、wrong schema、wrong digest或unknown field全部拒绝。除下述local journal item外，每个digest都是对应exact canonical body digest；bundle携带完整body及所需signed envelope/provider Inspect，Fence signer按schema验证签名或从trusted DB读取immutable committed body，并重算body digest。provider request只能由同组signed provider response的request_digest及provider immutable Inspect共同认证，caller-only bytes不算evidence；current_provider_head必须是fresh signed ClaimV1ProviderHeadV1且逐字等于全部precondition Inspect的expected Head。

`semantic_id_journal` item不接受caller提供的record或signature。commit-attestor/Fence signer在任何candidate row/challenge之前，经B11 authenticated reservation call把exact write-once FenceDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveSemanticIDJournalRecordV1写入自身rollback-resistant journal；body字段恰为 `journal_record_id`、`activation_id`、`fence_reason`、`recovery_id_or_null`、`recovery_prefix_key_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_id_or_null`、`staging_recovery_id_or_null`、`staging_outcome_kind_or_null`、`semantic_key_count`、`semantic_keys`、`recorded_at`，keys按global key bytewise排序且item恰为 `row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`。schema固定为`database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-journal-record.v1`，body digest按§4.1共同公式即 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-journal-record.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(body))` 重算。journal内部唯一reservation key固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-reservation-key.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"activation_id":...,"fence_reason":...,"recovery_id_or_null":...,"recovery_prefix_key_digest_or_null":...,"staging_exclusion_id_or_null":...,"staging_recovery_id_or_null":...,"staging_outcome_kind_or_null":...,"semantic_key_count":...,"semantic_keys":bytewise-sorted-array-of-{row_schema,semantic_row_id}}))`；首次reservation原子绑定exact record body/digest，same authenticated reservation exact retry返回原body/digest，换journal_record_id、recorded_at、context或keys创建第二record固定conflict。epoch两种reason还必须在同一journal transaction先CAS共享subject slot；slot key固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.epoch-recovery-prefix-decision-subject-slot.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"activation_id":...,"recovery_id":...,"recovery_prefix_key_digest":...}))`，slot value恰为decision_id，并以decision_id为反向唯一key。slot不含fence_reason、decision kind、semantic keys、journal record ID/time或candidate；absent时可绑定一次，exact slot/value retry复用，same slot/different decision ID与same decision ID/different slot都在任何reason-specific record落盘前固定conflict。pair record的decision key及replacement record的唯一key都必须逐字等于slot value；pair record的application key还必须等于intent预分配recovery_application_id。signer必须用evidence item的digest在自身journal唯一索引record，zero/multiple match固定corrupt；bundle里的journal_record_id只能是untrusted lookup hint，不能替代digest index、reservation-key/subject-slot/decision反向唯一索引、append-only seal或rollback floor验证，caller即使提交相同bytes也不能建立信任。journal key set必须与request archive_keys exact相等，reason-specific nullable context必须与下表其余evidence逐字相等。同一record只可在这些context/keys逐字相同的后续PITR attempt中复用semantic-ID reservation；复用本身不授权推进，每次仍必须用当前generation、新鲜reason evidence、Head、Inspect与candidate tuple构造request，跨activation/recovery/prefix/exclusion/outcome replay固定拒绝。

reason/schema/phase对应的evidence role registry封闭如下；每个列出的role/schema cardinality都恰为1，未列出的role cardinality恰为0：

| fence reason / target / phase | exact evidence roles (`role:schema`) |
|---|---|
| epoch_recovery_pair_rerun / application+apply-decision / transcript_issued且无consumer | `semantic_id_journal:database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-journal-record.v1`; `epoch_recovery_intent:authority-epoch-transition-recovery-intent.v1`; `epoch_recovery_request:recover-authority-epoch-transition-request.v1`; `epoch_recovery_response:authority-epoch-transition-recovery.v1`; `current_provider_head:claim-v1-provider-head.v1` |
| epoch_recovery_replacement_decision_rerun / replacement decision / transcript_issued且next ordinal尚未消费 | 与上一行相同五个role/schema；journal只含same prefix的单一decision key |
| staging_generic_row_rerun / recovery application / held_recovery_abort_only | `semantic_id_journal:database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-journal-record.v1`; `staging_recovery_intent:staging-import-capability-recovery-intent.v1`; `staging_recovery_request:recover-staging-import-capability-request.v1`; `staging_recovery_response:staging-import-capability-recovery.v1`; `current_provider_head:claim-v1-provider-head.v1` |
| staging_outcome_row_rerun / revocation application / held normal Abort | `semantic_id_journal:database-commit-archive-fence-semantic-id-journal-record.v1`; `staging_capability:staging-import-capability.v1`; `staging_acquire_request:acquire-fresh-v7-staging-exclusion-request.v1`; `staging_exclusion_lease:fresh-v7-staging-exclusion-lease.v1`; `current_provider_head:claim-v1-provider-head.v1`; `staging_revocation:staging-import-capability-revocation.v1` |
| staging_outcome_row_rerun / revocation application / held_recovery_abort_only | normal Abort六个role/schema再加 `staging_recovery_intent:staging-import-capability-recovery-intent.v1`、`staging_recovery_request:recover-staging-import-capability-request.v1`、`staging_recovery_response:staging-import-capability-recovery.v1` |

Fence signer逐项验证所有evidence的activation/recovery/prefix/exclusion/capability/outcome kind与current Head state；journal的application/decision或outcome semantic IDs必须分别等于intent、signed revocation、provider request/response中预分配值。epoch pair与replacement record的decision ID还必须都等于same prefix trusted subject slot value，且反向唯一索引无另一slot；epoch pair必须是same-prefix apply decision且provider history无consumer；replacement必须是journaled same-prefix replacement decision与exact next ordinal；staging generic必须是stored recovery application ID；staging outcome target schema固定为staging-import-capability-revocation-application.v1，其normal/recovery submatrix由current Head与stored recovery request是否存在唯一导出，FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1在所有Fence reason下都拒绝。任一链关系不直连、phase错误、provider ledger已有terminal consumer或以caller body替代trusted journal/provider/DB evidence均固定拒绝。

precondition set digest按 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-fence-precondition-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(bytewise-sorted array of {row_schema,semantic_row_id,inspect_response_digest}))` 重算；每份fresh signed Inspect response必须显示同一expected Provider Head与同一live candidate tuple、archive_status=absent、candidate_row_count=0、对应generation及零body/entry。稳定once-key `fence_attempt_key_digest=SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.database-commit-archive-fence-attempt.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"semantic_id_journal_record_digest":...,"activation_id":...,"fence_reason":...,"recovery_id_or_null":...,"recovery_prefix_key_digest_or_null":...,"staging_exclusion_id_or_null":...,"staging_recovery_id_or_null":...,"staging_outcome_kind_or_null":...,"semantic_key_count":...,"semantic_keys":bytewise-sorted-array-of-{row_schema,semantic_row_id},"candidate_database_identity_digest":...,"candidate_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest":...,"candidate_database_incarnation_registration_digest_or_null":...,"candidate_runtime_instance_binding_digest":...,"candidate_runtime_instance_id":...,"candidate_runtime_instance_generation":...}))`。journal digest/context/key set必须逐字来自trusted journal；once-key中的六项稳定candidate holder字段必须逐字来自全部fresh signed precondition Inspect responses并与live mutually-authenticated candidate相等。Inspect响应仍须直接签入并逐字核验完整七项candidate tuple；其中candidate_runtime_instance_lease_digest只证明本次request/CAS freshness，刻意不进入once-key，因为同一binding/ID/generation的合法descendant lease仍是同一holder。once-key还刻意不含expected Provider Head、reason-authorization/precondition digest、当前archive generation、request ID/nonce/time；这些易变freshness字段仍由完整signed request、archive_keys与Inspect set逐字绑定并在CAS时重验，但不得让同一journal/context/key set与同一holder在成功后生成第二个once-key。只有严格更高runtime generation且具有相匹配新binding/ID的new holder才产生新key；单纯lease rollover永不产生新key。

signer_role=`database_incarnation` 的 FenceDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveResponseV1 body字段恰为 `request_digest`、`fence_request_id`、`activation_id`、`fence_reason`、`fence_reason_authorization_evidence_set_digest`、`fence_attempt_key_digest`、`archive_key_count`、`result_archive_keys`、`precondition_inspect_response_set_digest`、`fenced_at`；result items按相同key排序，每项字段恰为 `row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`previous_archive_fence_generation`、`result_archive_fence_generation`，且result=previous+1。archive先只读命中same request exact-retry时返回byte-identical response；否则mutation锁序固定为sorted global latches→stable fence_attempt_key_digest once-row→response exact-retry key。它在一个事务中重新展开reason authorization evidence、按封闭registry重新派生key set并检查所有generation、global latch absent、primary stream/related owner均不存在及once-row absent，再原子推进全部generation、写入不可删除的successful once-row并journal response；同ID/body exact retry不再次推进，once-row已存在而request不同则即使Head/evidence/generation已变化也零副作用conflict。generation、once-row、response任一步失败整体回滚，只能全无或完整可见。旧attestation append先赢则fence零副作用失败且只能exact恢复locked body；fence先赢则任何旧generation append永久stale，新transaction才可用journaled same semantic IDs创建current body。partial key advancement、orphan once-row/response、same candidate第二次推进、fence后仍采用旧generation、未先fence即以same ID创建different body或把fence当commit evidence固定拒绝。fence不锁body、不生成entry、不改变fixed 10项registry或26张DB控制面表。

InspectDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveRequestV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_id`、`row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`expected_locked_row_body_digest_or_null`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`expected_parent_postgres_system_id`、`expected_parent_timeline`、`expected_parent_database_identity_digest`、`expected_parent_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`expected_parent_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`expected_parent_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`candidate_database_identity_digest`、`candidate_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`candidate_database_incarnation_registration_digest_or_null`、`candidate_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`candidate_runtime_instance_id`、`candidate_runtime_instance_generation`、`candidate_runtime_instance_lease_digest`、`caller_nonce`。请求bundle携带digest逐字等于expected_provider_head_digest的fresh signed Provider Head、受信lineage、candidate holder lease以及目标为epoch recovery application时的exact provider consumption ledger；若需要计算canonical_unconsumed_candidate_lineage，还必须携带从candidate entry所含PostRecovery bridge到本次trusted parent/candidate的exact PostRecovery attestation、lineage与全部intermediate holder永久termination envelopes，缺一即该selector为null。attestor从这些envelope机械推导current trusted lineage parent，并与live candidate相互认证。expected-provider/head-parent字段只做equality check，不能让caller选择历史Head或parent；candidate字段只指定本次必须连接并观察的exact live candidate。

signer_role=`database_incarnation` 的 InspectDatabaseCommitAttestationArchiveResponseV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_digest`、`archive_status`、`row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`archive_fence_generation`、`semantic_body_lock_role_or_null`、`locked_row_body_digest_or_null`、`locked_row_body_or_null`、`locked_related_body_count`、`locked_related_bodies`、`archive_entry_count`、`archive_entries`、`matched_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`canonical_current_parent_entry_digest_or_null`、`canonical_unconsumed_candidate_lineage_entry_digest_or_null`、`canonical_recovery_continuity_entry_digest_or_null`、`recovery_continuity_state_or_null`、`provider_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`provider_consumption_history_count`、`provider_consumption_history`、`candidate_database_identity_digest`、`candidate_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`candidate_database_incarnation_registration_digest_or_null`、`candidate_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`candidate_runtime_instance_id`、`candidate_runtime_instance_generation`、`candidate_runtime_instance_lease_digest`、`candidate_row_count`、`candidate_row_body_digest_or_null`、`candidate_database_point_or_null`、`archive_head_sequence_or_zero`、`archive_head_entry_digest_or_null`、`inspected_at`、`expires_at`；archive_status只有absent或locked，expires_at-inspected_at不超过2分钟。expected_provider_head_digest与七个candidate字段必须逐字回显request并直接进入signed response body，不能只由inspect_request_digest间接承诺；除registration按scope允许nullable外，candidate tuple不因candidate_row_count=0而变null。任何consumer必须在自身CAS前比较该Head digest与current/expected Provider Head，另一Head上的fresh response也不得复用。

locked_related_bodies按 `(row_schema,semantic_row_id)` bytewise排序，每项字段恰为 `row_schema`、`semantic_row_id`、`row_digest`、`canonical_body`；普通primary count=0且数组空，epoch recovery application primary count=1且必须通过owner edge连接同一global decision latch。每个decision global latch另存exact-zero-or-one `related_owner_primary_key_or_null`；首次application related attach在同一sorted two-key transaction中执行null→exact application global key CAS，同一application exact retry允许，different application永久conflict且不得留下partial latch/edge/entry。absent时role/body/matched/canonical/head字段全null、locked-related/archive-entry/history count与head sequence为0、三个数组为空，但archive_fence_generation仍为该key当前positive值。related-only时archive_status=locked、role=related_only、body present、entries/head sequence=0、matched/canonical均null且自身locked_related_body_count=0；必须恰有一条完整已提交的反向owner edge，且反查该application primary的exact related set包含本decision；0条、>1条、owner不同或反查不一致均为archive corruption。primary时role=primary、body present且entries按sequence从1连续到head，每项字段恰为 `archive_sequence`、`previous_archive_entry_digest_or_null`、`commit_challenge_schema`、`commit_challenge_digest`、`commit_challenge_envelope`、`commit_attestation_schema`、`commit_attestation_digest`、`commit_attestation_envelope`，digest/envelope交叉验证且previous链直连。related-only零entry绝不独立充当commit、fork-cut或consumer proof；application Inspect必须重算edge、related set与global body，缺owner/latch/body或different均视为archive corruption。

attestor在同一response中通过live candidate trusted-read semantic key：只签candidate_row_count=0或1；0时恰只有candidate_row_body_digest_or_null与candidate_database_point_or_null为null，1时二者都non-null且body digest与唯一row canonical body重算相等。different locked body、多行、无法锁定一致snapshot、response/request/live connection/holder lease的candidate tuple任一不等或检查期间generation变化一律不签。commit_challenge non-null时matched字段等于该exact retry entry或null；同inspect ID/body exact retry返回byte-identical response。

canonical current-parent entry没有caller选择：attestor在locked body相等、签名/role/policy仍有效的entries中，只保留attested `(postgres_system_id,timeline,current_database_identity_digest,database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest,current_database_incarnation_registration_digest,runtime_instance_binding_digest)` 逐字等于fresh trusted parent tuple者，再唯一选择最大archive_sequence；没有匹配则canonical=null。旧parent entries永久保留但不能进入missing-row evidence branch。archive head始终是全链最后entry，可能不同于canonical parent；二者都进入§7.2 evidence-set以同时证明机械选择与无截断。canonical=null对一般consumer以及candidate_row_count=0的staging分支仍保持held；唯一例外是§7.2由同一Inspect证明candidate_row_count=1且digest等于locked body的exact_existing_candidate模式。

只有epoch recovery application primary可产生recovery continuity字段。provider_consumption_history按ordinal从1连续，每项字段恰为 `ordinal`、`event_kind`、`request_schema`、`request_digest`、`application_commit_attestation_digest`、`previous_history_digest_or_null`、`result_history_digest`、`provider_response_schema`、`provider_response_digest`、`database_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`pre_provider_head_digest`、`post_provider_head_digest`。event_kind只有pre_catchup_applied_rebind、terminal_application_catchup或post_catchup_marker_cleared_rebind；result digest按 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.epoch-recovery-application-consumption-event.v1") || 0x00 || raw previous digest-or-32-zero-bytes || 0x00 || ASCII(canonical ordinal) || 0x00 || ASCII(event kind) || 0x00 || raw request digest || 0x00 || raw application attestation digest)` 计算，避免response固定点。terminal_application_catchup的database result固定null；每个已完成pre/post-catchup rebind event的database result必须non-null并携带exact preimage，唯有history tail可在provider response已成功而DB result尚未reconcile时暂为null，此时state必须是rebind_result_reconcile_required且禁止追加任何event。PITR后non-null result body缺失时，Inspect attestor必须按§4.6 first-consumer矩阵携带并验证exact next-winning PostRecovery或Head witness；没有later consumer的tail只接受dedicated reconcile，不能让provider history digest或caller body替代fork-cut witness。count=0时provider_consumption_history_digest_or_null=null；count>0时它逐字等于最后item.result_history_digest，且每项previous逐字等于前一item.result。Inspect response签署完整array及每个response、每个non-null result、其required witness与全部Head preimage，所以tail digest只承担无环的event-order commitment而不把opaque summary提升为完整evidence。全history至多64个rebind event且恰有零或一个catchup event。

history为空时recovery_continuity_state=unconsumed_pre_catchup、history digest=null且canonical_recovery_continuity entry逐字等于canonical current-parent。此时另行机械选择canonical_unconsumed_candidate_lineage entry：只保留body/set exact、purpose=external_admission、provider ledger未消费、其challenge内含有效PostRecovery bridge或attested tuple本就等于trusted parent、commit timeline位于从fresh provider parent到本次candidate的受信lineage、所有intermediate holder均有永久termination的entries，再唯一取最大archive_sequence；没有则null。它只允许下一份PostRecovery basis跨越“fresh proof已归档但candidate在provider CAS前死亡”的缝，不能进入staging missing-row、fork-cut或provider consumer proof。

history非空且无catchup时state=consumed_pre_catchup，有catchup且其后只有marker-cleared rebind时state=consumed_post_catchup_marker_cleared。若tail rebind response已成功但对应DB result缺失，state只能是rebind_result_reconcile_required。history非空时continuity entry只能是tail event逐字引用的application commit-attestation entry；attestor必须从provider immutable ledger、每份rebind/catchup request/response、DB result与pre/post Head逐项重算完整直连history，并验证tail proof的candidate tuple正是current provider holder或产生它的winning rebind candidate，绝不能选择first consumer、任意ancestor、最大sequence或caller给定entry。candidate_row_count=0的continuity response只允许§4.6 historical restricted restore；任何rebind/catchup前都必须恢复exact locked row并取得count=1、matched=fresh candidate attestation的另一份Inspect。partial archive、related body缺失、sequence/previous gap、same challenge两个attestation、same semantic key两个locked body、history gap/extra/duplicate、pending result却继续mutation或依赖不可用固定fail closed。

CompleteClaimV1GenesisRequestV1 body字段恰为 `completion_id`、`activation_id`、`preparation_digest`、`activation_digest`、`database_activation_attestation_digest`、`database_legacy_absence_projection_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1GenesisCompletionV1 response使用相同字段并额外恰有 `provider_history_high_water`、`phase=genesis_completed_pending_release`、`completed_at`。

PrepareClaimV1GenesisReleaseRequestV1 body字段恰为 `release_preparation_id`、`open_id`、`activation_id`、`activation_digest`、`completion_digest`、`database_completion_attestation_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`。同一signer role的 ClaimV1GenesisReleasePreparationV1 response使用相同字段并额外恰有 `open_nonce`、`provider_history_high_water`、`phase=genesis_release_prepared`、`prepared_at`。

OpenClaimV1GenesisRequestV1 body字段恰为 `open_id`、`activation_id`、`mode`、`preparation_digest`、`activation_digest`、`completion_digest`、`provider_release_preparation_digest`、`database_release_digest`、`database_release_attestation_digest`、`open_nonce`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1GenesisOpenV1 body字段恰为同一组字段加 `open_request_digest`、`result_phase`、`initial_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`provider_history_high_water`、`opened_at`；result_phase按mode只能是active或fresh_v7_staging_closed。Open前任一普通Reserve/Finalize/Abort/epoch transition固定provider_transition_incomplete。

InspectClaimV1GenesisRequestV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_id`、`activation_id`、`transition_kind`、`transition_id`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`caller_nonce`；transition_kind只有credential_policy、runtime_registration、preparation、completion、release_preparation、open、runtime_rebind、serving_lease_renewal、serving_lease_revocation、epoch_transition_preparation、epoch_transition_resolution、epoch_transition_cancellation、epoch_transition_recovery、staging_exclusion、staging_capability_recovery、staging_exclusion_release、staging_exclusion_abort。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 InspectClaimV1GenesisResponseV1 body字段恰为 `inspect_request_digest`、`transition_kind`、`transition_id`、`stored_schema`、`stored_body_digest`、`stored_request_body`、`stored_evidence_bundle`、`stored_envelope`、`current_provider_head_digest`、`inspected_at`；三个stored object必须逐字等于provider为对应mutation永久保存的exact request、bundle、response且交叉digest相等，不能只返回opaque digest。Inspect只读，caller nonce防response replay；epoch_transition_recovery的完整历史DB transcript仍以§4.6专用provider response为准。

transition_kind=epoch_transition_recovery时，Inspect.`transition_id`必须使用RecoverAuthorityEpochTransitionRequestV1.`recovery_transcript_id`而不是semantic recovery ID；因此同一recovery在holder再次失效后产生的更长suffix replacement transcript可分别exact Inspect，且current Head/tail决定哪一份仍可被DB application采用。

本设计不定义第二层archive wrapper摘要：每个 `archive_entry_digest` 恰等于该entry的 `commit_attestation_digest`，`previous_archive_entry_digest_or_null`与`archive_head_entry_digest_or_null`都只引用这个值。commit-challenge fixed registry把generic row_kind映射到唯一row_schema与semantic ID字段，把staging outcome_kind映射到FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1或StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1且semantic_row_id恰等于outcome_id；unknown mapping固定拒绝。该challenge映射不授予Fence权限：`staging_outcome_row_rerun`的封闭reason registry仍只允许StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1，FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1绝不允许以任一Fence reason重跑。

### 6.2 ClaimV1ProviderHeadV1 与普通 transcript

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的稳定 ClaimV1ProviderHeadV1 body字段恰为 `provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`protocol_profile=claim_v1`、`genesis_credential_policy_digest`、`current_credential_policy_digest`、`credential_policy_chain_digest`、`activation_id`、`mode_or_null`、`preparation_digest_or_null`、`activation_digest_or_null`、`provider_completion_digest_or_null`、`database_completion_digest_or_null`、`provider_release_preparation_digest_or_null`、`database_release_digest_or_null`、`open_digest_or_null`、`phase`、`genesis_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`latest_database_timeline_lineage_attestation_digest_or_null`、`genesis_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`incarnation_id`、`incarnation_public_key_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`serving_lease_id_or_null`、`serving_lease_generation_or_null`、`serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`database_release_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_evidence_digest_or_null`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest_or_null`、`selected_genesis_epoch_or_null`、`current_epoch_or_null`、`epoch_transition_chain_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`pending_epoch_transition_digest_or_null`、`latest_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_state_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_request_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_id_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_recovery_request_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_state_or_null`、`latest_reserved_sequence`、`latest_committed_sequence`、`latest_reservation_digest_or_null`、`latest_committed_operation_id_or_null`、`latest_committed_receipt_digest_or_null`、`latest_committed_database_point_or_null`、`provider_control_sequence`、`provider_history_high_water`。open_digest是immutable ClaimV1GenesisOpenV1 body digest。初始genesis/current policy相等且chain digest等于该policy body digest；genesis/current identity相等、lineage为§4.2 root且latest attestation null。每个后续policy的previous digest必须等于current，new body digest成为chain head，genesis保持不变；每个timeline-changing recovery rebind按§4.2推进lineage一次，其他rebind保持。Open时terminal chain等于§4.6 genesis terminal root；每次Resolve或cancellation-rebind只按§4.6公式推进一次。epoch recovery state只有required或transcript_issued，并与request digest分别满足null或present；它不推进epoch/terminal chain。Head不含observed_at、challenge nonce、lease expiry或尚未存在的provider response digest；held exclusion只引用预先存在的acquire/recovery request digest，避免Head/response fixed point。同authority state产生byte-identical body。

初始runtime_rebind_chain_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.runtime-rebind-root.v1") || 0x00 || raw genesis-registration-digest)` 且genesis/current registration相等。每个ClaimV1RuntimeInstanceRebindV1 body额外绑定 `previous_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`；rebind后Head current registration更新且chain digest等于该response body digest，genesis registration保持不变。readiness从activation中的genesis registration逐份验证provider-signed rebind chain、operator authorization与DB continuity；不能把current registration直接与immutable activation row混为同一字段。

phase枚举恰为registered_pending_genesis、genesis_prepared、genesis_completed_pending_release、genesis_release_prepared、active、active_lease_revoked、fresh_v7_staging_closed；present/null matrix固定为：

| phase | mode/preparation/epoch | DB activation | provider completion | DB completion + provider release preparation | DB release + attestation + Open | serving lease |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| registered_pending_genesis | all null | null | null | null | null | null |
| genesis_prepared | all present | null | null | null | null | null |
| genesis_completed_pending_release | all present | present | present | null | null | null |
| genesis_release_prepared | all present | present | present | present | null | null |
| active | all present | present | present | present | present | present |
| active_lease_revoked | all present | present | present | present | present | null |
| fresh_v7_staging_closed | all present | present | present | present | present | null |

registered与3个genesis closed phase以及fresh_v7_staging_closed的ordinary authority head字段固定为reserved/committed sequence=`0`且所有latest digest/operation/database-point为null。active/active_lease_revoked使用上位Head规则：committed不大于reserved；latest reservation digest当且仅当reserved>0存在；三个latest committed anchor全null或全present并来自同一exact receipt。provider pending epoch digest只允许null，或present且current epoch/effective chain/terminal chain/latest resolution仍为previous；pending期间所有ordinary/policy/第二transition关闭，只有Resolve或§6.3 cancellation-rebind可改变它。epoch recovery state非null时pending epoch与staging tuple必须全null，ordinary/policy/下一epoch关闭，serving lease即使present也只能rollover。staging exclusion四字段只允许：(id,acquire,recovery,state)全null；仅fresh_v7_staging_closed的(present,present,null,held)、(present,present,null,held_recovery_required)；或(present,present,present,held_recovery_abort_only)。时间经过不清空它们；held在capability identity/runtime仍与current逐字相等时允许exact既有import；无论是否rebind，已提交且由current attestor证明其old runtime/identity为exact ancestor的import/revocation outcome都允许fresh commit proof及对应Release/normal Abort。除此之外held只可进入intent-backed recovery。recovery_required只允许proof/Inspect/recovery-intent commit challenge/Recover及保持状态的rebind；abort-only只可recovery revoke/commit proof/Abort及保持状态的rebind。三种state都禁止第二acquire、新capability admission与ordinary/policy/epoch/serving；required/abort-only另行永久禁止import与Release。
