signer_role=`database_runtime_attestor` 的 DatabaseRuntimeInstanceLeaseDescendantChainV1 body字段恰为 `runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`ancestor_runtime_instance_lease_digest`、`ancestor_lease_sequence`、`current_runtime_instance_lease_digest`、`current_lease_sequence`、`descendant_count`、`descendant_lease_digests`。数组按lease sequence升序、排除ancestor并包含current，count为1..64；第一项previous digest等于ancestor，后续逐项直连且所有lease的binding/runtime/generation相同，current sequence必须checked等于ancestor sequence+count。bundle携带ancestor、全部descendant与chain envelope；超过64代、gap或current过期必须终止旧binding并走operator rebind。

AcquireDatabaseIncarnationProofRequestV1 body字段恰为 `proof_id`、`caller_challenge_nonce`、`purpose`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`activation_id`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`expected_current_database_identity_digest`、`expected_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`expected_attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`attestor_runtime_lease_descendant_chain_digest_or_null`、`requested_at`；proof ID与32-byte challenge由verifier生成并在本次检查内single-use，purpose只有readiness_pre、readiness_post、ordinary_mutation、genesis_transition、epoch_transition、epoch_transition_recovery、serving_lease_rollover、staging_import。epoch_transition要求current serving lease的identity/lineage、policy/effective epoch/terminal chain逐字等于stable Head且所有DB/provider pending/recovery字段null；context已推进但lease尚未rollover时不能开始第二次epoch transition。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 DatabaseIncarnationProofV1 body字段恰为 `proof_id`、`request_digest`、`caller_challenge_nonce`、`purpose`、`provider_challenge_nonce`、`provider_head_digest`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`incarnation_id`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`attestor_runtime_lease_sequence`、`attestor_runtime_lease_expires_at`、`attestor_runtime_lease_descendant_chain_digest_or_null`、`serving_lease_id_or_null`、`serving_lease_generation_or_null`、`serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`serving_lease_expires_at_or_null`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`。proof最长30秒且永远不得晚于current attestor lease；相同proof ID/request exact retry返回同一response，ID或caller nonce重用于另一body固定conflict。

proof与lease evidence matrix是封闭的：ordinary_mutation及active readiness要求current、未过期serving lease present，attestor digest与serving绑定值相等、chain=null，provider epoch recovery fields与staging tuple全null。lease的activation/selected genesis epoch/genesis transition root、current identity/lineage、effective epoch/chain、terminal chain与latest resolution必须与stable Provider/DB Head逐字相等；lease的policy digest/chain只与provider-owned stable Head逐字相等。lease.latest terminal application必须与DB Head逐字相等，而Provider Head通过其exact serving lease digest间接承诺该值、不复制第二个字段。proof expiry还不得晚于serving expiry。registered/genesis closed、active_lease_revoked non-ready与fresh staging的serving四字段全null，chain=null，lease evidence恰含current attestor envelope且provider serving envelope必须absent；serving_lease_rollover要求Head仍引用exact serving lease，允许它已过期、被recovery标记为restricted或因连续context descendant而ordinary-invalid，proof expiry只受current attestor lease/30秒约束。rollover direct equality时chain=null；descendant时必须携带上述1..64完整chain。epoch_transition_recovery允许provider Head recovery state为null、required或transcript_issued：null只允许intent记录的exact terminal Head尚无recovery-only rebind、suffix count=0且terminal mutation以来ordinary/policy/epoch/staging history全零；required对应已追加deferred suffix；transcript_issued只用于同tail Inspect/同transaction apply decision+DB application，holder在application前永久失效时还必须验证唯一replacement decision+commit proof，application后永久失效则改用epoch_transition_recovery_applied与post-recovery attestation，不能混用。proof必须返回fresh current Provider Head与current attestor tuple，serving lease可为已过期或restricted但必须是Head引用的exact lease，其activation/genesis/effective/terminal context仍与provider terminal ledger一致；DB anchor与provider current runtime不同只由exact recovery intent、DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1、deferred suffix与可选replacement decision证明，post-application runtime改变只由DatabaseAuthorityPostRecoveryRebindAttestationV1与immediate result证明，不能借proof本身伪造相等。lease evidence子集在普通active恰为current attestor+provider serving，在null branch恰为current attestor，在rollover/recovery descendant恰为provider serving+ancestor+全部descendant+chain envelope；missing、extra、gap、identity/lineage/context/decision/application mismatch或null/present错配固定拒绝。readiness pre/post各生成新的proof ID/caller nonce并检查response request digest；缓存proof不能匹配本次challenge，比较的是stable Head与同一current lease ID/generation/digest而非两个proof envelope。

ordinary mutation admission使用 AuthorityProtocolContextV1；其字段恰为 `protocol_profile=claim_v1`、`activation_id`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`credential_policy_digest`、`credential_policy_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`serving_lease_id`、`serving_lease_generation`、`serving_lease_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`current_epoch`、`epoch_transition_chain_digest`、`epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest`、`latest_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`latest_epoch_transition_terminal_application_digest_or_null`、`preparation_digest`、`activation_digest`、`provider_completion_digest`、`database_completion_digest`、`provider_release_preparation_digest`、`database_release_digest`、`open_digest`。DB immutable runtime/result/resolution/terminal-application preimages必须进入bundle，provider逐字段匹配Head与current serving lease；任一unresolved rebind/epoch recovery/staging transition或identity/lineage mismatch拒绝。下列request/reservation字段集包含该context的全部字段并禁止额外字段：

- ClaimV1ReserveRequestV1 额外恰有 `operation_id`、`effect_kind`、`scope_kind`、`scope_digest`。
- ClaimV1ReservationV1 额外恰有 Reserve request 的四个字段与 `authority_sequence`；provider envelope body_digest就是reservation digest，不在body内自引用。
- ClaimV1FinalizeRequestV1 额外恰有 `operation_id`、`reservation_digest`、`effect_digest`、`postgres_system_id`、`timeline`、`database_oid`、`database_name`、`incarnation_id`、`required_lsn`。
- ClaimV1AbortRequestV1 额外恰有 `operation_id`、`reservation_digest`、`abort_reason`。
- ClaimV1ReceiptV1 不继承caller当前context；其body字段恰为 `reservation_envelope`、`reservation_digest`、`terminalization_protocol_context`、`bound_effect_digest_or_null`、`bound_database_point_or_null`、`terminal_status`、`abort_reason_or_null`。reservation envelope是完整ClaimV1ReservationV1；terminalization context是完整AuthorityProtocolContextV1。除attestor/provider lease与current runtime-rebind字段可以是经验证的current descendant外，activation/credential/genesis/current epoch chain字段必须等于reservation；provider envelope body_digest就是receipt digest。
- ClaimV1RecordV1 是稳定历史object且不继承caller当前context；其body字段恰为 `reservation_envelope`、`reservation_digest`、`bound_effect_digest_or_null`、`bound_database_point_or_null`、`terminal_receipt_or_null`。provider重算reservation digest；terminal receipt必须是完整ClaimV1ReceiptV1 envelope且引用同一reservation digest。lease renewal本身不能改变Record/Receipt digest。

`bound_database_point_or_null` 非null时字段恰为 `postgres_system_id`、`timeline`、`database_oid`、`database_name`、`incarnation_id`、`required_lsn`。effect_kind、scope_kind、terminal_status与abort_reason继续使用上位设计的封闭枚举和组合规则；所有整数编码为canonical nonempty decimal string，LSN使用uppercase canonical `X/Y`。Reserve/Finalize/Abort/Inspect 的 provider response signer_role固定为 `claim_v1_provider`。未知字段、legacy V1 schema、context mismatch或body digest由caller/provider选择均固定拒绝。

每个普通provider mutation与production CheckReady都必须取得fresh DatabaseIncarnationProofV1并匹配stable Head/current lease generation。fresh staging evidence使用同一proof schema但要求provider serving lease字段全null。旧endpoint/profile/credential、过期attestor/provider lease、另一runtime binding/incarnation、database clone或rollback challenge均不能获得可接受reservation、receipt或production-ready Head。

### 6.3 serving lease lifecycle

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1ServingLeaseV1 body字段恰为 `renewal_id`、`issuance_request_schema`、`issuance_request_digest`、`activation_id`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`lease_id`、`lease_generation`、`previous_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`credential_policy_digest`、`credential_policy_chain_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`current_epoch`、`epoch_transition_chain_digest`、`epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest`、`latest_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`latest_epoch_transition_terminal_application_digest_or_null`、`previous_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`result_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`latest_reserved_sequence`、`latest_committed_sequence`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`；issuance request按来源只能是open-claim-v1-genesis-request.v1、rebind-claim-v1-runtime-instance-request.v1或renew-claim-v1-serving-lease-request.v1，digest必须等于provider已保存的exact request body。有效期最长5分钟且expires_at不得晚于它绑定的attestor runtime lease。Open的初始lease generation=1、previous=null，renewal_id确定性等于 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.claim-v1-serving-lease-open-id.v1") || 0x00 || raw open UUID)`。Open、rebind与renew生成lease时，activation、selected genesis epoch与genesis transition root必须从同一stable DB/Provider Head逐字复制并等于immutable AuthorityGenesisEpochEvidenceV1；任何一路mutation或cross-activation copy固定拒绝。lease绑定current identity/lineage、registration与完整policy/effective+terminal epoch context；两个ordinary sequence字段是签发时的连续prefix baseline，不伪装为之后current Head的完整六字段projection，对应runtime rebind chain由stable Head与每次admission context绑定。两个history字段只有epoch recovery applied rebind或epoch recovery terminal catchup来源才可non-null，previous逐字等于request expected tail，result按§6.1 event公式由request digest与fresh application attestation计算；其他来源两者均null。lease不引用包含它的Open/rebind/renew response digest，history event也不引用lease/response digest，因而不存在response↔lease或history摘要固定点。

RenewClaimV1ServingLeaseRequestV1 body字段恰为 `renewal_id`、`renewal_kind`、`activation_id`、`current_serving_lease_digest`、`new_attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`attestor_runtime_lease_descendant_chain_digest_or_null`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`database_authority_head_digest`、`database_authority_head_attestation_digest`、`expected_current_database_identity_digest`、`expected_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`expected_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`ordinary_closed_prefix_digest`、`expected_current_credential_policy_digest`、`expected_current_credential_policy_chain_digest`、`expected_selected_genesis_epoch`、`expected_genesis_epoch_transition_root_digest`、`expected_current_epoch`、`expected_current_epoch_transition_chain_digest`、`expected_current_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest`、`latest_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`latest_epoch_transition_terminal_application_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_id_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`provider_epoch_transition_recovery_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_archive_inspect_response_digest_or_null`、`expected_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`；renewal_kind只有same_context、context_descendant或terminal_application_catchup。renewal_id确定性等于 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.claim-v1-serving-lease-renewal-id.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(request-body-with-renewal_id-omitted))`；fresh proof/Head/attestation、attestor lease、ordinary prefix、identity/lineage、authority context或recovery proof/history任一改变都会得到新ID且无self-reference。attestor在provider调用前rollback-resistant记录exact request body digest。same_context、context_descendant与cancellation terminal catchup要求新增九项全部null；epoch recovery terminal catchup要求前八项全部present，expected history当且仅当history为空时为null、否则present。challenge/attestation必须是本次current holder上的fresh external_admission或archive_continuity proof，signed Inspect的matched entry、candidate tuple、locked application+decision closure与history tail必须逐字相等。

ordinary_closed_prefix_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.serving-lease-ordinary-closed-prefix.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"lease_latest_reserved_sequence":...,"lease_latest_committed_sequence":...,"lease_latest_reservation_body_digest_or_null":...,"lease_latest_committed_record_body_digest_or_null":...,"current_latest_reserved_sequence":...,"current_latest_committed_sequence":...,"terminal_records":[exact post-lease ClaimV1RecordV1 body digests in authority-sequence order],"pending_reservations":[exact post-lease ClaimV1ReservationV1 body digests in authority-sequence order]}))`。两个baseline body digest分别当且仅当lease reserved/committed sequence为0时null；非null时bundle携带该sequence的exact Reservation/Record envelope并以lease exact issuance request/Provider Head历史证明它就是签发prefix tail。CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1还必须携带两个数组所列每份exact Record/Reservation envelope，而不只携带digest；verifier从baseline与post-lease body重算current latest reservation digest、operation ID、receipt digest和database point四个非lease anchors。两个数组合并后必须无gap、exact覆盖lease两个sequence anchors之后直到current Head的全部ordinary history；same_context可有pending reservations但bundle必须携带其exact envelopes并服从terminalization lease-descendant规则。context_descendant要求pending array空、current reserved=committed、DB/Provider Head完整六字段ordinary projection逐字相等，且全部terminal records在第一条policy/epoch context mutation之前完成，之后零ordinary mutation。terminal_application_catchup要求两个post-lease数组都空、lease的latest reserved/committed两个sequence anchors与两种current Head逐字不变，并由exact baseline preimages证明两种Head其余四个anchors也未改变。这样lease签发后已合法完成的ordinary prefix不会阻断rollover，同时context或recovery变化后的旧lease仍不能授权新操作。

same_context要求request中的activation、selected genesis epoch/root、current identity/lineage与effective epoch/chain/terminal chain/latest resolution逐字等于current serving lease及两种Head；request policy digest/chain只与serving lease和provider-owned Head逐字相等；request.latest terminal application逐字等于serving lease与DB Head，Provider Head仍只通过exact serving lease digest间接承诺它。lease只直接携带签发时latest reserved/committed两个sequence anchors，它们是prefix baseline而不要求等于已经合法前进的current Head；DB/Provider Head当前完整六字段ordinary projection必须逐字相等，上段exact Record/Reservation evidence必须从lease baseline无gap重放并重算current两项sequence与另外四项anchors。DB与provider pending epoch/recovery字段、staging/rebind都必须null，pending reservation只有在上位terminalization lease-descendant规则仍能封闭时允许。context_descendant允许current Head的policy和/或epoch成为lease context的连续兼容后继，但activation、selected genesis epoch/root、identity/lineage不变，Head仍精确引用旧serving lease、current registration/rebind/runtime不变、上段closed ordinary prefix完整且零pending reservation/epoch recovery/staging/rebind。CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1在本contract禁用“provider已持有即可省略”：same_context与context_descendant都exact携带ordinary prefix的terminal record/可选pending reservation envelopes、fresh proof、attestor lease/可选descendant chain与内嵌DB Head的fresh DatabaseAuthorityHeadAttestationV1；same_context不得携带intervening policy/epoch evidence，context_descendant还必须exact携带从lease policy到current policy的每份ClaimV1CredentialPolicyV1，以及从lease epoch/terminal chain到current的每份intent、provider transition、DB application/resolution、commit challenge/attestation、provider resolution receipt、DB terminal application与可选cancellation evidence。DB Head只证明DB-controlled runtime/ordinary/effective+terminal epoch tuple；policy continuity只由provider signed ledger证明。每条previous link与两种Head须直连；missing、extra、opaque summary、取消transition混入effective chain或任一不连续固定拒绝。policy或epoch一经推进，旧lease立即只可用于purpose=serving_lease_rollover，不能用于ordinary proof/readiness/writer；provider以这条closed prefix原子签发绑定current context的generation `g+1` ClaimV1ServingLeaseV1。

terminal_application_catchup是第三个封闭matrix，不是context_descendant的宽松特例。current lease provenance只能是四个exact分支之一：(a) scope=epoch_transition_cancellation的赢家rebind request/response所签发的restricted lease；(b) scope=epoch_transition_recovery的赢家rebind request/response所签发的restricted lease；(c) same-holder epoch recovery开始时由RecoverAuthorityEpochTransitionRequestV1.`expected_provider_head_digest`间接引用、且从该recovery request起一直未被替换的exact lease；或(d) apply decision/application提交后scope=epoch_transition_recovery_applied的赢家rebind所签发、且对应immediate AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1已经exact提交的restricted lease。分支(c)的issuance request可以是合法Open/rebind/renew schema，但provider terminal ledger、terminal mutation以来完整的零ordinary lease chain、recovery request/response与零later ordinary history必须证明它已ordinary-invalid；不能把任意旧lease升级为catchup资格。分支(d)可有按§4.4逐次完成result reconcile的连续applied-scope rebind，但latest lease、两种Head与last immediate result必须直连且中间零ordinary/policy/epoch/staging mutation。

lease与两种Head的activation、selected genesis epoch/root、identity/lineage、current registration/runtime、effective epoch/chain、latest effective resolution及terminal chain必须逐字相等；lease的latest reserved/committed两个sequence anchors必须等于两种Head，DB/Provider Head完整六字段ordinary projection必须逐字相等，另外四个非lease anchors由exact closed-prefix Record/Reservation evidence重算。policy只在lease与provider-owned Head之间逐字相等。Provider/DB Head的runtime rebind chain必须相等，lease则由其exact issuance request/response provenance绑定该chain；Provider Head仍引用该lease且不复制terminal-application字段。cancellation分支要求lease.latest terminal application恰等于current DB terminal application的previous digest，且lease terminal chain已等于rebind resulting chain、三row同transaction exact。epoch recovery分支要求§4.6 recovery intent/commit proof/provider exact transcript、same-transaction apply decision/recovery application、atomic two-key archive closure、所有older replacement decision proofs及恢复后的terminal group完整；request显式绑定本次current holder上的fresh application challenge/attestation与matched Inspect。applied-scope分支还要求每个provider rebind都有direct DB result，完整history逐项直连且不存在result-pending latch。history为空时direct catchup CAS与首个applied rebind竞争并把本次fresh proof记为first consumer；history非空时expected digest必须等于provider机械重算的tail，本次fresh proof purpose=archive_continuity且first consumer不变。lease.latest terminal application只能等于current DB terminal application的previous digest，或在provider transcript证明该exact terminal application早已被该lease/后继rollover消费时等于current digest。request与DB Head都绑定current terminal application。cancellation时provider epoch recovery fields与新增proof/history字段必须null；epoch recovery时marker必须为transcript_issued并在成功mutation清空。staging与所有pending字段null，ordinary arrays空，policy/effective/terminal chain都不再推进。

catchup bundle必须exact携带cancellation rebind request/authorization/rebinding-registration/response、AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1、AuthorityEpochTransitionCancellationV1、AuthorityEpochTransitionTerminalApplicationV1，或epoch recovery intent/commit proof/response、apply decision/recovery application、fresh application commit challenge/attestation、generation evidence、matched Inspect、archive locked primary+related bodies与完整entry chain、全部replacement decision+proof、完整consumption history与exact restored terminal group；分支(b)还携带deferred recovery rebind全套，分支(c)携带recovery-start Head、current lease exact preimage及terminal mutation以来的完整零ordinary lease chain，分支(d)携带每份post-recovery attestation、applied-scope authorization/request/registration/response/post-Head与immediate DB result，不得携带另一个policy/effective successor。previous terminal application、terminal chain、runtime/identity/lineage任一不直连、partial decision/application/application-proof或two/three-row group、related decision不同transaction、resolved evidence冒充cancelled、无上述provenance的normal rebind lease、nonempty ordinary prefix或并发第二transition固定拒绝。provider原子消费或重验first consumer、以本renew request与fresh proof追加唯一terminal_application_catchup event、撤销g、签发绑定current terminal application及previous/result history digest的g+1，并在epoch recovery分支同一mutation清空recovery marker、更新Head serving lease digest；此后且仅此后恢复ordinary proof/readiness。catchup response成功后任何PITR resume只可走§4.4 post_catchup_marker_cleared，绝不再次catchup。

三种renewal都允许new attestor lease等于current serving lease已绑定的lease且chain=null；attestor rollover exception允许它是§6.2同binding/generation的完整连续1..64代descendant，proof与request必须绑定同一chain。attestor lease、identity/lineage或authority context更新后直到provider原子签发新serving generation，Reserve/Finalize/Abort/readiness全部拒绝；即使旧provider lease已经过期，只要对应封闭matrix全部成立且current attestor lease仍有效，也只允许该rollover request。provider随后原子撤销旧provider lease、generation加一并返回issuance_request_schema/digest等于本renew request的ClaimV1ServingLeaseV1；同renewal ID/body exact retry返回同一envelope，字段改变fixed conflict，response loss先Inspect旧ID/Head。若先前n+1已应用则沿其新Head继续；若未应用而current attestor已到n+k，则用完整chain和包含current context的新deterministic ID提交一次g→g+1。超过64代、gap、另一holder/generation、Head不再引用旧lease或current attestor过期必须终止binding并走rebind。

RevokeClaimV1ServingLeaseRequestV1 body字段恰为 `revocation_id`、`activation_id`、`current_serving_lease_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`expected_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`reason`、`request_nonce`；reason只有planned_shutdown、runtime_holder_lost、incarnation_rotation、security_revoke。revocation ID/body digest与nonce在provider调用前由attestor rollback-resistant保存。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1ServingLeaseRevocationV1使用相同字段并额外恰有 `revoked_generation`、`phase=active_lease_revoked`、`revoked_at`。exact retry幂等；rebind按§4.4验证后才能从新runtime generation签发generation=1的新lease并回到active。lease自然过期即readiness失败，不隐式续租或允许第二holder。

RebindClaimV1RuntimeInstanceRequestV1 body字段恰为 `rebind_id`、`activation_id`、`mode`、`rebind_scope`、`expected_phase`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`old_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`expected_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`old_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`new_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`new_runtime_instance_lease_digest`、`runtime_rebind_authorization_digest`、`database_authority_head_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_rebind_gap_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_post_recovery_rebind_attestation_digest_or_null`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`database_point`、`credential_policy_digest`、`previous_database_identity_digest`、`new_database_identity_digest`、`previous_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`result_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_id_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_evidence_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_ordinal_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_archive_inspect_response_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_resume_phase_or_null`、`expected_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`cancellation_id_or_null`、`epoch_terminal_application_id_or_null`、`expected_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`pending_epoch_transition_intent_digest_or_null`、`pending_epoch_transition_application_digest_or_null`、`pending_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`expected_provider_pending_epoch_transition_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_id_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest_or_null`、`staging_exclusion_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_recovery_commit_evidence_set_digest_or_null`、`request_nonce`。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ClaimV1RuntimeInstanceRebindV1使用相同字段并额外恰有 `previous_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`new_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`revoked_old_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`initial_new_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_intent_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_application_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_provider_pending_transition_digest_or_null`、`cancellation_kind_or_null`、`preserved_epoch_or_null`、`preserved_epoch_transition_chain_digest_or_null`、`previous_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`resulting_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_state_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_request_digest_or_null`、`previous_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`result_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`database_result_application`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_id_or_null`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_recovery_commit_evidence_set_digest_or_null`、`result_staging_exclusion_state_or_null`、`provider_control_sequence`、`result_phase`、`rebound_at`；previous runtime/lineage chains必须等于request expected chains，response body digest成为new runtime chain head，timeline lineage按§4.2保持或推进。scope=epoch_transition_cancellation时cancellation/epoch/terminal字段按§4.6 present并推进terminal chain；其他scope的cancellation tuple全null且previous/result terminal chain逐字相等。scope=epoch_transition_recovery只设置或保持recovery state、application/proof/Inspect/resume/history字段全null、绝不改变既有terminal outcome且`database_result_application=deferred_epoch_recovery`。scope=epoch_transition_recovery_applied时fresh application proof与Inspect逐字复制request，previous history等于request expected tail，result history按§6.1事件公式增长一次且`database_result_application=immediate`；pre_catchup保持state=transcript_issued并发rollover-only lease，post_catchup_marker_cleared保持state=null并发普通active rebind lease。其余scope全部epoch recovery/suffix/decision/application/application-proof/Inspect/resume/history/gap/post-recovery字段null且`database_result_application=immediate`。

phase matrix固定为：registered_pending_genesis、genesis_prepared、genesis_completed_pending_release与genesis_release_prepared保持原phase且new serving lease=null；fresh_restore_target的fresh_v7_staging_closed保持该phase且lease=null；只有empty_in_place的active或active_lease_revoked进入active并发new generation=1 lease。pending epoch只允许scope=epoch_transition_cancellation，request/authorization逐字列出同一pending/terminal context，provider按§4.6 CAS取消且全部response cancellation字段exact反映已有值；provider已terminal但DB因PITR落后只允许epoch_transition_recovery，发出的lease绑定current provider context但标记rollover-only，Head recovery state进入required或保持transcript_issued。DB recovery application已提交且marker=transcript_issued时只允许epoch_transition_recovery_applied/pre_catchup，发rollover-only lease、要求immediate DB result且仍不清marker；marker=null但exact history证明唯一catchup后零无关mutation时只允许epoch_transition_recovery_applied/post_catchup_marker_cleared，发普通active rebind lease、要求immediate result且不重建marker。无pending/recovery且不存在该受限marker-cleared resume时才允许normal scope且对应tuple全null。

held staging只允许staging_held_preserve或staging_exclusion_recovery、fresh_v7_staging_closed→same phase、serving始终null、同一exclusion/acquire request；preserved字段与result state必须等于原hold/recovery intent。authorization/request/response/result的`staging_recovery_commit_evidence_set_digest_or_null`必须按§7.2 exact archive集合重算并逐字相等。runtime_rebind_operator在签authorization前必须展开exact set及每份signed Inspect response；所有response的七项candidate echo必须逐字相同并等于本次new candidate：identity=new_database_identity，lineage=result lineage，binding=new binding，instance ID/generation/lease等于bundle中exact new binding+holder-lease envelopes重算值。staging candidate registration必须non-null，并等于authorization内嵌DB Head的current registration；Rebind request.old_database_incarnation_registration_digest必须逐字复制该值。任一item来自另一candidate、旧generation/lease或wrong/null registration固定拒绝。

RebindClaimV1RuntimeInstanceRequestV1必须逐字复制authorization的evidence-set digest与new-candidate tuple；provider不得只信operator signature或opaque digest，而要在CAS前独立展开同一set、验证每份Inspect签名/generation/body/head与上述逐字段等式。成功CAS永久保存exact set preimage、全部signed Inspect envelopes以及new candidate binding/holder-lease envelopes；遗漏已attest body、添加未attest candidate、cross-candidate splice或集合digest变化固定拒绝。首次staging_exclusion_recovery把held变为held_recovery_required；已required或abort-only的后续holder更换必须保持原state、exact intent/recovery request与当时evidence set。新holder不得开始新的import；若pre-rebind import/revocation outcome已exact提交，可取得fresh current-Head continuity challenge/attestation并Release/normal Abort；否则只能提交recovery intent、Recover、recovery revocation、fresh commit proof并Abort。pending epoch、epoch recovery与held staging互斥。rebind初始lease的renewal_id固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.claim-v1-serving-lease-rebind-id.v1") || 0x00 || raw rebind UUID)`，issuance request schema/digest等于exact rebind request，所以lease可在response之前计算且不引用response chain digest。provider验证§4.4 authorization、termination、lineage、DB head attestation、旧holder失效与new generation后，在一个CAS mutation中创建DatabaseIncarnationRebindingRegistrationV1、注册new binding、撤销旧lease、执行可选pending取消、设置或保留可选recovery state并按matrix发放或不发放lease；Resolve已经赢得terminal CAS时取消分支零registration/lease副作用，必须先Inspect并落DB resolved terminal application，或在真正PITR时走epoch recovery。同rebind ID/body exact retry幂等，字段变化conflict，response loss按rebind ID Inspect。

provider response直返或Inspect恢复后，除下述deferred epoch-recovery suffix外，任何下一步provider mutation/readiness前必须提交 AuthorityRuntimeRebindResultV1；body字段恰为 `rebind_id`、`activation_id`、`runtime_registration_result_digest`、`previous_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`provider_rebind_request_digest`、`provider_rebind_digest`、`authorized_database_authority_head_digest`、`database_authority_rebind_gap_attestation_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_post_recovery_rebind_attestation_digest_or_null`、`authorized_database_point`、`previous_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`previous_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_attestation_digest_or_null`、`previous_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`previous_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`previous_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_id`、`current_runtime_instance_generation`、`current_attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`revoked_previous_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`current_serving_lease_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_intent_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_application_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_epoch_transition_resolution_digest_or_null`、`cancelled_provider_pending_transition_digest_or_null`、`cancellation_id_or_null`、`previous_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`resulting_epoch_transition_terminal_chain_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_state_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_request_digest_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_suffix_id_or_null`、`deferred_runtime_rebind_ordinal_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_prefix_decision_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_application_archive_inspect_response_digest_or_null`、`epoch_transition_recovery_resume_phase_or_null`、`previous_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`result_epoch_transition_recovery_consumption_history_digest_or_null`、`database_result_application`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_id_or_null`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_exclusion_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`preserved_staging_recovery_commit_evidence_set_digest_or_null`、`result_staging_exclusion_state_or_null`、`provider_phase`、`provider_head_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`recorded_database_point`、`recorded_at`。首项previous result为null并从registration result root开始，后续逐字引用上一result；current runtime chain必须等于provider rebind body digest，current identity/lineage必须按scope matrix保持或推进。row保存authorization/request/response/post-Head/new DatabaseIncarnationRebindingRegistrationV1/binding/attestor/timeline lineage及可选serving lease、epoch cancellation/recovery decision/application、fresh application proof/Inspect、history event与staging preservation exact evidence，按rebind ID唯一且禁止UPDATE/DELETE。deferred recovery result的nullable decision proof逐字来自provider transcript且application proof/Inspect/resume/history字段全null；applied scope必须逐字保存fresh application challenge/attestation、matched Inspect、resume phase及previous/result history并立即提交本row，phase=pre_catchup时state=transcript_issued，phase=post_catchup_marker_cleared时state=null。response已成功而row缺失时provider continuity state只能rebind_result_reconcile_required，且只允许§4.4 exact reconcile。

`database_result_application=immediate`时request.latest DB result必须就是本row.previous result，response含epoch cancellation则result、AuthorityEpochTransitionCancellationV1与AuthorityEpochTransitionTerminalApplicationV1同transaction提交；staging recovery result仍可独立先提交并保持closed。`deferred_epoch_recovery`时provider response之后DB result必须保持absent，唯一下一provider mutation是同recovery/suffix的下一ordinal deferred rebind或Recover；每个request仍观察同一gap anchor latest DB result，不能伪称DB已推进。restricted recovery transaction才按provider suffix创建这些row：ordinal 1的previous result是同transaction已恢复的terminal-cancellation result或原gap anchor，后续逐份引用刚创建的直接前驱；因此只有该分支允许row.previous result不同于原request.latest observed result。provider成功但DB immediate result缺失时只按同ID Inspect/reconcile；deferred response loss按suffix/ordinal Inspect。DatabaseAuthorityHead始终只从已提交连续result链采用new runtime/current identity/lineage。

### 6.4 exact retry 与 crash recovery

- provider registration成功、DB registration result未提交：Inspect exact registration并重读registered_pending_genesis Head，再写同一result；postmaster restart可从result走closed-phase rebind；
- provider rebind成功、DB rebind result未提交：Inspect exact request/bundle/rebinding-registration/rebind与post-rebind Head；normal/held/staging-exclusion-recovery分支补写连续immediate result，epoch-cancellation分支原子补写result+cancellation+terminal application；deferred epoch-recovery response只按same recovery/suffix/ordinal Inspect并保持DB result absent，唯一下一provider mutation是下一ordinal rebind或Recover，完整suffix只在§4.6 restricted transaction原子落库。timeline-changing evidence必须逐字保存；其间所有普通/genesis下一步保持关闭；
- attempt已提交、provider未prepared：使用row中preparation ID重试同一 Prepare；
- provider prepared、DB activation未提交：复用相同 activation ID/attempt，保持关闭；
- DB activation已提交、provider未completion：重试相同 Complete；
- provider completion response loss：使用row中completion ID Inspect exact completion；provider仍处于 closed phase；
- DB completion已提交、provider未release-prepared：重试相同 PrepareRelease；
- provider release response loss：使用row中release-preparation ID Inspect exact release；provider仍处于 closed phase；
- DB release已提交、provider未active：重试相同 Open；
- Open response loss：使用row中open ID Inspect exact ClaimV1GenesisOpenV1并重读stable Head；DB barrier在Open前已完整；
- lease renew/revoke response loss：Inspect exact renewal/revocation ID；不创建重叠generation；
- epoch intent存在、provider未pending：holder存活时重试同一transition request，holder死亡时只走cancellation-rebind CAS；provider pending response loss按transition ID Inspect后写application+resolution；DB resolution已提交时Resolve与cancel CAS只能一个胜出。普通crash未丢DB row时，resolve response loss按resolution ID Inspect后写resolved terminal application，cancel response loss按cancellation/rebind ID Inspect后原子写三row；两种Head必须引用同一terminal chain；
- provider terminal领先且PITR实际丢失intent/application/resolution或已被provider消费的terminal group时，普通current-tx insert固定拒绝；必须先提交§4.6 recovery intent，holder/timeline改变只能随后用intent-bound gap attestation追加deferred recovery-only suffix，transcript_issued后的replacement还必须先由唯一prefix decision+commit proof赢得仲裁；最终以suffix-bound row proof取得provider exact transcript并在单个restricted transaction提交apply decision、逐字rehydrate terminal group、完整result suffix与recovery application。application后holder丢失只走post-recovery attestation、本candidate fresh proof与applied-scope immediate result；pre_catchup之后仍需唯一catchup，post_catchup_marker_cleared则result后直接恢复ordinary且不二次catchup。证据/lineage不可得、DB不是exact common ancestor、ordinary/domain anchor落后或provider已有later mutation时永久fail closed；
- epoch/staging recovery intent commit response未知时trusted-read exact row并重取challenge，不创建第二intent；epoch provider recovery response loss按recovery_transcript ID、staging按recovery ID Inspect exact request/bundle/response；DB recovery application response未知按ID、paired apply decision、post Head与archive state exact重判。epoch transcript后但apply decision尚未提交时，holder失效只可由same-prefix replacement decision仲裁后追加same suffix下一ordinal并用更长prefix的新transcript ID重取完整response；apply decision/application已提交后禁止该路径，必须先取得或恢复atomic application+decision closure，再走direct catchup或applied-scope rebind。第二次PITR按§4.6同一recovery的absent-latch→atomic Fence→未归档重跑、已归档exact pair恢复、已消费history恢复或marker-cleared零later-mutation resume四分支处理；staging different-body重跑同样先用对应single-key Fence。不能猜测旧transaction metadata、仅废弃旧challenge或追加different application；
- 任一unresolved registration/rebind/epoch result、intent/application/resolution/terminal/recovery prefix decision/application、timeline lineage或staging recovery：writer/readiness保持关闭，不从时间、最新Head或caller response猜测成功；
- 任一 request字段改变、epoch变化、inventory/credential/incarnation sequence变化：fixed conflict；
- 任何依赖不可用：保持关闭，不回滚 provider、不降低 profile、不删除 attempt/barrier。

## 7. Fresh restore target

### 7.1 sealed source 与 FreshRestoreRequirementV1

00007 Up只有两个边界：若LocalRuntimeIsolationV1缺少本地session drain、database route closure或local deployment capability revocation，Up在DDL前拒绝且不写任何row；一旦Up成功，即使inventory/provider依赖仍不可用，coordinator也必须立即提交本地可构造的IndeterminateSourceSealV1并触发DB freeze。

IndeterminateSourceSealV1 body字段恰为 `source_seal_id`、`upgrade_intent_digest`、`activation_id`、`request_nonce`、`classification=indeterminate`、`reason`、`failed_checks`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`observed_deployment_id`、`observed_database_identity_digest`、`migration_version=7`、`normalized_catalog_digest_or_null`、`local_locked_table_inventory_digest_or_null`、`environment_inventory_digest_or_null`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest_or_null`、`observed_provider_identity_digest_or_null`、`observed_provider_namespace_or_null`、`observed_provider_head_digest_or_null`、`missing_external_evidence`、`sealed_at`。missing_external_evidence使用§4.3枚举且可为空；failed_checks是bytewise排序、非重复且非空的封闭集合，枚举只有catalog_integrity、local_locked_inventory、environment_inventory、inventory_anchor、provider_inspect、provider_identity_match、provider_credential_policy、external_shutdown、nonce_consistency、legacy_epoch_maximum。reason恰为dependency_unavailable、dependency_timeout、environment_inventory_missing、environment_inventory_incomplete、environment_inventory_stale、environment_inventory_replayed、environment_inventory_signature_invalid、provider_identity_mismatch、provider_inspect_unavailable、provider_credential_policy_invalid、external_shutdown_incomplete、nonce_conflict、catalog_unknown、legacy_epoch_indeterminate之一，并必须与failed check一致；因此“external齐全但catalog unknown”可编码为missing=[]、failed_checks=[catalog_integrity]。不可观测外部字段显式null，不允许伪造zero digest。该row与FreshRestoreRequirement同一transaction提交，足以永久拒绝DB写和Down；它不能授权archive、epoch evidence或fresh target activation。

依赖恢复后只能追加完整LegacySourceSealV1，不能改写minimal seal。完整legacy source必须先完成§4.3永久LegacySourceMembershipRetirementV1，再对inventory每个member完成LegacyDatabaseSourceRetirementV1、environment/provider永久retirement与exact LegacySourceRetirementSetV1；短时shutdown或单一DB seal不能替代。provider accepted key set先清空，route/credential/deployment capability永久关闭，coordinator再生成本节exact epoch source set，并在每个source DB锁定manifest全部表/catalog提交该DB自己的LegacySourceSealV1；transaction内不调用provider。其 body 字段恰为：

- `source_seal_id`、`upgrade_intent_digest`、`activation_id`、`classification=legacy_or_nonempty`、`indeterminate_source_seal_digest_or_null`；
- `environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`；
- `deployment_id`、`database_identity_digest`、`normalized_catalog_digest`、`legacy_source_inventory_digest`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`；
- `provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`provider_namespace`、`provider_profile`、`provider_head_digest`、`provider_history_high_water`、`credential_policy_digest`；
- `database_route_closed_digest`、`deployment_capability_revocation_digest`；
- `legacy_epoch_source_set_digest`、`legacy_epoch_maximum`、`legacy_epoch_maximum_evidence_digest`、`sealed_at`。

LegacySourceInventoryV1 body字段恰为 `inventory_id`、`inventory_stage`、`source_database_identity_digest`、`normalized_catalog_digest`、`stable_inventory_content_digest`、`table_inventory`、`maximum_database_fence_epoch_or_zero`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`observed_at`，inventory_stage只有retirement、seal或post_seal。table_inventory使用§4.5 exact per-table item schema；stable_inventory_content_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.legacy-source-inventory-content.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"source_database_identity_digest":...,"normalized_catalog_digest":...,"table_inventory":...,"maximum_database_fence_epoch_or_zero":...}))`，明确排除stage/snapshot/time/point。retirement→seal→post_seal只允许snapshot/point/time合法前进且stable content byte-identical。

三种epoch source identity与evidence schema没有caller选择。database_fence identity固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.legacy-epoch-source-database-fence.v1") || 0x00 || raw database_identity_digest)`，唯一evidence schema是legacy-source-inventory.v1。provider_namespace identity固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.legacy-epoch-source-provider-namespace.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"provider_identity_digest":...,"provider_endpoint_identity_digest":...,"namespace":...}))`，唯一evidence schema是legacy-provider-epoch-transcript.v1。deployment_manifest identity固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.legacy-epoch-source-deployment-manifest.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"deployment_id":...}))`，唯一evidence schema是legacy-deployment-epoch-manifest.v1。

signer_role=`legacy_epoch_provider` 的 LegacyProviderEpochTranscriptV1 body字段恰为 `transcript_id`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`protocol_profile`、`provider_control_sequence`、`provider_history_high_water`、`epoch_count`、`epochs`、`maximum_epoch_or_zero`、`credential_policy_digest`、`observed_at`、`expires_at`。epochs是截至high-water在所有legacy/claim-v1 reservation、terminal、genesis、cutover和epoch-transition history出现过的完整、numeric-ascending、非重复canonical decimal集合；空集max=`0`，否则max等于末项。provider retirement request/response绑定该digest与high-water，之后tombstone禁止新增epoch。

signer_role=`release_deployment_operator` 的 LegacyDeploymentEpochManifestV1 body字段恰为 `manifest_id`、`manifest_sequence`、`previous_manifest_digest_or_null`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`deployment_id`、`deployment_revision`、`environment_count`、`environment_record_digests`、`legacy_environment_retirement_digests`、`deployment_retirement_aggregate_digest`、`deployment_capability_revocation_digest`、`epoch_count`、`epochs`、`maximum_epoch_or_zero`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`。两个environment数组按该deployment的inventory record顺序一一对应、同count且exact覆盖。aggregate固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.legacy-deployment-retirement-aggregate.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"deployment_id":...,"environment_record_digests":...,"legacy_environment_retirement_digests":...}))`，deployment observation的source_retirement_digest必须等于该值。epochs是该deployment manifest中所有authority epoch entry的完整排序唯一集合并按同一规则取max；release operator在backup domain之外rollback-resistant保存latest sequence/digest，retirement后同deployment禁止新manifest或capability。

signer_role=`legacy_epoch_auditor` 的 LegacyEpochSourceObservationV1 body字段恰为 `observation_id`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`environment_record_digests`、`source_kind`、`source_identity_digest`、`source_retirement_digest`、`evidence_schema`、`evidence_body_digest`、`maximum_epoch_or_zero`、`observed_at`；source_kind只有database_fence、provider_namespace、deployment_manifest，record digests按bytewise排序并exact列出引用该source的inventory records。database source_retirement_digest是DB retirement row digest，provider是terminal provider retirement digest，deployment按下文aggregate公式；CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1携带唯一允许schema的exact preimage/envelope。verifier重算membership、identity、record coverage、epoch array/max与retirement binding；kind/schema错配、caller summary或opaque Head固定拒绝。

signer_role=`legacy_epoch_auditor` 的 LegacyEpochSourceSetV1 body字段恰为 `release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`member_derivation=inventory-databases-plus-distinct-provider-namespaces-plus-distinct-deployments.v1`、`expected_member_count`、`observation_count`、`observations`、`legacy_epoch_maximum`、`observed_at`；observations是完整LegacyEpochSourceObservationV1 envelopes并按 `(source_kind,source_identity_digest)` 排序。verifier从永久membership与完整inventory机械派生每个environment database_fence、每个distinct `(provider identity,endpoint,namespace)` provider_namespace及每个distinct deployment的manifest member，要求retirement set exact覆盖且全部tombstone terminal，再要求observation exact coverage、无extra/duplicate，逐份验证evidence并checked-max全部maximum。

LegacyEpochMaximumEvidenceV1使用同一signer role，body字段恰为 `environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`legacy_epoch_source_set_digest`、`expected_member_count`、`legacy_epoch_maximum`、`observed_at`；它重验permanent membership、retirement/source set exact coverage和maximum，不接受singular provider Head或caller给出的member count。任一source缺失/unknown/overflow则不能签发。

若由indeterminate升级为完整seal，reference必须等于同DB existing minimal seal；直接完整分类时为null。每个local seal中的retirement/source-set/maximum/evidence digest必须逐字段匹配global objects。seal提交后§5.5 trigger继续冻结source。signer_role=`legacy_source_archive_attestor` 的 LegacySourceSealCommitAttestationV1 body 字段恰为 `source_seal_digest`、`source_database_identity_digest`、`legacy_source_inventory_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`timeline`、`commit_lsn`、`database_route_closed_digest`、`provider_credential_policy_digest`、`attested_at`。archive attestor不接受caller提供的seal body/digest/LSN；它通过绑定exact source DB identity的可信只读连接自行读取唯一immutable seal及inventory row、重算digest、确认另一个transaction已提交可见并读取server WAL point后才签署。

每个environment seal后，signer_role=`legacy_source_archive_attestor` 的 LegacySourcePostSealObservationV1 body字段恰为 `source_membership_retirement_digest`、`environment_record_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_member_digest`、`source_seal_digest`、`source_seal_commit_attestation_digest`、`epoch_database_observation_digest`、`epoch_provider_observation_digest`、`post_seal_legacy_source_inventory_digest`、`post_seal_provider_head_digest`、`stable_database_content_digest`、`terminal_provider_head_digest`、`legacy_environment_retirement_digest`、`legacy_provider_namespace_retirement_digest`、`database_point`、`observed_at`。attestor自行trusted-read exact permanent membership、retired DB/seal/inventory并Inspect terminal provider/environment tombstone；post-seal stable content必须等于retirement与seal inventory，provider Head必须仍是terminal retired state。

signer_role=`release_deployment_operator` 的 LegacySourcePostSealExactCoverV1 body字段恰为 `release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`legacy_epoch_source_set_digest`、`legacy_epoch_maximum_evidence_digest`、`expected_member_count`、`observation_count`、`observations`、`all_database_content_unchanged=true`、`all_provider_heads_terminal=true`、`all_environment_retirements_current=true`、`observed_at`。observations按永久membership的inventory record顺序exact覆盖，无extra/duplicate，bundle携带membership、全部local seal/attestation/post-seal/retirement evidence。

最后发布 signer_role=`release_deployment_operator` 的 LegacySourceArchiveEvidenceV1；body字段恰为 `release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`legacy_source_retirement_set_digest`、`legacy_source_post_seal_exact_cover_digest`、`legacy_epoch_source_set_digest`、`legacy_epoch_maximum_evidence_digest`、`archive_snapshot_id`、`archive_point`、`archive_digest`、`archived_at`。archive与后续target admission都必须逐份验证permanent membership、不可逆tombstone和exact cover；singular DB/provider/route check永远不能替代。任一member content/head/retirement mismatch固定source_seal_violated；archive后同release scope不能新增member，在原deployment/provider namespace恢复route、credential、policy或更高epoch的尝试由tombstone永久拒绝。

FreshRestoreRequirementV1 body 字段恰为 `requirement_id`、`upgrade_intent_digest`、`source_seal_kind`、`source_seal_digest`、`classification`、`reason`、`failed_checks`、`missing_external_evidence`、`source_deployment_id_or_null`、`source_database_identity_digest`、`source_provider_identity_digest_or_null`、`source_provider_namespace_or_null`、`environment_inventory_digest_or_null`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest_or_null`、`local_runtime_isolation_digest`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_digest_or_null`、`legacy_runtime_shutdown_set_digest_or_null`、`required_new_database_identity=true`、`required_new_deployment_id=true`、`required_new_provider_namespace=true`、`required_non_exportable_incarnation=true`、`blocked_capabilities`、`created_at`。source_seal_kind只有indeterminate或legacy_complete；indeterminate branch的nullable/failed/missing/reason逐字段等于minimal seal，legacy_complete branch的failed/missing均为空且reason只有legacy_state_present或provider_history_present。blocked_capabilities 恰为按bytewise排序的 `["c12_root_metadata_signer_rotation","node_authority_restore_reenrollment","node_operator_server_ca_rotation","operator_authorizer_change","trust_bundle_publish"]`。row与对应source seal同transaction提交并禁止UPDATE/DELETE。

manifest只接受两条exact append-only chain：direct-full要求requirement.source_seal_kind=legacy_complete且requirement.source_seal_digest等于full seal；minimal-then-full要求requirement引用minimal seal、full seal.indeterminate_source_seal_digest引用同一minimal seal。后一分支还要求upgrade intent、activation ID、observed/source DB identity、deployment及所有已观测provider字段在minimal、requirement与full seal之间逐字段相等；archive必须引用该full seal。任何跨source、跨activation或只替换full seal的splice固定拒绝。

legacy source 只用于离线取证/受签 export，不作为 target runtime dependency，不得创建 claim-v1 activation，也不得恢复 route、凭据或部署能力。

### 7.2 fresh target 与 canonical import

fresh target 必须使用新的 PostgreSQL system/cluster identity、database OID/name、deployment ID、数据库凭据、备份域、claim-v1 provider namespace/profile 与 §4.4 non-exportable incarnation credential。AuthorityGenesisEpochEvidenceV1 必须证明 selected epoch 严格高于 sealed legacy maximum；无法证明 maximum、source provider identity 或完整 source inventory时，target固定 staging closed，不能猜测 epoch。

允许 import 的 object_type 枚举恰为 pop、failure_domain_definition、capacity_profile_definition、node_reconstruction_seed。node_reconstruction_seed 只含 canonical node ID 与 POP归属；consumer 必须新建 operator_state=disabled、identity_state=unauthorized、health_state=unknown 且所有 active/resume/pending pointer为 null。禁止整库 pg_dump/restore nodecontrol authority schema。

四种payload是封闭的：pop恰有 `pop_code`、`iso_country`、`region`；failure_domain_definition恰有 `failure_domain_id`、`domain_type`、`stable_id`；capacity_profile_definition恰有 `profile_id`、`version`、`adapter`、`egress_limit_bps`、`connection_limit`、`handshake_limit_per_second`、`cpu_quota_millicores`、`cpu_limit_basis_points`、`memory_limit_bytes`、`task_limit`、`file_descriptor_limit`、`queue_limit`、`packet_loss_limit_basis_points`、`required_metrics`；node_reconstruction_seed恰有 `node_id`、`pop_code`。canonical key分别是pop_code、failure-domain UUID、`profile UUID/version`与node UUID。payload_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.fresh-import-payload-") || ASCII(object_type) || ASCII(".v1") || 0x00 || JCS(payload))`。

FreshImportTopologyProjectionV1 是与import control/evidence无关的纯投影；body字段恰为 `projection_version=1`、`target_activation_id`、`target_deployment_id`、`target_database_identity_digest`、`normalized_catalog_digest`、`object_count`、`objects`。objects按 `(object_type,canonical_key)` 排序，每项字段恰为 `object_type`、`canonical_key`、`normalized_payload`。pop normalized payload在source payload上固定增加 `operator_state=disabled`、`version="1"`；failure domain固定增加 `version="1"`；capacity profile等于上列payload；node项固定增加 `operator_state=disabled`、`security_state=quarantined`、`identity_state=unauthorized`、`health_state=unknown`、`identity_epoch="0"`、`inventory_version="1"`、`security_version="1"`、`next_desired_generation="1"`、`next_recovery_generation="1"`、`resume_operator_state_or_null=null`、`pending_operator_transition_or_null=null`、`active_pointer_set=[]`、`authority_anchor_set=[]`。consumer必须逐字段构造这些值，timestamp只取本次applied_at。投影明确排除manifest/capability/application/evidence digest、transaction ID、snapshot、timestamp与database point；因此pre、manifest expected-post与actual-post可独立计算且不会形成摘要环。

signer_role=`fresh_restore_export_operator` 的 FreshRestoreImportManifestV1 body 字段恰为 `manifest_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`fresh_restore_requirement_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`source_legacy_retirement_set_digest`、`source_post_seal_exact_cover_digest`、`source_archive_evidence_digest`、`source_legacy_epoch_source_set_digest`、`source_legacy_epoch_maximum_evidence_digest`、`source_snapshot_id`、`source_archive_point`、`target_activation_id`、`target_deployment_id`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`target_epoch_evidence_digest`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`、`object_count`、`objects`、`complete_node_set_digest`、`forbidden_object_class_set_digest`、`expected_post_import_inventory_digest`。有效窗口不超过 15 分钟；只有§7.1两种local seal chain之一且permanent membership/global retirement/post-seal/archive exact cover全部完整时可签发，indeterminate或singular seal本身不能进入manifest。

objects 按 `(object_type, canonical_key)` 排序；每项字段恰为 `object_type`、`canonical_key`、`payload`、`payload_digest`，payload 使用 object_type 对应的封闭 schema。unknown/duplicate object、digest/count mismatch、wrong source snapshot、wrong target、expired manifest或重用 apply ID固定拒绝。禁止类别包括 fence/operation/authority tuple/digest、grant/certificate/issuance/lineage/credential、incident/fault/approval/recovery session、signing/publish intent/share、desired/recovery state、root/metadata/trust high-water、active/resume/pending pointer、provider result、secret/token/key/signature以及 authority audit/outbox payload。

`expected_post_import_inventory_digest`必须是由manifest objects构造的FreshImportTopologyProjectionV1 body digest；pre/post inventory digest也只能是该schema。complete_node_set_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.complete-node-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(sorted-node-id-array))`；allowed_object_set_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.allowed-import-object-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(sorted-array-of-{object_type,canonical_key,payload_digest}))`；forbidden_object_class_set_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.forbidden-import-object-class-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(bytewise-sorted-fixed-enum-array))`。manifest签发者与consumer都从同一objects重算，不能由caller给摘要。

B11先在DB transaction外验证manifest/source retirement/archive、取得fresh pre-acquire Head/proof，并预生成capability/exclusion/terminal IDs；凡可能授权§6.1 Fence的semantic ID，还必须在任何candidate row/challenge前通过commit-attestor/Fence signer authenticated reservation API写入其rollback-resistant semantic-ID journal，B11不能直接写archive。signer_role=`fresh_restore_staging_import_authorizer` 的 StagingImportCapabilityV1 body字段恰为 `capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`manifest_digest`、`target_activation_id`、`target_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`planned_staging_exclusion_id`、`expected_pre_acquire_provider_head_digest`、`pre_acquire_database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`provider_phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed`、`provider_serving_lease_absent_digest`、`database_route_closed_digest`、`pre_import_inventory_digest`、`allowed_object_set_digest`、`expected_post_import_inventory_digest`、`transaction_nonce`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过5分钟且不绑定尚未开启的import transaction ID。authoritative executor先用dedicated role在独立DB transaction按下述统一锁序提交exact capability envelope；recovery intent已存在时即使同capability exact retry也固定拒绝。提交后按§6.1以row_kind=staging_import_capability_registration、row_digest=capability body digest取得DatabaseCommitChallengeV1，再由incarnation attestor按(activation,row kind,row digest)自行trusted-read恰一行并签DatabaseCommitAttestationV1。不存在、未提交、多行、wrong DB/LSN或只按activation猜row固定拒绝；没有该exact commit challenge+attestation禁止provider acquire。

随后发送 AcquireFreshV7StagingExclusionRequestV1；body字段恰为 `exclusion_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`capability_registration_commit_challenge_digest`、`capability_registration_commit_attestation_digest`、`target_activation_id`、`target_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`expected_provider_head_digest`、`pre_acquire_database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`requested_admission_expires_at`、`request_nonce`。字段必须逐字等于capability/commit evidence且request bundle exact携带capability、challenge、attestation；provider原子消费commit challenge。provider只在capability/current request均未过期、phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed、serving lease absent、ordinary Head zero且没有pending epoch/recovery/rebind/exclusion时递增control sequence，把Head staging四字段设为exact exclusion ID/acquire request digest/null/`held`，并发布 signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 FreshV7StagingExclusionLeaseV1；response body字段恰为与request相同的字段加 `request_digest`、`exclusion_lease_id`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`provider_serving_lease_absent_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`exclusion_state=held`、`issued_at`、`admission_expires_at`，其中acquisition anchor是该mutation后的stable Head digest且Head不引用response。admission窗口最长5分钟；同ID/body exact retry，`admission_expires_at`只截止新的DB import admission，经过该时间绝不改变held state。held时provider拒绝Prepare/Open、credential-policy、epoch、ordinary registration、serving-lease、ordinary Head mutation及第二acquire，只允许Head/proof/Inspect、§6.3 held-preserving/recovery rebind、下述intent-backed recovery、fresh staging commit challenge及尚未recovery时的Release/Abort。

B01 Go verifier在开启写transaction前完成全部envelope/preimage验证；authoritative executor以dedicated `nodecontrol_staging_importer` role开启单一锁定DB transaction。所有capability registration、recovery intent、import、revoke、rehydrate与inventory/evidence snapshot统一按migration latch→target activation singleton→current runtime registration/rebind singleton→recovery-intent key→capability key→import-application key→revocation-application key→recovery-application key→manifest domain keys canonical order加锁；前三个existing singleton序列化absent key，禁止假装 `FOR UPDATE` 锁住不存在row，provider/attestor调用全部在transaction外。restricted `begin_staging_import`重验caller role、本地route/current identity/lineage/runtime、FreshImportTopologyProjection、provider state=held、current Head恰等于acquisition anchor及当前clock；只要求transaction开始时capability/admission未过期。它必须看到intent/capability/application/revocation/recovery=`0/1/0/0/0`，且capability registration/runtime/identity逐字等于current；held后发生任何rebind或recovery intent都会使import固定拒绝，只能转revocation/Abort。随后domain trigger只对manifest exact payload开放§5.5 branch；deferred constraint要求同一transaction提交application row、每个manifest object恰消费一次、post projection等于expected、forbidden state为零。WAL flush、同步复制或commit acknowledgment即使跨过admission expiry仍安全，因为provider保持held。整个DB transaction不调用provider或外部signer。

FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1 body 字段恰为 `single_use_apply_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null=null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null=null`、`manifest_digest`、`target_activation_id`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`staging_exclusion_lease_digest`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`database_route_closed_digest`、`pre_import_inventory_digest`、`post_import_inventory_digest`、`imported_object_count`、`complete_node_set_digest`、`forbidden_state_zero_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`applied_at`。apply ID unique且row禁止UPDATE/DELETE；expected post-import projection不匹配、普通SQL、已commit重放、manifest field tamper、recovery intent/application nonnull或任一non-allowlist mutation都固定拒绝并回滚。

provider仍持有exclusion但PITR丢失capability或使其runtime/identity成为受信ancestor时，不允许DB-only补写。若holder/timeline变化，先完成scope=staging_exclusion_recovery的recovery-only rebind及DB result；真实promotion必须携带§4.2 lineage且provider state从held变为held_recovery_required，same-timeline或后续rebind保持required/abort-only。随后B11以统一锁序提交 StagingImportCapabilityRecoveryIntentV1；body字段恰为 `recovery_intent_id`、`recovery_id`、`recovery_application_id`、`revocation_id`、`revocation_application_id`、`terminal_transition_id`、`target_activation_id`、`capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`capability_registration_commit_attestation_digest`、`staging_exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`expected_staging_exclusion_state`、`observed_capability_row_count`、`observed_exact_capability_digest_or_null`、`observed_import_application_row_count=0`、`observed_revocation_application_row_count=0`、`observed_recovery_application_row_count=0`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`pre_intent_database_authority_head_digest`、`recovery_reason`、`recovery_scope=rehydrate_for_revocation_only`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`transaction_nonce`、`created_at`。

observed capability count只能0或1；0要求digest=null且reason=pitr_capability_row_missing，1要求row byte-identical等于provider原Acquire bundle、digest present且reason=pitr_exact_capability_runtime_stale。timeline-changing的0分支还必须按§4.2证明provider archive中的原capability registration commit LSN严格晚于timeline_fork_cut；否则不能把capability当作合法丢失suffix。1分支的capability identity/runtime必须是current lineage/rebind chain的exact ancestor。application/revocation/recovery全零；intent body只引用pre-intent Head，提交后Head引用intent digest，无摘要环。capability registration若先取得singleton locks，intent可观察exact-one；intent若先提交，任何迟到registration包括exact retry固定拒绝。intent无TTL/cancel且禁止UPDATE/DELETE；存在后只允许commit proof、Recover、recovery-preserving rebind、rehydrate、recovery revoke与Abort。

intent提交后以 `row_kind=staging_import_capability_recovery_intent` 取得§6.1 commit challenge/attestation。RecoverStagingImportCapabilityRequestV1 body字段恰为 `recovery_id`、`recovery_intent_digest`、`recovery_intent_commit_challenge_digest`、`recovery_intent_commit_attestation_digest`、`recovery_application_id`、`revocation_id`、`revocation_application_id`、`terminal_transition_id`、`target_activation_id`、`capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`capability_registration_commit_attestation_digest`、`staging_exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`expected_staging_exclusion_state`、`observed_capability_row_count`、`observed_exact_capability_digest_or_null`、`database_authority_head_digest`、`database_authority_head_attestation_digest`、`database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`recovery_reason`、`recovery_scope=rehydrate_for_revocation_only`、`request_nonce`。provider从原Acquire archive重验exact capability/commit attestation，消费intent challenge，要求current Head是same exclusion的held或held_recovery_required、phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed、serving null、ordinary zero、DB Head exact引用intent且无application/revocation/recovery或pending epoch；然后原子把Head recovery request digest设为本request、state改为held_recovery_abort_only并使全部旧staging challenge失效。

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 StagingImportCapabilityRecoveryV1 body字段恰为request全部字段加 `request_digest`、`recovery_intent_body`、`staging_import_capability_envelope`、`capability_registration_commit_attestation_envelope`、`result_staging_exclusion_state=held_recovery_abort_only`、`result_provider_head_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`recovered_at`；三个嵌入preimage必须与provider永久archive及request digest逐字相等。同recovery ID/body exact retry，response loss通过Inspect返回exact request/bundle/response。restricted recovery transaction采用统一锁序并重验provider response/current identity/lineage：intent为exact-one时复用；因第二次PITR为zero时只可从provider response恢复原exact body，包括原transaction ID、snapshot、database point与time，restricted guard不得用current transaction重算它。capability为zero时插入原exact envelope，为exact-one时byte-identical复用且零UPDATE；application/revocation必须zero，recovery application可zero或byte-identical exact-one。任一different/multiple row固定拒绝。

StagingImportCapabilityRecoveryApplicationV1 body字段恰为 `recovery_application_id`、`provider_staging_import_capability_recovery_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest`、`recovery_id`、`capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_acquire_request_digest`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`current_held_provider_head_digest`、`observed_capability_row_count`、`materialized_capability_row_count=1`、`capability_materialization_kind`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`recovery_scope=rehydrate_for_revocation_only`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`recovered_at`；materialization kind只有inserted_from_provider_bundle或exact_existing_reused。row禁止UPDATE/DELETE；intent/application存在后即使capability尚未过期也永久禁止import与Release，只允许signed recovery revocation、commit proof与Abort。transaction提交后必须以§6.1 `row_kind=staging_import_capability_recovery_application` 取得fresh challenge和trusted-read DatabaseCommitAttestationV1；database-incarnation attestor只有在自行读到唯一committed exact row后才签名，并在返回attestation前把exact application body、challenge与attestation原子写入rollback-resistant archive。没有该proof禁止revoker签名，caller body、provider challenge或row digest本身都不能证明application曾提交。

第二次PITR时，recovery application为zero且global latch absent、尚无commit attestation或terminal consumer，必须先以fresh Inspect证明candidate row count=0，再用§6.1 `fence_reason=staging_generic_row_rerun`推进该semantic key generation；fence成功后才可在current restricted transaction以同一预分配semantic ID创建current body并取得绑定新generation的新challenge。旧attestation append先赢则fence失败且只能exact restore旧body；fence先赢则此前签发但未attest的generic challenge永久stale，不能仅靠“废弃旧challenge”重启。attestor一旦签名并完成rollback-resistant archive，exact body即被global latch锁定；此后无论revoker是否已经签名，都只能从该archive逐字恢复原application body，包括原transaction ID/snapshot/database point/time，不能以current transaction重算。recovery-branch revoker必须在签名前trusted-read验证exact application commit challenge/attestation，并永久保存provider recovery response、exact intent/capability/recovery-application及commit proof preimages；同revocation ID/body exact retry返回同一envelope，ID相同而application digest变化固定conflict。缺失或partial archive、attestation未提交/row kind错误、commit LSN不满足§4.2 timeline_fork_cut都只能fail closed，不能把signed digest、fence或未attest challenge当作足以重建body的commit承诺。

`staging_recovery_commit_evidence_set_digest_or_null`固定为null，或 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.staging-recovery-commit-evidence-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"candidate_key_observations":bytewise-sorted-array-of-{row_schema,semantic_row_id,archive_inspect_response_digest},"locked_evidence_items":bytewise-sorted-array-of-{row_schema,semantic_row_id,locked_row_body_digest,commit_evidence_mode,archive_inspect_response_digest,canonical_commit_attestation_schema_or_null,canonical_commit_attestation_digest_or_null,archive_head_sequence,archive_head_digest}}))`。candidate key universe绝不从PITR后的五表或caller列出的items推断：operator与provider分别从provider永久保存的exact StagingImportCapabilityRecovery request/response、当前exclusion/recovery phase及其中预分配ID机械派生。没有成功recovery response的held/held_recovery_required phase universe恰为空；held_recovery_abort_only及其保持性后继恰包含StagingImportCapabilityRecoveryApplicationV1/recovery_application_id与StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1/revocation_application_id两键。另一activation/exclusion/recovery、unknown schema或非预分配ID不进入universe也不得加入集合。

candidate_key_observations必须与完整universe exact-cover；每个key都绑定fresh signed Inspect response，即使archive_status=absent也不得省略。所有observation response的expected_provider_head_digest与七项candidate echo必须逐字相同；Head digest等于authorization.exact_provider_head_digest与request.expected_provider_head_digest，candidate tuple等于本次rebind new candidate。candidate registration必须non-null并等于DB Head current registration，runtime binding/ID/generation/lease必须等于bundle中exact new binding与holder-lease envelopes。locked_evidence_items必须机械等于observations中archive_status=locked且semantic_body_lock_role=primary的exact子集；absent observation不进入locked items，staging遇到related_only固定拒绝。

每个locked item必须复用同key observation的Inspect digest，绑定locked body、该key archive_fence_generation与完整archive head；mode没有caller选择而由response机械决定：candidate_row_count=1且candidate digest恰等于locked body时只能是`exact_existing_candidate`，canonical schema/digest逐字复制response且允许二者为null；candidate_row_count=0时只能是`archived_missing_suffix`，要求timeline-changing recovery、canonical schema/digest non-null并等于response机械选择的immediate trusted lineage parent entry，且其exact commit LSN严格大于timeline_fork_cut。different/multiple candidate row、count/nullability矛盾、cross-Head/cross-candidate/mixed-generation splice、wrong/null registration、旧parent、缺canonical的missing-row分支、用absent伪装locked response或caller指定mode一律固定拒绝。

只有candidate key universe本身为空时digest才必须null；universe非空时digest必须non-null，即使全部observation均archive_status=absent且locked subset为空。archive partial或Inspect/head不一致保持held。每次staging held-preserve/exclusion-recovery rebind authorization、request、response与DB result都绑定当时exact set digest。bundle必须携带全部observation的exact signed Inspect response envelopes；对每个locked item还携带locked primary body、全部locked related bodies、sequence 1..head的全部challenge/attestation preimages及其generation evidence；另携带provider保存的universe派生request/response与本次rebind exact new-candidate binding/holder-lease envelopes。只给included locked-item response、省略absence observation、用null隐藏nonempty universe、缺signed envelope或任一candidate tuple不等固定拒绝。operator与provider分别独立派生universe、验证所有observation、重算locked subset；成功CAS永久保存完整observation set及locked preimages。rebind之后新增proof或archive head变化产生新的set digest，不能把旧集合冒充current。`exact_existing_candidate`允许R1→R2成功后R2在未生成fresh archive entry前死亡时，由R3对live candidate上仍存在的exact locked row继续；它不把任意ancestor proof提升为missing-row证据。`archived_missing_suffix`仍只接受immediate-parent canonical proof与fork-cut规则。该集合证明locked historical body及本次live 0/1观察，不自行让missing row复活；missing row仍须按restricted restore规则处理。

staging本地状态矩阵按intent/capability/import-application/revocation-application/recovery-application顺序，恰为empty=`0/0/0/0/0`、issued=`0/1/0/0/0`、imported=`0/1/1/0/0`、revoked=`0/1/0/1/0`、recovery_intent_zero=`1/0/0/0/0`、recovery_intent_exact_one=`1/1/0/0/0`、recovered_abort_only=`1/1/0/0/1`、recovered_revoked=`1/1/0/1/1`；其他27种组合全部拒绝。provider已abort-only但DB再次PITR呈zero/exact-one时只能用stored recovery bundle收敛，不能按表面empty/issued走普通path。

主动取消使用独立 signer_role=`fresh_restore_staging_import_revoker` 的 StagingImportCapabilityRevocationV1；body字段恰为 `revocation_id`、`revocation_application_id`、`capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`recovery_application_commit_challenge_digest_or_null`、`recovery_application_commit_attestation_digest_or_null`、`manifest_digest`、`target_activation_id`、`planned_staging_exclusion_id`、`expected_pre_acquire_provider_head_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`current_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`revocation_reason`、`request_nonce`、`revoked_at`，reason只有cancelled_before_import、admission_expired_unapplied、source_or_archive_revoked、manifest_revoked、authorizer_revoked、import_transaction_resolved_rolled_back、pitr_recovery_abort_only。normal branch必须在journal reservation与revoker签名前预分配revocation_application_id并使signed body、trusted journal key逐字相等；intent/recovery及两个recovery commit-proof字段全null且current identity/runtime等于capability。recovery branch要求revocation_application_id逐字等于recovery intent/request预存值，exact intent+recovery及两个proof字段全present、provider state=held_recovery_abort_only；revoker从trusted archive重算application body/digest并验证challenge/attestation的row kind、identity/lineage/commit LSN，不能接受caller-only preimage。restricted revoke path采用统一锁序；只有application=0且revocation=0时插入 StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1，body字段恰为 `revocation_application_id`、`staging_import_capability_revocation_digest`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`capability_id`、`single_use_apply_id`、`manifest_digest`、`target_activation_id`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`current_database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`current_runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`current_runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`import_application_absence_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`applied_at`，其中revocation_application_id必须逐字复制signed revocation且与journal key相等。absence digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.staging-import-application-absent.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"single_use_apply_id":...,"row_count":"0"}))`。import先commit则revoke等待后拒绝；revoke先commit则import等待后拒绝；未知COMMIT时锁等待真实outcome，绝不能按timeout推断rollback。

AcquireStagingDatabaseCommitChallengeRequestV1的CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1必须逐字携带outcome row body、generation evidence与purpose；recovery branch还必须携带revoker的exact archived request bundle、revocation envelope与intent/recovery-application commit-proof bodies。provider challenge及generic Inspect保存的是尚未trusted-read的candidate preimage，challenge签发本身不锁定row body，也不证明transaction committed。若第二次PITR发生在StagingDatabaseCommitAttestationV1产生前并丢失outcome row，只有 `outcome_kind=capability_revocation_application`、target schema=StagingImportCapabilityRevocationApplicationV1且global latch/terminal consumer均absent时，caller才可先用candidate row count=0的fresh Inspect与§6.1 `fence_reason=staging_outcome_row_rerun`推进该semantic key generation，再以同一预分配revocation_application_id创建current body并取得新digest/new challenge；旧append先赢则只能exact restore，fence先赢则旧challenge永久stale。若丢失的是 `outcome_kind=import_application` / FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1，任一Fence reason都不授权该key，也绝不允许重新import；provider已经held后只能进入intent-backed staging recovery，取得signed recovery revocation并以revocation application收敛到Abort。database-incarnation attestor只有在trusted-read exact committed row后才签名，并在返回attestation前原子归档exact body/challenge/attestation；自该归档点起body才被锁定，后续PITR只能逐字恢复原transaction-local字段。恢复后旧challenge因Head/runtime/lineage仍可能stale，必须对同一locked outcome digest取得fresh archive_continuity challenge/attestation；different locked body、partial/tampered archive、未fence same-ID/different-body revocation rerun、对fresh import使用Fence或把historical body改写为current transaction固定拒绝。provider Abort已经terminal并清空staging tuple后不再开放本恢复，target保持aborted且只能换新activation。

DB application或revocation-application提交后，caller发送 AcquireStagingDatabaseCommitChallengeRequestV1；body字段恰为 `challenge_id`、`target_activation_id`、`outcome_kind`、`outcome_id`、`outcome_row_digest`、`commit_proof_purpose`、`archive_fence_count`、`archive_fences`、`archive_generation_evidence_set_digest`、`staging_exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`current_staging_exclusion_state`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`request_nonce`，outcome_kind只有import_application或capability_revocation_application，state只有held或held_recovery_abort_only。archive_fence_count必须为1，唯一item逐字等于outcome schema/semantic ID/current generation；generation evidence set按§6.1相同domain/字段重算。commit_proof_purpose初次terminal admission为external_admission；body已锁定且holder/recovery变化后为同一Release/Abort取得fresh proof时为archive_continuity，不能重复terminal effect或换body。

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 StagingDatabaseCommitChallengeV1使用相同字段并额外恰有 `request_digest`、`provider_challenge_nonce`、`challenge_sequence`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，最长2分钟且同ID/body exact retry；provider只对exact current held/abort-only Head、purpose与fresh signed generation evidence签发，rebind/recovery造成Head、lineage、candidate lease或generation变化后旧challenge固定失效。signer_role=`database_incarnation` 的 StagingDatabaseCommitAttestationV1 body字段恰为 `target_activation_id`、`outcome_kind`、`outcome_id`、`semantic_row_id`、`outcome_row_digest`、`commit_proof_purpose`、`archive_fence_count`、`archive_fences`、`archive_generation_evidence_set_digest`、`staging_exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`current_staging_exclusion_state`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`commit_challenge_digest`、`postgres_system_id`、`timeline`、`database_oid`、`database_name`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`incarnation_id`、`incarnation_public_key_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`runtime_instance_id`、`runtime_instance_generation`、`attestor_runtime_lease_digest`、`commit_lsn`、`provider_challenge_nonce`、`challenge_sequence`、`archive_sequence`、`previous_archive_entry_digest_or_null`、`attested_at`；semantic_row_id必须等于outcome_id。attestor自行trusted-read唯一committed outcome row、exact optional recovery intent/application与连续DB rebind/lineage chain，证明outcome旧runtime/identity是current exact ancestor；再按§6.1同一trusted WAL archive/decoder规则把该row version的inserting
