xid映射为原system/timeline的exact COMMIT/COMMIT PREPARED record end LSN。稍后的server WAL、flush、replay或observation point不能写入commit_lsn；exact record缺失或不可比较固定拒绝。attestor使用与DatabaseCommitAttestationV1相同的global body latch、challenge exact-retry、sequence/previous链与atomic archive-before-return规则，并在最终append CAS重新验证generation仍等于challenge；stale generation、wrong purpose或caller body/digest/LSN固定拒绝。

成功路径的 ReleaseFreshV7StagingExclusionRequestV1 body字段恰为 `terminal_transition_id`、`exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`current_staging_exclusion_state=held`、`target_activation_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null=null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null=null`、`fresh_restore_import_application_digest`、`staging_database_commit_challenge_digest`、`staging_database_commit_attestation_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`request_nonce`。signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 FreshV7StagingExclusionReleaseV1使用相同字段并额外恰有 `request_digest`、`result_phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed`、`result_provider_head_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`released_at`。任何recovery intent或lineage-changing rebind都使Release永久不适用。

abort路径的 AbortFreshV7StagingExclusionRequestV1字段恰为 `terminal_transition_id`、`exclusion_id`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`acquisition_locked_provider_head_digest`、`expected_current_held_provider_head_digest`、`current_staging_exclusion_state`、`target_activation_id`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_intent_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_recovery_application_digest_or_null`、`staging_import_capability_revocation_digest`、`revocation_application_digest`、`staging_database_commit_challenge_digest`、`staging_database_commit_attestation_digest`、`current_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`current_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`latest_runtime_rebind_result_digest_or_null`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`request_nonce`；FreshV7StagingExclusionAbortV1使用相同字段并额外恰有 `request_digest`、`result_phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed`、`result_provider_head_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`aborted_at`。provider在terminal mutation中原子比较exact current Head并消费challenge；Release只接受held且intent/recovery null，Abort接受held+intent/recovery null或held_recovery_abort_only+exact intent/recovery/revocation application。held_recovery_required不能直接Abort；必须先以committed intent完成Recover。rebind/recovery发生在challenge后时旧request固定Head/lineage mismatch，必须重取challenge/attestation。成功后清空Head staging四字段；同terminal ID/body exact retry，Release/Abort互斥，response loss只Inspect。依赖不可用、challenge过期或DB outcome未知时永久保持当前held/recovery state。

### 7.3 FreshTargetInventoryV1 与 staging evidence

只有provider exact Release response存在或Inspect确认后才能生成inventory；held、recovery-required、abort-only、Abort、recovery intent/application、缺Release或wrong application都固定拒绝。B11先取得新nonce/purpose=staging_import的pre proof，要求Head仍是fresh_v7_staging_closed、staging exclusion四字段全null、ordinary zero、serving null；随后按统一锁序在route仍关闭的单一locked DB snapshot中重验intent/capability/application/revocation/recovery=`0/1/1/0/0`并生成inventory，事务内不调用provider；最后取得新nonce post proof并要求stable Head/current identity/lineage/registration/rebind/runtime逐字等于pre。signer_role=`fresh_target_inventory_operator` 的 FreshTargetInventoryV1 body字段恰为 `target_activation_id`、`mode=fresh_restore_target`、`target_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_attestation_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`staging_exclusion_digest`、`staging_exclusion_release_digest`、`import_application_commit_attestation_digest`、`pre_database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`post_database_incarnation_proof_digest`、`normalized_catalog_digest`、`fresh_import_topology_projection_digest`、`table_inventory`、`forbidden_authority_state_zero_digest`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`import_application_digest`、`complete_node_set_digest`、`all_nodes_disabled_digest`、`all_identity_unauthorized_digest`、`all_health_unknown_digest`、`all_active_resume_pending_pointer_null_digest`、`provider_head_digest`、`provider_phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed`、`provider_ordinary_head_zero_digest`、`provider_serving_lease_absent_digest`、`database_route_closed_digest`、`restore_completion_barrier_absent_digest`、`readiness_reason=restore_incomplete`、`transaction_snapshot_digest`、`database_point`、`observed_at`。table_inventory采用§4.5 exact per-table item schema并扩展到全部target tables；projection digest必须等于application post-import digest。

provider_ordinary_head_zero_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.provider-ordinary-head-zero.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"latest_reserved_sequence":"0","latest_committed_sequence":"0","latest_reservation_digest_or_null":null,"latest_committed_operation_id_or_null":null,"latest_committed_receipt_digest_or_null":null,"latest_committed_database_point_or_null":null}))`；provider_serving_lease_absent_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.provider-serving-lease-absent.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"head_serving_lease_id_or_null":null,"head_serving_lease_generation_or_null":null,"head_serving_lease_digest_or_null":null,"proof_serving_lease_id_or_null":null,"proof_serving_lease_generation_or_null":null,"proof_serving_lease_digest_or_null":null,"proof_serving_lease_expires_at_or_null":null}))`。两者必须从绑定的stable Head/proof重算，不能由caller自报。

其余target derived digest也有固定preimage：forbidden_authority_state_zero_digest与application.forbidden_state_zero_digest都等于 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.forbidden-authority-state-zero.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(sorted-array-of-{table_name,row_count:"0"}))`，table names来自00007 fixed forbidden registry；all_nodes_disabled_digest、all_identity_unauthorized_digest、all_health_unknown_digest分别使用对应domain separator与按node ID排序的 `{node_id,state}` array；all_active_resume_pending_pointer_null_digest使用按node ID排序的 `{node_id,resume_operator_state_or_null:null,pending_operator_transition_or_null:null,active_pointer_set:[]}`；restore_completion_barrier_absent_digest固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.restore-completion-barrier-absent.v1") || 0x00 || JCS({"target_activation_id":...,"barrier_row_count":"0"}))`。所有array从同一locked target snapshot重算。

signer_role=`fresh_restore_evidence_operator` 的 FreshV7StagingEvidenceV1 body 字段恰为 `fresh_restore_requirement_digest`、`source_membership_retirement_digest`、`source_legacy_retirement_set_digest`、`source_post_seal_exact_cover_digest`、`source_archive_evidence_digest`、`epoch_evidence_digest`、`selected_genesis_epoch`、`target_activation_digest`、`target_completion_digest`、`target_release_digest`、`target_open_digest`、`target_database_identity_digest`、`database_timeline_lineage_chain_digest`、`target_database_incarnation_registration_digest`、`runtime_rebind_chain_digest`、`runtime_instance_binding_digest`、`import_manifest_digest`、`staging_import_capability_digest`、`import_application_digest`、`import_application_commit_attestation_digest`、`staging_exclusion_release_digest`、`fresh_target_inventory_digest`、`provider_head_digest`、`database_route_closed_digest`、`restore_completion_barrier_absent_digest`、`blocked_capabilities`、`statement=safe_staging_established_and_closed`、`issued_at`。

evidence生成前后必须按统一锁序重验同一 locked target snapshot、exact released provider Head、两份fresh incarnation proof、current identity/lineage、source retirement/post-seal exact cover、route closure与固定 restore_incomplete。任一held/recovery-required/abort-only/aborted exclusion、recovery intent/application、capability revocation、额外 authority/credential/serving/pointer row、provider reservation、节点启用、route reopen或 Head变化都拒绝签发。该 evidence只能支持“安全 staging 已建立且保持关闭”，不能支持 legacy upgrade、destructive restore或production serving完成。

manifest/capability/application中的identity/lineage/registration/runtime必须在import transaction时逐字相等。若import后postmaster restart，FreshTargetInventory/evidence可以使用same-identity current runtime descendant，但只接受§6.3 fresh_v7_staging_closed->fresh_v7_staging_closed、lease始终null且无recovery intent的完整provider-signed normal rebind chain；任一timeline-changing recovery、active phase或non-null lease固定拒绝。

### 7.4 当前硬阻塞

完成 destructive restore至少需要：

- node/operator/server CA轮换；
- C1.2 root/metadata/signer轮换；
- operator authorizer credential轮换；
- 每个 node 的新 authority_restore session、reenrollment与 stopped attestation；
- 完整 trust_bundle_publish 与 operator_authorizer_change durable workflow，或另行批准同等强度的 OOB协议。

前述两个 effect kind 在当前批准设计中仍固定 unsupported。因此本修订不把 fresh_restore_target 描述为当前可完成的 legacy upgrade。

## 8. Readiness、writer 与 guarded serving

### 8.1 exact readiness

CheckReady 的验证集合是封闭的，并按下列顺序 fail closed：

1. exact v7 catalog/guard registry、唯一runtime registration result、连续runtime rebind result/timeline lineage链与upgrade attempt、activation、completion、release digest chain；
2. current PostgreSQL system ID、database OID/name与genesis activation逐字段相等；activation/历史commit timeline只须为current受信lineage ancestor，live timeline/current identity必须等于current registration、DB/Provider Head与proof。逐份重算genesis lineage root及每个rebind link；missing/extra/gap/sibling、fork早于release或provider-accepted ordinary anchor、wrong system/OID/name/incarnation固定database_identity_mismatch；
3. 用两个verifier-owned single-use caller challenge取得的 DatabaseIncarnationProofV1、purpose/phase-specific exact lease evidence，以及从registration result到current registration的exact provider rebind+DB result/identity/lineage chain；empty_in_place还要求one-live provider serving lease generation/freshness，并把lease activation、selected genesis epoch/root逐字比较DB Head、Provider Head及genesis evidence；fresh_restore_target则要求serving fields全null并继续检查而不是提前返回lease unavailable；
4. 从activation的genesis policy到Head current policy的完整previous-digest chain、latest sequence、provider endpoint identity、accepted claim-v1 key-set与legacy revocation digest；
5. historical EnvironmentInventory/anchor-set/shutdown在prepared_at时签名有效且当时latest、其digest此后等于immutable preparation/barrier anchor；不得要求这些15分钟admission object在当前时间仍unexpired/latest；
6. mode、AuthorityGenesisEpochEvidenceV1、selected genesis epoch、provider transition-chain root与DB latest epoch application/resolution/terminal chain一致；active lease的activation、selected genesis epoch与genesis root必须和evidence及两种Head四方逐字相等。任何pending transition/recovery intent、provider pending/recovery state、missing exact recovery application或resolution mismatch固定epoch_transition_unresolved；
7. empty_in_place 的 provider profile=claim_v1、phase=active、Open digest与 database release attestation完全一致；
8. DB 中零 legacy_v6 fence与零 legacy authority-bearing/credential/serving/pointer/pending row；
9. repository按 provider Head 的 exact current_epoch查询 head，禁止 MAX(authority_epoch) safety fallback；
10. current epoch无sequence gap或unresolved intent/application/recovery；locked DB重算的reserved/committed sequence、latest reservation digest、operation ID、receipt digest与database point六字段ordinary projection必须与stable Provider Head逐字相等。current lease的两个sequence字段只作为签发prefix baseline，经exact Record/Reservation evidence证明是current projection的连续ancestor；terminal_application_catchup因prefix为空才要求lease两个sequence与两种Head相等。provider terminal领先DB只能经§4.6 exact transcript rehydrate；recovery application已提交但provider marker/受限lease未catchup仍固定provider_serving_lease_unavailable。ready前后stable ClaimV1ProviderHeadV1 body digest逐字相等；两个proof各自回显本次不同proof ID/caller nonce/request digest且fresh，并引用同一Head/current identity/lineage与mode-appropriate lease state，不要求proof envelope/time相等；
11. fresh_restore_target 必须验证 provider phase=fresh_v7_staging_closed、零普通 operation、route closed；若已有import application，还必须有exact commit attestation+Release且Head staging exclusion四字段全null，recovery intent count=0。held/recovery-required/abort-only/Abort/recovery intent/application/revocation固定拒绝inventory/evidence。随后因 destructive-restore completion barrier 缺失固定 restore_incomplete；不得为其发放 production lease。

有限 upgrade readiness reason 恰为 runtime_result_missing、protocol_attempt_missing、protocol_activation_missing、protocol_completion_missing、protocol_release_missing、provider_protocol_mismatch、provider_transition_incomplete、epoch_transition_unresolved、staging_exclusion_held、historical_admission_evidence_mismatch、credential_policy_mismatch、legacy_state_present、database_identity_mismatch、database_incarnation_mismatch、runtime_instance_mismatch、attestor_lease_unavailable、provider_serving_lease_unavailable、genesis_epoch_mismatch、authority_head_mismatch、source_seal_violated、restore_incomplete。内部 precedence 按上述验证顺序；客户端只接收无值 ErrAuthorityUnavailable，具体 reason只进入受控运维 evidence。

### 8.2 writer gate

RecordPending 要求 provider reservation携带 §6.2 的全部强制 claim-v1 fields并逐字段匹配current stable Head与fresh incarnation proof。所有普通领域 writer在同一事务验证current identity/lineage、runtime registration/rebind result、attempt/activation/completion/release、resolved epoch chain、exact locked fence、credential policy、incarnation及current attestor/provider lease。provider处于registered_pending_genesis、任一genesis closed phase、active_lease_revoked、fresh_v7_staging_closed，或有pending epoch/recovery/rebind/staging exclusion、barrier不完整、restore_incomplete、受限/过期lease、identity/lineage/profile不匹配时零写入。唯一例外是§5.5/§7.2 exact held-exclusion staging-import branch，它要求recovery intent absent且不能调用RecordPending或写authority state。

旧 binary因缺少 profile/activation ID，即使恢复数据库连接也会被 trigger拒绝。

### 8.3 guarded serving

Coordinator 是 BoundAuthorityReadSource 的唯一生产工厂；factory不接受另一个 pool/repository参数。Serving reader只能消费该 source并返回 facts，不承担 B03 endpoint authorization。

每次 certificate/desired read先取得fresh DatabaseIncarnationProofV1与stable Head digest，再使用同一物理 PostgreSQL connection：

1. DB readiness precheck；
2. exact domain query；
3. DB readiness postcheck；
4. 重读stable provider Head与第二个fresh proof，要求Head digest、runtime binding、provider lease ID/generation与epoch transition-chain equality。

查询要求领域 fence的 profile、activation ID、incarnation registration和epoch等于本次 ready snapshot，并验证 exact applied resolution、pointer、identity与全部 dependency。任一步失败、provider fresh challenge失败或lease在postcheck前过期都清空临时 facts。

错误优先级固定：

~~~text
cancellation > authority unavailable > domain not found/conflict
~~~

Legacy source因 source seal且没有 activation/completion/release而永久不能创建 production reader、listener或signer。fresh restore target因restore_incomplete与provider staging-closed同样不能创建。

## 9. PITR、故障与 downgrade

### 9.1 PITR

- 恢复到 attempt前：production intent、provider policy/registration precursor或latest inventory anchor set可能领先；production永不允许Down，disposable fixture也只有在全部provider namespace先永久down_retired后才能进入registered Go Down；
- provider registration或immediate rebind领先于DB result：Inspect exact request/bundle/response并以post-provider Head补写连续result；timeline-changing rebind还必须保存exact lineage envelope。scope=epoch_transition_recovery的deferred suffix明确排除在本规则外：逐份result保持absent，直到§4.6 restricted transaction按ordinal原子采用。旧DB Head与provider Head mismatch期间零普通/genesis下一步；
- 普通crash未丢DB immutable row时，epoch intent/application/resolution/provider terminal/DB terminal-application任一侧领先只按原transition/resolution/cancellation/terminal ID Inspect/retry；provider pending永不因TTL取消。provider resolved领先DB时创建尚未被provider消费的resolved terminal application；provider cancelled领先DB时以同一transaction创建尚未被消费的rebind result+cancellation+terminal application；DB缺terminal application或两种Head terminal chain不等时零普通mutation；
- 真正PITR丢失provider已固定的intent/application/resolution或terminal group时，Inspect envelope本身不够：先提交recovery intent；holder/timeline改变再以same intent/suffix的gap attestation逐次追加deferred epoch_transition_recovery rebind，最终commit proof绑定完整suffix。provider返回exact signed transcript后，restricted DB transaction逐字恢复provider-consumed historical body，按terminal edge与ordinal连续写入全部result并最后追加current recovery application；unconsumed terminal group才可用current transaction创建。用新txid/snapshot/time重算已锚定resolution、缺原preimage/commit anchor、suffix缺失/乱序、非exact common ancestor或普通authority history也落后全部固定拒绝；
- 恢复到 attempt后、activation前：Inspect exact preparation，fail closed；
- 恢复到 activation后、provider completion前：fail closed并恢复 exact completion；
- provider completion后、DB completion缺失：Inspect provider并恢复相同 completion，provider仍拒绝普通operation；
- DB completion后、provider release preparation前后：exact retry/Inspect release，provider仍拒绝普通operation；
- DB release后、Open前：exact retry Open；Open前DB barrier已完整；
- Open后 response loss：按open ID Inspect exact ClaimV1GenesisOpenV1并重读stable Head，不创建第二activation/lease；
- 恢复到普通 fence/domain commitment前：normal authority head/database point检查阻断；
- attempt/activation/completion/release被删除、回放、篡改或来自不同 database identity：fixed unavailable；
- source任一environment/provider retirement领先于local seal/archive：tombstone保持永久，只能完成剩余exact-cover member；回放到retirement前的DB也不能恢复route/provider authority；
- staging intent/capability/application/revocation/recovery与provider held/required/abort-only任一组合都从immutable apply ID、五表matrix与current Head重判：application存在且intent absent可在same-identity held-preserving rebind后重取challenge/attestation并Release；capability存在、intent/application absent可normal revoke并Abort；provider held但PITR丢capability或capability runtime成为ancestor时，只能按可选lineage recovery rebind→durable recovery intent+commit proof→provider CAS→DB intent/capability zero-or-exact-one rehydrate+recovery application→recovery revocation→fresh challenge/attestation→Abort。DB不可用或COMMIT未知保持当前provider state，绝不按admission expiry自动解锁；
- PostgreSQL physical PITR要接受写入必须promotion；new timeline不再与历史activation raw-equal，而必须由§4.2外部rollback-resistant history ledger证明为anchor之后的连续descendant。same-cluster clone因OID/name mismatch拒绝；physical clone/PITR即使复制全部row、system ID、旧timeline与deployment ID，也不能取得原holder或伪造lineage。只有same-incarnation的epoch terminal recovery或fresh staging ordinary-zero held recovery可以推进identity/lineage；前者恢复exact DB suffix并mandatory lease catchup，后者只产生required→abort-only且永不能import/Release/serve。fork早于release/ordinary anchor、sibling/cross-system/incarnation或lineage gap固定拒绝。

### 9.2 concurrency

Up 与 legacy runtime 的竞争先由外部 shutdown barrier解决：旧 route/credential/session 未全部撤销时Up工具固定拒绝；迁移锁等待期间任何 revoke/disable 失败也固定拒绝，而不是允许旧 listener继续serve。

post-Up legacy writer与 activation 的真实数据库锁竞争只能有两个结果：

- legacy writer先提交，empty inventory改变，activation拒绝并分类为 fresh_restore_required；
- activation先提交，legacy writer因profile/activation guard拒绝。

provider 在 genesis_prepared、genesis_completed_pending_release与genesis_release_prepared期间和普通 Reserve/Finalize/Abort/epoch transition的并发只允许 transition先固定拒绝普通operation，或旧operation先改变history使genesis固定拒绝。Open必须在DB release commit attestation后；one-live lease竞争只允许一个incarnation成功。

staging capability registration、recovery intent、import、revocation与rehydrate使用两条DB连接及§7.2同一固定锁序；registration先赢时intent只能观察exact-one，intent先赢时迟到registration拒绝；import先锁并commit时revocation等待后拒绝，revocation先commit时import等待后拒绝，import rollback后revocation可继续。provider held/required/abort-only期间Release/Abort按同exclusion有限矩阵互斥；unknown COMMIT、challenge过期或terminal response loss都不能触发第二import、第二recovery/terminal transition或自动unlock。

测试使用两条连接与可观测 lock-wait/provider barrier seam，不使用 sleep判定赢家。

### 9.3 downgrade

普通Goose CLI未注册00007，raw Down恒定失败。disposable fixture首先冻结完整inventory membership。signer_role=`provider_downgrade_retirement_admin` 的 RetireEnvironmentInventoryMembershipRequestV1 body字段恰为 `membership_retirement_id`、`installation_id`、`installation_kind=disposable_fixture`、`migration_latch_digest`、`database_identity_digest`、`release_scope`、`final_environment_inventory_digest`、`final_inventory_sequence`、`final_environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`environment_member_set_digest`、`environment_count`、`authorization_nonce`、`request_nonce`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过5分钟。signer_role=`release_deployment_operator` 的 EnvironmentInventoryMembershipRetirementV1 response使用相同字段并额外恰有 `request_digest`、`membership_retirement_tombstone_id`、`phase=inventory_membership_retired`、`retired_at`。release inventory authority在rollback-resistant域永久拒绝该release_scope的新inventory sequence/member；每个provider retirement也必须验证并保存同一tombstone。旧anchor set、非latest sequence、production latch或后续成员变化固定拒绝。

B11为每个provider预生成retirement ID/nonce/body，并取得独立 signer_role=`provider_downgrade_retirement_admin` 的 ProviderProtocolDowngradeRetirementAuthorizationV1；body字段恰为 `authorization_id`、`retirement_id`、`installation_id`、`installation_kind=disposable_fixture`、`migration_latch_digest`、`database_identity_digest`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`environment_inventory_membership_retirement_digest`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`retirement_member_set_digest`、`member_count`、`authorization_scope=permanent_disposable_namespace_retirement`、`authorization_nonce`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过5分钟。该trust policy独立于claim-v1 credential policy、B11 DB downgrade authorizer与数据库role；unauthenticated、wrong role/root/policy或cross-provider/scope replay固定拒绝。首次mutation必须unexpired，成功后的同ID/body在过期后仍返回stored response。

RetireProviderProtocolForDowngradeRequestV1 body字段恰为 `retirement_id`、`retirement_nonce`、`provider_downgrade_retirement_authorization_digest`、`environment_inventory_membership_retirement_digest`、`installation_id`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`member_count`、`members`、`retirement_member_set_digest`、`request_nonce`。members按environment inventory record顺序排列，每项字段恰为 `environment_record_digest`、`environment_attestation_digest`、`deployment_id`、`postgres_system_id`、`timeline`、`database_oid`、`database_name`、`database_identity_digest`、`environment_instance_generation`、`provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`provider_namespace`、`provider_profile`；它必须exact等于完整final inventory中属于该provider的全部records，而不是distinct namespace字符串或任意单值deployment/database。

Down membership request的 `environment_member_set_digest` 使用与§4.3相同的environment-record digest preimage；每个provider request/auth的 `retirement_member_set_digest` 固定为 `SHA-256(ASCII("talenro.c12.provider-protocol-retirement-member-set.v1") || 0x00 || JCS(exact-members-in-final-inventory-order))`。provider与set verifier都从final inventory重算，不能由caller给count/digest。

signer_role=`claim_v1_provider_history_auditor` 的 ProviderProtocolHistoryZeroProjectionV1 body字段恰为 `provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`namespace`、`observed_profiles`、`unknown_profile_count=0`、`unknown_record_count=0`、`legacy_reservation_count=0`、`legacy_terminal_count=0`、`legacy_epoch_transition_count=0`、`legacy_cutover_count=0`、`claim_v1_credential_policy_count=0`、`claim_v1_accepted_key_mutation_count=0`、`incarnation_registration_count=0`、`genesis_preparation_count=0`、`genesis_completion_count=0`、`genesis_release_preparation_count=0`、`genesis_open_count=0`、`claim_v1_reservation_count=0`、`claim_v1_terminal_count=0`、`claim_v1_epoch_transition_count=0`、`claim_v1_epoch_recovery_count=0`、`serving_lease_event_count=0`、`runtime_rebind_count=0`、`timeline_lineage_event_count=0`、`staging_exclusion_count=0`、`staging_recovery_count=0`、`source_retirement_count=0`、`genesis_authorizing_control_count=0`、`other_mutation_count=0`、`provider_history_high_water=0`、`observed_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过2分钟。observed_profiles恰为按bytewise排序的`["claim_v1","legacy_v6"]`；fixed provider ledger manifest必须分类全部mutation，包括lineage、recovery intent consumption、exact transcript export与terminal-application catchup，未知profile/category即拒绝。inventory-anchor/read-only bookkeeping可使control sequence非零，但不能使任一authority/history counter非零。retirement首次mutation必须验证fresh signed projection envelope；CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1按external_signed_envelope携带它，不允许unsigned body或database_immutable_body类型。

全部projection为零后provider在一个不可逆mutation发布 signer_role=`claim_v1_provider` 的 ProviderProtocolDowngradeRetirementV1；body字段恰为request全部字段加 `request_digest`、`provider_control_sequence`、`namespace_states`、`phase=down_retired`、`retired_at`。namespace_states按namespace排序，每项字段恰为 `namespace`、`environment_record_digests`、`database_identity_digests`、`history_zero_projection_digest`、`pre_retirement_control_sequence`、`provider_history_high_water=0`、`retirement_tombstone_id`，两个数组从request members exact派生。tombstone永久拒绝该namespace的credential policy、registration、Prepare/Complete/Release/Open、Reserve/Finalize/Abort、epoch/cutover、lease/catchup、rebind/timeline-lineage、epoch recovery、source-retirement与staging recovery APIs；没有TTL、cancel或复活路径，只能换新namespace。相同retirement ID/body exact retry，字段变化conflict。

ProviderProtocolDowngradeRetirementSetV1 body字段恰为 `installation_id`、`release_scope`、`environment_inventory_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`environment_inventory_membership_retirement_digest`、`provider_count`、`retirements`、`retired_member_count`、`retired_members`、`created_at`。retirements按provider identity/endpoint排序，每项字段恰为 `provider_identity_digest`、`provider_endpoint_identity_digest`、`provider_protocol_downgrade_retirement_digest`；retired_members使用request exact member schema并按inventory顺序排列。set必须重建final inventory并exact覆盖全部record/provider/namespace，无遗漏、extra或alias；CanonicalEvidenceBundleV1携带membership retirement、admin authorizations、zero projections与全部retirement envelopes。

全部provider永久retired后，authoritative migration executor才调用registered 00007 Down。Goose创建`*sql.Tx`并原样传入context；migration从context取得已由B01验证的exact retirement set，不在transaction内调用provider。dedicated `nodecontrol_migration_downgrader` role取得固定清单全部ACCESS EXCLUSIVE lock。PristineDowngradeInventoryV1 body字段恰为 `installation_id`、`installation_kind=disposable_fixture`、`database_identity_digest`、`migration_latch_digest`、`current_catalog_digest`、`manifest_id`、`manifest_digest`、`stable_table_count`、`stable_table_inventory`、`control_table_count=2`、`non_control_protocol_row_count=0`、`migration_latch_count=1`、`downgrade_authorization_count=0`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_nonce`、`stage=pre_authorization`、`observed_at`。stable inventory按fully-qualified table排序并明确排除migration-latch与downgrade-authorization两张control table；它证明R0/R1/C/A0/A1、19条domain、credential/serving/pointer/pending/recovery、production intent、runtime registration/rebind result、attempt、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal/recovery intent/prefix decision/recovery application、source retirement/seal/requirement、activation/completion/release、staging capability/revocation/application/recovery intent/recovery application与claim-v1 row全部为零。latch必须恰一行且digest/fixture kind exact，authorization必须零；`non_control_protocol_row_count`只统计前述fixed forbidden registry。

同一Goose transaction内，B11 signer发布 signer_role=`authority_protocol_downgrade_authorizer` 的 AuthorityProtocolDowngradeAuthorizationV1；body字段恰为 `authorization_id`、`installation_id`、`migration_latch_digest`、`database_identity_digest`、`migration_version=7`、`current_catalog_digest`、`pristine_downgrade_inventory_digest`、`provider_protocol_downgrade_retirement_set_digest`、`environment_inventory_anchor_set_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_nonce`、`authorization_scope=down_00007_only`、`issued_at`、`expires_at`，有效窗口不超过2分钟。restricted function重验dedicated role、current txid、locks/identity/catalog、stored pre-auth inventory/latch与exact typed preimages后插入唯一authorization。PristineDowngradeAuthorizedStateV1 body字段恰为 `pristine_downgrade_inventory_digest`、`migration_latch_digest`、`downgrade_authorization_digest`、`provider_protocol_downgrade_retirement_set_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_nonce`、`migration_latch_count=1`、`downgrade_authorization_count=1`、`non_control_protocol_row_count=0`、`stage=authorization_inserted`、`observed_at`；它验证stored pre-auth inventory，不尝试把auth=1重构成原digest。

Down三阶段matrix固定为：pre_authorization时latch/auth=`1/0`且只允许插入exact auth；authorization_inserted时=`1/1`且只允许embedded guard；guard_consumed时=`0/0`且只允许同一Go callback继续DDL/version update。guard以单个CTE锁定、重验并`DELETE ... RETURNING` exact authorization+latch，再扫描fixed forbidden registry。PristineDowngradeConsumeReceiptV1 body字段恰为 `pristine_downgrade_inventory_digest`、`authorized_state_digest`、`consumed_migration_latch_digest`、`consumed_downgrade_authorization_digest`、`database_transaction_id`、`transaction_nonce`、`post_consume_migration_latch_count=0`、`post_consume_downgrade_authorization_count=0`、`non_control_protocol_row_count=0`、`stage=guard_consumed`、`consumed_at`。extra latch/auth、cross-tx、wrong nonce、漏表或consume前并发行均固定拒绝。后续DDL与Goose version更新同transaction；任一失败整体回滚为`1/0`，成功则两control tables/schema/version原子消失。commit response loss先检查Goose version/catalog/latch：已提交不得重跑，未提交用永久retirement set开启新transaction/auth。Down永远不恢复route/credential或授权v6 serving。

## 10. 计划所有权

### B01 / Plan 01

- 全部canonical body/envelope/evidence bundle、DatabaseAuthorityRebindGapAttestationV1、DatabaseAuthorityPostRecoveryRebindAttestationV1、provider genesis/epoch application-resolution-cancellation-terminal/exact-recovery/prefix-decision/deferred-suffix/lease descendant+terminal-catchup/staging hold+intent-recovery/commit-archive/source+Down retirement contract与golden vectors；
- generic commit related-row-set、commit purpose/generation-evidence、atomic multi-key Fence request/response、epoch recovery application+apply-decision全局body-latch closure、Inspect related-only/live-candidate 0/1/candidate echo/continuity-history与staging exact-existing/missing-suffix两种机械evidence mode的schema、registry、negative golden；
- Environment/Shutdown/DatabaseIncarnation/timeline lineage/runtime registration+rebind result/absence DAG/Epoch/EmptyDatabase verifier；
- 00006 parser annotations和registered Go 00007全部additive tables/ACL/guard registry，包括migration latch/production intent、downgrade authorization、runtime results、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal/recovery intent+prefix decision+application、database source retirement、indeterminate/full seal、staging capability/revocation/application/recovery intent+application；
- attempt/source retirement+seal与activation/completion/release barrier、Down三阶段/staging共同锁序security-definer verifier；
- exact-epoch repository/readiness；
- Coordinator-owned BoundAuthorityReadSource；
- empty-in-place、legacy classification、crash/PITR tests。

### B02 / Plan 02

- FreshRestoreImportManifestV1 consumer；在B01创建的table/function内消费single-use staging capability并写application row，recovery intent存在即拒绝import；
- disabled node skeleton和state-domain恢复处理；
- 不复制legacy pointer、generation、resume intent或signing result。

### B03 / Plan 03

- production provider完整genesis/epoch prepare+resolve-vs-cancel terminal CAS/Inspect/exact recovery transcript/Head adapter、credential policy、incarnation/runtime-holder与rollback-resistant timeline-history/DatabaseAuthorityRebindGap/PostRecovery attestor、trusted DB read-back/WAL decoder/commit archive generation fence/Inspect（含global primary+related latches、sorted multi-key CAS、candidate echo与continuity history）、application-proof first-consumer/history-tail CAS、pre-catchup与post-catchup-marker-cleared applied rebind、provider lease renew/revoke/rebinding-registration/terminal-application catchup、durable staging hold/required/abort-only recovery/challenge/Release/Abort、source membership+retirement及permanent downgrade retirement；
- identity、certificate与trust restore consumer；
- runtime composition、listener/signer gate；
- guarded certificate/desired facts的authorization消费。

### B10 / Plan 08

- canonical C1.2 spec-set sole builder与validator；
- 本 amendment进入spec set后唯一计算新的 SpecDigest。

### B11 / Plan 09

- 唯一生产upgrade/restore/Down orchestrator与registered migration/shutdown runbook；预分配全部recovery/terminal/suffix/prefix-decision/fence-attempt ID，签发source/downgrade/rebind authorization，编排epoch terminal→recovery intent→可选deferred rebind suffix→suffix-bound commit proof→provider transcript→apply-vs-replacement prefix decision→必要时先fence旧unattested generation→DB exact rehydrate/application→每个candidate fresh proof→可选pre/post-catchup applied rebind→lease catchup，以及staging capability/intent/recovery-application commit-proof/fence/archive Inspect/revocation/recovery、FreshTargetInventory与staging evidence；外部调用不得发生在DB transaction内；
- LegacySourceRetirementSet/PostSealExactCover/ArchiveEvidence、FreshTargetInventory与FreshV7StagingEvidence生产生成/签署/验证；
- provider/DB/PITR/destructive-restore conformance；
- completion validator不得把 fresh_v7_staging_closed当作legacy upgrade完成。

B01拥有canonical schema、digest/signature/lineage helper、全部00007物理表/trigger/function与低层DB evidence input，包括generic related-row-set、Fence/generation/purpose/application-history wire fields与staging evidence-mode/candidate-tuple verifier；B02只拥有import consumer，不创建schema；B03拥有生产provider/incarnation/runtime/timeline attestor、PostgreSQL/data-directory/VM backup domain之外的trusted WAL decoder、rollback-resistant commit-attestation global latch/related edge/primary stream/Fence/Inspect与lease/recovery adapter及application-proof first-consumer/history-tail CAS；B11只编排Fence、challenge、Inspect与consumer bundle，不直接写archive，并唯一签发downgrade/staging capability、recovery intent、epoch application proof与staging evidence。B10只拥有SpecDigest，不生成upgrade/staging/recovery evidence。

## 11. TDD 与验收矩阵

### 11.1 catalog 与 legacy compatibility

- R0/R1/C/A0/A1 分别从真实 v6 catalog执行00007 Up；
- 逐行证明只增加legacy_v6分类，claim/commitment/resolution/evidence仍为空；
- legacy aborted不因新constraint阻断migration；
- 五种shape在source seal后分别拒绝fence及全部domain/credential/serving/pointer mutation；
- 00006每个PL/pgSQL function恰有一个Goose statement block；
- registered 00007 Up原子生成down_locked migration latch，production还生成upgrade intent；未注册普通Goose CLI、missing/expired/cross-transaction authorization、普通SQL/role直接伪造row全部固定失败；
- disposable pristine fixture取得latest membership retirement与all-provider永久downgrade retirement、在Goose-owned transaction通过`1/0 -> 1/1 -> 0/0` matrix consume latch后，pinned registered-Go up -> down-to 5 -> up catalog digest一致；
- production intent、R0/R1/C/A0/A1、runtime registration/rebind result、indeterminate/full source retirement/seal、fresh requirement、attempt、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal/recovery intent/prefix decision/recovery application、prepared-only crash、activation/completion/release、staging capability/revocation/application/recovery intent/recovery application与claim-v1 row逐项使Down固定失败；policy/registration precursor后但attempt前crash同样不能retire/Down；新增的epoch recovery intent/prefix-decision/application与staging recovery-intent四表及既有staging recovery-application表的ACL、FK、INSERT-only、non-null prefix UNIQUE/CHECK、fixed registry/drop order逐项catalog验证；
- downgrade membership old/nonlatest/可新增member、unauthenticated/wrong admin key/root/policy、cross-provider/scope、DB空但provider任一legacy reservation/terminal/epoch/recovery/timeline-lineage/staging/cutover或unknown profile/history counter为1全部拒绝；fixed zero-projection每个counter有单独negative；
- “provider retirement -> 尝试Prepare/注册/policy/source-retirement/timeline-rebind/epoch-recovery/staging-recovery/catchup mutation”因永久tombstone固定失败；transaction跨任意fake-clock expiry仍不能重新开放provider，任一provider/member retirement缺失使set拒绝；
- Down pre-auth/authorized/consumed每个非法latch/auth组合、extra row、cross-tx、wrong nonce、fixed registry漏表、authorization insert/consume/DDL/version/commit response各crash seam逐项验证；rollback恢复`1/0`，成功后schema/version/control rows原子消失；
- migration lock与并发legacy session/revoke/disable使用可观测barrier证明Up前shutdown，而非sleep。

### 11.2 empty proof

- DB空/provider空且identity/nonce/catalog完全匹配；
- DB空/provider非空；
- provider空/DB非空；
- provider namespace错误、deployment错误、nonce重放、stale signed inventory replay；
- environment_count>1即使target DB/provider为零也不能走singular empty proof，必须fresh；
- unknown table/column/FK/trigger；
- environment inventory/environment attestation wrong signer/key/policy/trust root、过期、截断、重复/遗漏environment、alias namespace、sequence rollback与digest tamper；multi-provider anchor set或LegacyRuntimeShutdownSet缺失/重复/错endpoint/漏environment固定拒绝；
- 旧provider credential逐一对Reserve/Finalize/Abort/epoch transition/genesis APIs固定拒绝；endpoint identity、policy version、accepted-key-set或revocation digest mutation固定拒绝；
- attempt/activation两次observation snapshot不同但stable projection相等时成功；protocol row变化不改变projection，任一legacy/domain row变化使activation失败；
- ProviderNamespaceAbsenceV1在epoch evidence前可构造、Prepare原子consume；head/control-sequence变化或proof expiry固定拒绝，证明依赖DAG无cycle；
- same-cluster template clone因OID/name拒绝；同宿主attestor上的两个physical data directory/postmaster只有一个取得runtime binding；old holder失效后PITR clone仍因head/WAL continuity、缺operator rebind authorization或缺受信timeline lineage拒绝；lineage 1与64 links成功，65、gap、sibling、fork早于anchor、wrong system/OID/name/incarnation/history ledger/holder generation逐项拒绝；timeline_fork_cut前缀exact成功，cut前missing row、cut后different row、missing body commit point等于/早于cut及point不可比较逐项拒绝；
- database identity四字段preimage、三种legacy source identity domain与kind→evidence schema matrix逐项golden；tuple/schema/role、provider epoch omission、deployment manifest rollback、maximum mutation与uint64 overflow固定拒绝；
- empty mode source字段非null/target字段错误、selected epoch不等于floor；fresh mode retirement/post-seal/source字段缺失、selected epoch小于等于legacy maximum、expected member缺失、provider/DB/manifest observation splice固定拒绝；
- source destructive action前必须先取得permanent membership tombstone；旧/nonlatest inventory、member set/action plan/ID/nonce mismatch及tombstone后任何新inventory sequence/deployment/provider member固定拒绝；
- membership首次mutation前authorization/shutdown expiry拒绝；membership已terminal后跨任意fake-clock expiry可按原action plan exact完成剩余member，换authorization、增删member或重做已terminal member固定拒绝；
- discovery与封存扫描之间出现writer；
- provider preparation response loss与exact Inspect；CanonicalEvidenceBundle missing/extra/preimage mismatch固定拒绝。

### 11.3 activation crash matrix

分别在以下点注入crash/response loss：

- provider registration前后、DB registration result commit前后、registered_pending_genesis restart/rebind与DB rebind result前后；
- durable attempt commit与provider preparation前后；
- DB activation commit前后；
- activation commit challenge、attestor trusted read与attestation前后；
- provider completion mutation前后；
- DB completion commit/challenge/attestation前后；
- provider release preparation前后；
- DB release commit/challenge/attestation前后；
- provider Open mutation/response前后。
- epoch intent、provider prepare CAS/response、DB application+resolution transaction、commit challenge/attestation、Resolve-vs-cancellation-rebind CAS两种赢家、provider terminal response、DB resolved terminal application及cancelled三row transaction的每一点；cancel后旧Prepare/Resolve replay与普通terminal response loss逐项恢复；
- 真正PITR分别落在intent前、application/resolution pair前、provider terminal后、DB terminal group后；所有入口验证recovery intent严格早于任何recovery-only rebind，same-holder count=0以及deferred suffix count=1/64两条入口、每个gap attestation/authorization/request/registration/response、intent与replacement decision commit challenge/attestation、provider exact transcript、restricted historical rehydrate、same-transaction apply decision/recovery application与terminal catchup每点注入crash/response loss。provider-consumed original txid/snapshot/time逐字恢复成功，用current transaction重算固定拒绝；unconsumed terminal group只允许current tx创建；
- epoch recovery exact-zero/exact-one intent/application/resolution组合、resolved与cancelled两种exact-set逐项验证；cancelled先在同transaction恢复R1+cancellation+terminal，resolved直接从gap anchor起步，再按ordinal原子写deferred results与application。相同non-null prefix key上apply-vs-replacement双连接race只允许一个commit，包括count=0/tail=null；直接nullable UNIQUE导致双赢的fixture必须失败。apply允许count=0/64，replacement只允许0..63且next=count+1。replacement decision commit后candidate A在provider CAS前死亡时，A的gap/challenge/authorization作废，同一decision以fresh candidate B、fresh gap/challenge/attestation成功；同一proof candidate mutation拒绝，B CAS成功后A及其他alternate proofs全部stale。response loss按winning proof Inspect/exact retry，不能退回apply；旧application transaction与replacement provider CAS交错时由DB unique winner使一侧整体回滚；
- application单向引用apply decision成功；缺失/wrong decision digest、伪造decision反向application字段、transaction ID/snapshot/database point不同固定拒绝。application attestation以sorted two-key CAS原子锁application primary+decision related global latch；replacement-decision primary与application-related竞争同一decision key只能一方完整提交，败方无partial latch/owner edge/entry。same application+different related body/set永久conflict，两个application key竞争同一exact decision latch只能一方取得唯一related owner，另一方永久conflict；related-only Inspect返回locked body/positive generation/零entry/head且不得独立充当commit或fork-cut proof，零owner、multiple owner、owner反查不一致与缺latch/body/related-set digest固定fail closed；
- old append与Fence使用双连接barrier覆盖两种赢家：append先赢则Fence零副作用并只能exact restore；Fence先赢则旧generation即使已trusted-read/签名也不能append且sequence不增长。application pair两key只能同时推进，staging/generic单key严格推进；response loss exact retry不二次递增。四种reason及staging outcome两种revocation submatrix（normal Abort与recovery Abort）逐项做positive golden；FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1在任一Fence reason下均做negative golden。把reason/schema/key/activation/recovery/prefix/exclusion/preallocated semantic ID、evidence role/schema/digest/cardinality/order与authorization evidence逐字段交叉替换；missing/extra/duplicate role、caller伪造journal bytes、trusted journal seal/rollback、journal digest zero/multiple lookup、lookup-hint mutation、same record跨context replay、duplicate reservation record、provider request/response或Head splice、unknown key、fixed registry其他key与wrong reason全部拒绝。epoch subject-slot golden覆盖pair/replacement同prefix同decision ID复用；same prefix第二decision ID、换reason/key set绕过、same decision ID跨prefix及slot/record非原子crash全部拒绝。对fence_attempt_key_digest完整JCS每个journal/context/key/stable-holder字段逐项mutation；same holder首次成功后即使用fresh Head/evidence、advanced generation或同binding/ID/generation的合法descendant lease再次Fence仍命中同once-key并零推进拒绝，严格更高runtime generation的新holder按新once-key可成功。并发测试证明attestation实现只按global latches→forward/reverse owner→primary stream→exact-retry、Fence mutation只按sorted global latches→stable once-row→response exact-retry取锁；禁止primary后再取edge。未fence same-ID/different-body、stale/mixed generation、partial key advance、orphan once-row/response、把Fence当commit proof、application/replacement反向锁序与每个latch/edge/sign/entry/head/generation/once-row/response commit seam crash逐项拒绝或只观察全无/完整提交；
- apply commit后在application challenge前、challenge后、archive commit-before-return、fresh matched Inspect、first-consumer CAS前后及applied-result前后逐点crash。direct catchup与首个applied rebind绑定同pre-Head/fresh proof竞争first consumer只允许一个；每次后继rebind/catchup必须在本candidate取得新attestation，history tail/previous/result event digest逐项直连，旧challenge冒充current、选择first consumer/任意ancestor/最大sequence或pending result未reconcile固定拒绝。第二次PITR在proof未归档且未消费时先atomic two-key Fence再用same semantic IDs重跑current pair；archive已锁定时逐字恢复original paired bodies/transaction metadata；provider已消费时恢复全部fresh proofs、applied results与唯一catchup。pre_catchup连续holder death、direct catchup first consumer、catchup后marker-cleared PITR、post_catchup marker-cleared rebind、response成功但result缺失再死亡逐点成功；每个已被下一applied rebind消费却被PITR丢失的D_i只用next-winning PostRecovery bundle恢复，每个被catchup first消费的D_i只用winning Renew Head attestation恢复，unconsumed tail只走dedicated reconcile。D_i point等于/早于cut、wrong result/body/previous tail、unused/failed/alternate-candidate PostRecovery proof、later而非first consumer proof、两类witness同时present或用later attestor observation point替代D_i.recorded_database_point逐项拒绝。marker被重建、第二catchup、history gap/extra/duplicate、catchup后later ordinary/policy/epoch/staging mutation固定fail closed。direct write R2 over missing R1、wrong previous result/runtime chain、suffix missing/reordered/duplicate/extra/cross-ID、decision/proof missing/extra/cross-prefix、wrong non-cancellation terminal edge、live old holder、wrong lineage、provider tail与DB tail不等、provider声称unconsumed但ledger已有consumer、missing first consumer、同ID不同body、partial pair/triple、wrong outcome/namespace/activation、DB non-epoch ordinary anchor落后全部固定拒绝；

每个分支从durable attempt恢复preparation/completion/release/open ID并只能exact retry同一activation。provider open_nonce先于DB release、Open digest后于release commit可独立计算；golden证明无self/fixed-point reference。Open前每个phase都证明普通Reserve/Finalize/Abort/epoch transition固定拒绝，DB release commit前provider绝不active；全程零writer/reader/listener/signer。

- Head从registered_pending_genesis起每个progressive phase逐项验证present/null、genesis/current identity、lineage、pending epoch/recovery、effective/terminal chain、staging held/recovery-required/abort-only与ordinary-head zero rules；DB Head的runtime与effective/terminal epoch分别只从continuous runtime result与epoch terminal-application链推进，recovery application不推进这些链但会清除pending recovery projection；普通intent/application/resolution本身不推进effective Head；
- provider rebind原子创建DatabaseIncarnationRebindingRegistrationV1；new registration request/previous/runtime/identity/lineage/sequence任一mutation、response丢失重放、registration↔response伪固定点及DB result缺失全部逐项拒绝或exact恢复；normal/cancellation/held-preserve携带lineage或recovery scope改变identity固定拒绝；
- attestor runtime lease initial/renew与provider serving lease initial/renew/revoke在mutation/response-loss/crash每点exact retry；Open/rebind/renew逐字copy-through activation、selected genesis epoch/root，三个字段逐一mutation、cross-activation replay均拒绝。expired或identity/lineage/policy/epoch/terminal-context落后的serving lease普通proof失败但rollover proof成功，n→n+1及provider outage跨n→n+k完整1..64 descendant chain可恢复，gap、>64、错误holder/generation拒绝；
- serving renewal ID等于request除ID外完整JCS的domain hash；fresh proof/DB Head attestation、ordinary closed prefix或activation/genesis/identity/lineage/policy/epoch/terminal context变化产生新ID。lease签发后有合法completed ordinary history时，lease sequence baseline可落后current Head但exact prefix能重算两种Head完整六字段并继续same-context/rotation rollover；相同sequence却错误reservation/operation/receipt/database-point、prefix gap/extra、context mutation后的ordinary record、missing/extra policy/epoch/terminal evidence、pending epoch/recovery下same-context renew固定拒绝；
- cancellation初始lease=A0/C1且DB三row后=A1/C1时terminal_application_catchup成功；activation/genesis三元组或DB/Provider六字段ordinary projection任一mutation、lease两个sequence与空prefix Head不等、DB未提交、partial/wrong previous A、C0/C1 mutation、resolved evidence、无封闭provenance的normal rebind lease、wrong runtime/identity/lineage、nonempty ordinary prefix、并发policy/第二epoch/staging逐项拒绝。epoch recovery分别以recovery-rebind lease、same-holder recovery-start exact lease与applied-scope immediate-result lease完成catchup；Renew request九项application proof/Inspect/history字段逐一present/null/cross-recovery mutation，fresh matched proof与current holder tuple/history tail不等均拒绝。lease terminal application为previous/current两种合法分支逐项成功，provider原子追加唯一catchup event并清marker，previous/result history digest及issuance request copy-through逐项验证，任意旧lease冒充分支(c)/(d)或二次catchup固定拒绝；response loss只Inspect且g/g+1竞争唯一；
- commit attestor对不存在、多行、未提交、wrong DB/semantic ID/row digest/identity/lineage与caller-supplied LSN拒绝签名；fixed row-kind registry 10项逐一positive golden，并把每个body中的其他ID字段逐项冒充semantic ID/schema alias/related cardinality mutation后固定拒绝。epoch/staging recovery-intent、epoch prefix-decision、epoch recovery-application+related apply decision及staging recovery-application row kind逐项trusted-read。commit_proof_purpose、archive_fence count/sorted key set/generation/evidence schema与challenge→attestation copy-through逐项golden；missing/extra/duplicate/mixed key、stale generation、wrong purpose、post-recovery/gap同时present与provider challenge过期/重放固定拒绝。epoch intent ordinary/gap exact-one、suffix ID/count/tail、DB anchor/provider-current双tuple、replacement decision kind/prefix/ordinal、gap-bound candidate、application related set/transaction tuple任一mutation，以及交叉Recover、rebind或catchup固定拒绝；
- DatabaseAuthorityHeadAttestationV1必须内嵌并签完整Head body；把临时Head伪装为database_immutable_body、digest/body不等或policy ledger由DB Head伪证明固定拒绝；
- planned restart先revoke，new runtime generation用exact lease termination、DatabaseAuthorityHead/attestation与head/WAL continuity恢复；genesis closed phase保持closed，fresh staging保持staging+lease null，只有active branch发lease；epoch-cancellation/recovery new lease在terminal/recovery application落库后仍须catchup。真实PostgreSQL PITR base backup恢复并promotion必须观测timeline增加且lineage rebind成功；authorization replay、错scope、旧generation、并行holder、伪history与非三个recovery scope的PITR落后点拒绝。

### 11.4 readiness 与 serving

- provider phase/profile/activation/completion/release/Open/credential/current identity/lineage/incarnation/lease任一字段mutation；
- stable Head保留exact latest reserved/committed sequence、reservation/receipt/operation/database-point规则；splice或opaque summary替代固定拒绝；
- selected genesis epoch/effective root/terminal root mutation、exact current provider epoch/effective+terminal chain与错误MAX(epoch)反例；
- legacy row注入；
- certificate/desired使用旧profile、旧epoch、错误activation ID或另一/过期incarnation lease；
- 两个本次caller-challenge-bound proof的issued/expiry不同但stable Head相同时允许；缓存两份仍未过期的旧proof、wrong request digest、重复caller nonce固定拒绝；pre/query/post任一步stable Head、runtime binding、lease ID/generation/digest或credential policy改变则拒绝；
- proof lease bundle逐purpose/phase验证：serving null时provider lease envelope必须absent，active ordinary恰含两个lease；lease activation/selected genesis/root、identity/lineage/effective epoch/terminal chain等于两种Head，policy只等于Provider Head，terminal application只等于DB Head且由Provider Head的serving lease digest间接承诺。DB/Provider当前六字段ordinary projection逐项相等，lease两个sequence是经exact prefix验证的ancestor baseline；相同sequence但任一digest/operation/receipt/point错配固定拒绝。rollover descendant恰含serving+ancestor+全部descendants+chain；missing/extra、expired/recovery-restricted/context-stale serving用于wrong purpose或proof expiry错误固定拒绝；
- unresolved runtime result、epoch intent/application/resolution/terminal/recovery prefix decision/application或provider pending/recovery/terminal-chain mismatch时readiness/writer固定关闭；普通response loss经Inspect恢复，真正PITR经exact recovery transcript/decision/application及mandatory catchup后两种Head exact匹配。post_catchup_marker_cleared rebind只有marker仍null、唯一catchup history完整、immediate result已提交且两种Head/active lease直连时可恢复ordinary readiness；pending result、第二catchup或任意无关mutation均拒绝；
- 有pending reservation时credential-policy transition固定拒绝；零pending后rotation成功，rotate-between-Reserve-and-Finalize/Abort不会产生stranded operation；
- admission inventory/shutdown在prepared_at有效后自然过期或release inventory合法前进不使active readiness失败；historical signature/digest/ancestor篡改仍失败；
- cancellation、authority与domain error priority；
- failure path无certificate DER、desired payload或派生授权值泄漏。

### 11.5 fresh restore staging

- source shutdown、每environment database/environment/provider不可逆retirement、seal、post-seal exact-cover与archive逐项匹配；缺失/重复/wrong DB/provider/endpoint/release scope、alias namespace固定拒绝；
- retirement后逐项尝试runtime restart、route reopen、credential/capability issuance、policy、Reserve、epoch/rebind均由tombstone永久拒绝；post-seal DB content/provider terminal Head/retirement splice或任一member产生更高epoch固定失败；
- FreshRestoreImportManifestV1逐项拒绝non-allowlist、wrong signer/source snapshot/target、duplicate object、expired/replayed apply ID；
- missing provider/inventory/epoch evidence或catalog-only failure能原子写IndeterminateSourceSealV1/FreshRestoreRequirementV1并冻结DB；minimal seal不能export、签完整archive或激活target，direct-full与minimal->full两条chain逐项通过，cross-source/activation/DB/provider splice拒绝；
- 禁止整库dump/import；
- reconstructed node集合完整、全部disabled且零active/pending pointer；
- 普通SQL或普通writer role在staging-closed零写；capability先提交DB、再取得row-bound provider challenge与attestor commit attestation才可Acquire。missing/uncommitted/duplicate/wrong DB/row kind/digest/activation/identity/lineage/LSN、expired/replayed challenge均拒绝；fake clock跨admission deadline后provider仍held且ordinary Head/policy/epoch固定被挡，DB transaction不发provider call；
- import/revocation双连接barrier race恰有一个terminal DB row；unknown COMMIT时Abort等待/拒绝，rollback后revocation+Abort成功，application commit后revocation零写；expired capability不能import但可提交revocation；
- outcome commit后在challenge前/后、attestation后、terminal前逐点终止postmaster；exact old-holder termination→held-preserving rebind→DB rebind result→fresh current-Head challenge/attestation可Release/Abort。旧holder未terminal、同generation、wrong mount、runtime-chain gap、non-null serving或普通rebind scope固定拒绝；
- provider held后PITR丢capability或capability runtime/identity成为ancestor时，普通import/revoke与DB-only rehydrate固定拒绝；可选lineage recovery rebind→durable recovery intent+commit proof→provider recovery CAS→DB intent/capability zero-or-exact-one rehydrate+recovery application→generic trusted-read commit proof→recovery revocation→fresh staging challenge/attestation→Abort成功。provider CAS前后、response loss、intent/capability/application commit前后、第二次PITR与holder再次死亡逐点注入；第二次PITR恢复intent原txid/snapshot/point/time成功。recovery application在generic challenge前、challenge后/attestation前、attestor archive后、revoker签名前后分别丢失：global latch absent的未attest candidate必须先staging_generic_row_rerun Fence再用同semantic ID/current body/new generation challenge重建，attested body只能从exact body+challenge+attestation archive逐字恢复；missing/wrong row-kind/commit LSN/tampered archive固定拒绝。revocation application在staging challenge前、challenge后但attestation前、attestation生成后但archive原子返回前、archive后及Abort后分别丢失：challenge不锁body，same-ID/different-body rerun必须先staging_outcome_row_rerun Fence，attestation archive才锁body；trusted journal key、signed revocation.revocation_application_id、recovery intent/request中的预分配值、application row.revocation_application_id与challenge/attestation.outcome_id任一单点mutation或normal/recovery跨支路splice固定拒绝。fresh import application在未attest且未archive时被PITR丢失必须走intent-backed recovery revocation→Abort，断言不发生reimport且FreshRestoreImportApplicationV1在所有Fence reason下均拒绝。current transaction改写locked body、stale generation、wrong external_admission/archive_continuity purpose、missing/partial archive与Abort后复活固定拒绝；
- 两连接barrier覆盖registration先赢→intent exact-one、intent先赢→迟到registration拒绝、different/multiple capability与application/revocation竞入；不使用sleep。穷举五表32种count组合只允许§7.2八种，再与provider null/held/required/abort-only state做cross-product；zero/exact-one materialization不得UPDATE原body，different intent/capability/recovery application固定零写失败；
- 真实PostgreSQL base backup分别恢复到release后capability前及capability后application前并promotion，断言timeline增加；capability缺失只在原commit LSN严格晚于timeline_fork_cut时成功，等于/早于cut、cut前fixed row缺失或point不可比较固定拒绝。fork早于release、wrong parent/child、sibling、伪造/缺失history ledger、wrong system/OID/name/incarnation、lineage chain mutation拒绝。recovery splice到另一capability/apply/exclusion/Head/runtime/DB、recovered capability import/Release、Release使用历史pre-PITR attestation均固定拒绝；
- Release必须绑定真实application commit attestation且intent/recovery null，Abort必须绑定真实revocation-application及匹配state的可选intent/recovery；recovery Abort还要求revocation envelope绑定recovery-application generic commit challenge/attestation，后续rebind的staging recovery evidence-set精确覆盖已attest两种body。required state不能直接Abort。challenge outcome kind/ID/row/acquisition anchor/current Head/current identity/lineage/rebind/runtime mutation、evidence-set missing/extra/stale与cross-terminal replay固定拒绝。challenge后rebind/recovery使旧terminal request失效，response loss按terminal ID Inspect；
- held/acquire/recovery intent/response与terminal Head/response的DAG golden证明无fixed point；held、required、abort-only、Abort、recovery intent/row、missing Release、wrong application或pre/post Head变化时FreshTargetInventory/evidence拒绝，committed current-DB Release后成功；所有recovery DB transaction的provider-call counter恒为0；
- manifest expected-post、pre与actual-post只使用FreshImportTopologyProjectionV1；任何把capability/application/time纳入projection造成的摘要环由golden拒绝；
- source trigger与外部capability双重只读，target不依赖source runtime；
- post-import exact table inventory、zero forbidden state、provider exact staging-closed Head、route closure、locked snapshot/WAL point与restore_incomplete全部进入evidence；
- evidence前后注入certificate/desired/pointer/provider reservation/route reopen/Head mutation均拒绝签发；
- restore completion barrier缺失时永久restore_incomplete；
- trust_bundle_publish/operator_authorizer_change保持unsupported。

- 物理WAL测试固定三条边界：row真实COMMIT end LSN `< cut <` attestation时later flush/replay/observation LSN必须拒绝，exact COMMIT end LSN `= cut`必须拒绝，只有same parent timeline的exact COMMIT end LSN `> cut`成功；generic与staging两种attestation、COMMIT PREPARED及记录缺失/ambiguous xid逐项覆盖；
- archive commit-before-return后丢response，Inspect按semantic key+challenge返回matched entry，同challenge exact retry byte-identical且sequence不增长；same semantic key/different body或same primary/different related set CAS永久conflict。连续两次promotion保留旧parent entry并只把fresh current-parent最大sequence选为canonical。staging固定覆盖R1→R2 rebind成功、R2在生成fresh archive entry前死亡、R2→R3的Inspect从live candidate读到exact locked row并以`exact_existing_candidate`成功；candidate row absent只可用`archived_missing_suffix`+immediate-parent canonical proof+exact commit LSN>cut，different/multiple row、count/nullability或mode mutation拒绝。exact-existing允许canonical null；missing-row canonical null、caller选旧entry、archive head截断、sequence/previous gap、same challenge双entry、evidence-set旧head/跨activation key逐项拒绝。provider journal派生的nonempty universe即使全部archive absent也产生non-null observation set；省略已锁定但candidate row缺失的key、用null或较小集合隐藏locked key、只给included-item responses、把locked response替换为absent response、遗漏任一预分配recovery/revocation key，在operator/provider两层均拒绝；
- candidate A有exact locked row而candidate B缺行时，把A的signed Inspect拼入B的authorization/request，在operator与provider两层均拒绝；即使identity/lineage相同但binding、instance ID、generation、lease或registration任一不同仍拒绝。两项evidence分别来自A/B、response只含request digest而无直接七字段echo、缺signed response envelope、count=0时错误清空candidate tuple、count=1时row digest/database point缺一、wrong/null staging registration逐项拒绝。staging key related count必须始终0；把epoch related decision、Fence或generation evidence冒充第三个expected key固定拒绝；

### 11.6 canonical transcript conformance

- 每个V1 schema有固定JCS bytes、body digest、signature envelope与cross-language golden vector；
- missing/extra/unknown field、duplicate key/set member、wrong order normalization、wrong domain、BOM、noncanonical UUID/time/digest逐项拒绝；
- registration/rebinding-registration/result、timeline lineage、DatabaseAuthorityRebindGap/PostRecoveryAttestation、proof/lease-descendant/terminal-catchup、generic/staging commit challenge+attestation、Fence semantic-ID journal record/request/response、archive Inspect candidate/history fields、Prepare/Complete/PrepareRelease/Open/Inspect/Head、ordinary Reserve/Finalize/Abort/Record、epoch intent/application/resolution/cancellation/terminal/recovery intent/request/deferred-suffix/transcript/application、attestor runtime lease initial/renew/termination、provider serving lease renew/revoke/rebind、source membership/retirement/post-seal、staging capability/commit-proof/revocation/hold/recovery-intent/recovery/challenge/Release/Abort、down retirement与Down三阶段的每个schema都有literal golden；任一强制字段mutation固定conflict或unavailable；
- signature envelope逐项替换schema/role/key/policy/root/algorithm或noncanonical ECDSA/encoding固定拒绝；
- EnvironmentInventoryRecord/AnchorSet/ShutdownSet、credential policy、runtime-holder challenge、provider history与attestor/provider lease sequence的rollback逐项拒绝；
- ClaimV1Receipt/Record reservation digest重算、Head/rebind identity+lineage/recovery phase matrix、genesis effective/terminal root、每个resolved/cancelled terminal chain与DB terminal/recovery decision/application、provider exact transcript set/consumer anchors、LegacySourceRetirement/PostSeal/LegacyEpochSourceSet exact coverage、FreshImportTopologyProjection、staging五表matrix及PristineDowngrade三阶段逐项golden/negative覆盖。epoch recovery DAG golden固定为lineage/terminal anchor→recovery intent→可选gap-attested deferred rebind suffix→suffix-bound commit proof→provider transcript→唯一prefix apply-vs-replacement decision；apply分支→same-transaction DB terminal+result suffix+decision/application pair→atomic application+decision global-latch proof→每个candidate fresh matched proof→direct catchup或pre-catchup applied rebind+immediate result→fresh history-bound proof→唯一catchup→可选marker-cleared resume，replacement分支→fresh candidate gap attestation→candidate-bound decision commit proof→rebind CAS消费decision→更长prefix transcript；pre-CAS换candidate只换后半段proof，不改decision。different-body rerun DAG必须在row absent时先Inspect→Fence generation→current transaction→new challenge，Fence不引用attestation/consumer。staging challenge在trusted-read attestation归档前不形成body latch，archive entry digest恰等于attestation digest；Inspect把current-parent/continuity机械选择、signed live-candidate tuple与0/1 observation一起承诺，evidence mode无caller choice。history event不引用provider response，全部DAG均无self/fixed point。

### 11.7 independent review

设计、计划与实现分别接受独立correctness/security review。完成gate要求零Critical与零Important finding；Medium finding必须有明确处置或书面接受，不能静默忽略。

## 12. 完成条件

本修订只有在以下条件全部满足时才算落实到计划：

1. 文档状态为已批准并进入 canonical C1.2 spec-set；
2. suite index与Plans 01/02/03/08/09引用本文件的exact SHA-256；
3. Plan 08 sole builder计算包含全部approved amendments的新SpecDigest；
4. Plan 01不再把简单ADD abort_claimed_at描述为legacy-compatible migration；
5. shutdown、environment inventory、incarnation、empty proof、five-stage activation、provider profile、three-row barrier、exact-epoch readiness与downgrade规则均有reviewer-sized TDD tasks；
6. Plan 09明确区分empty-in-place完成与fresh-restore staging；
7. 没有任务声称B01已完成trust/authorizer rotation或legacy destructive restore；
8. 所有计划自审与独立review为零Critical/Important。

实现完成声明同样分开：

- empty_in_place：全部权威验证通过、provider active且one-live incarnation lease有效后可以声明claim-v1 protocol activated；
- fresh_restore_target：当前最多声明safe staging established and closed，不能声明legacy upgrade、destructive restore或production serving完成。
